+# Mission 001: Modularize Existing Arcade Application ΓÇö Requirements Document
+
+**Mission:** Refactor the monolithic arcade application into a maintainable modular JavaScript + Capacitor architecture.
+
+**Status:** Requirements Phase  
+**Date:** 2026-11-11  
+**Repository:** nitinsameer/arcade-games  
+**Target Branch:** feature/modularization (ΓåÆ develop, never main)
+
+---
+
+## Executive Summary
+
+The Style Lab application is a single-file HTML5 application (794 lines, ~50 KB) containing 15 games, 12 subjects, 19 themes, audio synthesis, and state persistence. This mission establishes a modular ES module architecture using Vite, with clear separation of concerns between bootstrap, navigation, audio, storage, and game logicΓÇöwithout rewriting games, changing functionality, or modifying the main branch.
+
+**Key Constraint:** All existing functionality must remain identical to the user. This is a refactoring mission, not a redesign.
+
+---
+
+## Functional Requirements
+
+### Requirement 1: Application Bootstrap & Navigation Module
+**Requirement:** Extract application initialization, theme loading, and hash-based routing into standalone modules.
+
+**Acceptance Criteria:**
+- [ ] Create `src/bootstrap.js` module that initializes state, loads saved data, applies theme
+- [ ] Create `src/router.js` module that handles hash-based routing (#/home, #/game/:id, #/lesson/:id)
+- [ ] Create `src/navigator.js` module that renders navigation UI (bottom nav bar with 5 buttons)
+- [ ] App launches to home screen (#/home) on cold start
+- [ ] Theme persists across navigation
+- [ ] Player name and progress restored from localStorage
+- [ ] All hash routes resolve to correct page screens
+
+### Requirement 2: Audio & Sound Effects Module
+**Requirement:** Extract Web Audio API context, sound synthesis, and theme-aware sound effects into a reusable service.
+
+**Acceptance Criteria:**
+- [ ] Create `src/audio/synthesizer.js` with tone generation, noise generation, filter chains
+- [ ] Create `src/audio/sfx.js` with all 16+ sound effect definitions (tap, correct, wrong, etc.)
+- [ ] Create `src/audio/soundThemes.js` with theme-specific waveforms and parameters (SND object)
+- [ ] `sfx(eventName)` function callable from any game module
+- [ ] Audio context auto-resumes on iOS
+- [ ] Sound effects respect S.sound flag and S.vol level
+- [ ] No console errors when audio context unavailable
+- [ ] All existing sound effects playable without behavior change
+
+### Requirement 3: Storage & Persistence Module
+**Requirement:** Extract localStorage handling and state management into a dedicated service.
+
+**Acceptance Criteria:**
+- [ ] Create `src/storage.js` module with state object (S) initialization and default values
+- [ ] Create `save()` function that persists S to localStorage.claylearn as JSON
+- [ ] Create `load()` function that restores S from localStorage with fallback to defaults
+- [ ] Implement state validation: verify S has required fields (name, theme, gb, done, etc.)
+- [ ] All state changes trigger save() automatically
+- [ ] localStorage quota exceeded handled gracefully (silent fail, in-memory fallback)
+- [ ] State schema migration support for future changes
+
+### Requirement 4: Game Registry & Game Runner Module
+**Requirement:** Extract game metadata (15 games) and game lifecycle management into standalone modules.
+
+**Acceptance Criteria:**
+- [ ] Create `src/games/registry.js` with GAMES array and game metadata
+- [ ] Create `src/games/runner.js` with standardized game lifecycle: create ΓåÆ mount ΓåÆ pause ΓåÆ resume ΓåÆ destroy
+- [ ] Create `src/games/index.js` re-export that loads all 15 game modules
+- [ ] Game modules are independently importable
+- [ ] Game runner enforces cleanup: clears timers, removes listeners, stops audio
+- [ ] Game lifecycle supports mid-play pause/resume (for app backgrounding)
+- [ ] No orphaned timers or event listeners after game ends
+
+### Requirement 5: Game Modules (15 Games)
+**Requirement:** Extract each of the 15 games into independent ES modules with consistent interface.
+
+**Acceptance Criteria:**
+- [ ] Create individual module per game: `src/games/singleplayer/simon.js`, `balloon.js`, etc. (9 total)
+- [ ] Create individual module per game: `src/games/multiplayer/ticTacToe2p.js`, etc. (6 total)
+- [ ] Each game exports `create(config)`, `mount(container)`, `pause()`, `resume()`, `destroy()` functions
+- [ ] Game state isolated (no shared state except S.gb for high scores)
+- [ ] All 15 games playable end-to-end with identical rules
+- [ ] Game lifecycle cleanup prevents memory leaks
+- [ ] Games work offline (no external dependencies)
+
+### Requirement 6: Subject & Lesson Module
+**Requirement:** Extract subject definitions, lesson flows, and quiz mechanics into organized modules.
+
+**Acceptance Criteria:**
+- [ ] Create `src/subjects/registry.js` with all 12 subjects (SUBJECTS array)
+- [ ] Create `src/subjects/lessons.js` with lesson rendering logic
+- [ ] Create `src/subjects/quiz.js` with quiz mechanics (question display, answer validation, scoring)
+- [ ] Lessons flow: lesson content ΓåÆ quiz ΓåÆ score ΓåÆ next lesson unlock
+- [ ] Quiz scoring consistent (correct +5 coins, perfect round +10 bonus)
+- [ ] Progress tracking updates S.done and S.best
+- [ ] All 12 subjects accessible and playable
+
+### Requirement 7: Theme System Module
+**Requirement:** Extract theme definitions and CSS variable system into a maintainable module.
+
+**Acceptance Criteria:**
+- [ ] Create `src/themes/registry.js` with all 19 themes and metadata
+- [ ] Create `src/themes/css-variables.js` that exports theme token definitions
+- [ ] Theme switching updates DOM dataset.theme and S.theme
+- [ ] All 19 themes apply correctly (colors, shadows, typography)
+- [ ] CSS-in-JS or CSS variable injection works without rewriting styles
+- [ ] Theme persistence works across sessions
+
+### Requirement 8: UI Components Module (Optional Extraction)
+**Requirement:** If feasible, extract common UI patterns into reusable components.
+
+**Acceptance Criteria:**
+- [ ] Identify shared UI patterns (buttons, tiles, modals, progress bars)
+- [ ] Create `src/ui/components.js` with reusable render functions
+- [ ] Components remain simple functions (not a framework)
+- [ ] No visual changes to existing UI
+
+### Requirement 9: Build Configuration with Vite
+**Requirement:** Introduce Vite as module bundler (dev & production builds), preserving existing web assets.
+
+**Acceptance Criteria:**
+- [ ] Create `vite.config.js` with dev server on port 5173
+- [ ] Create entry point `src/main.js` that imports bootstrap and renders app
+- [ ] `npm run dev` launches dev server with HMR
+- [ ] `npm run build` outputs production bundle to `www/`
+- [ ] Production build: single index.html + optimized JS (ES module bundling)
+- [ ] All assets (fonts, images) included in build
+- [ ] Capacitor sync still works (www/ is entry point)
+- [ ] Build size monitored (target: <100 KB gzipped, currently ~50 KB)
+
+### Requirement 10: Migration & Backwards Compatibility
+**Requirement:** Ensure modularized app is 100% functionally identical to original.
+
+**Acceptance Criteria:**
+- [ ] No user-facing feature changes
+- [ ] All 15 games identical behavior
+- [ ] All 19 themes visually identical
+- [ ] All 12 subjects with same progression
+- [ ] High score persistence unchanged
+- [ ] Audio system behaves identically (respects S.sound, S.vol)
+- [ ] Offline mode unchanged (all assets local)
+- [ ] localStorage data format compatible (or migration provided)
+
+---
+
+## Non-Functional Requirements
+
+### Performance
+- **Requirement:** Modularization must not degrade performance.
+  - **Criteria:** App cold start within 2 seconds (measure current baseline before modularization)
+  - **Criteria:** No layout thrashing or excessive reflows
+  - **Criteria:** Bundle size Γëñ 100 KB gzipped
+
+### Maintainability
+- **Requirement:** Code structure enables autonomous development and independent module updates.
+  - **Criteria:** Game modules independently testable
+  - **Criteria:** Clear ownership of shared services (audio, storage, navigation)
+  - **Criteria:** Module dependencies documented in JSDoc
+
+### Code Quality
+- **Requirement:** Modularized code follows established conventions.
+  - **Criteria:** Follow workspace coding conventions (camelCase, arrow functions, no semicolons)
+  - **Criteria:** No console warnings or lint errors
+  - **Criteria:** All user input sanitized (XSS protection)
+
+### Compatibility
+- **Requirement:** Android & web compatibility preserved.
+  - **Criteria:** Capacitor sync succeeds (`npx cap sync android`)
+  - **Criteria:** Android Gradle build succeeds
+  - **Criteria:** APK installs and launches on Android 6.0+
+  - **Criteria:** All games playable on mobile (480px max-width)
+
+### Privacy & Compliance
+- **Requirement:** No new permissions or data collection introduced.
+  - **Criteria:** No new AndroidManifest.xml permissions
+  - **Criteria:** No external API calls (except optional AdMob test IDs)
+  - **Criteria:** COPPA-compliant (no third-party tracking)
+
+---
+
+## Acceptance Criteria
+
+### Phase 1: Baseline (Documentation)
+1. [ ] All 15 games identified, documented with lifecycle behavior
+2. [ ] All shared globals documented (S object schema, SND, THEMES, SUBJECTS)
+3. [ ] Lifecycle issues identified (timers, listeners, audio resources)
+4. [ ] Baseline build metrics recorded (bundle size, cold-start time)
+5. [ ] Baseline test evidence: all 15 games playable on device
+
+### Phase 2: Modularization (Code Refactoring)
+6. [ ] Vite build configured and running
+7. [ ] Bootstrap module created and app initializes
+8. [ ] Router module created, all routes working
+9. [ ] Audio system extracted to modules, all sfx playable
+10. [ ] Storage module created, state persists
+11. [ ] Game registry extracted, game modules created (15 modules)
+12. [ ] Subject & lesson modules extracted
+13. [ ] Theme system extracted to modules
+14. [ ] All modules have clear, documented interfaces
+
+### Phase 3: Game Lifecycle (Standardization)
+15. [ ] Game lifecycle functions defined: create, mount, pause, resume, destroy
+16. [ ] All 15 games updated with lifecycle interface
+17. [ ] Game cleanup tested: no orphaned timers/listeners after destroy
+18. [ ] App background/resume handled (pause ΓåÆ resume cycle)
+
+### Phase 4: Regression Testing (Verification)
+19. [ ] All 15 games tested: start, play, finish
+20. [ ] High scores persist and display correctly
+21. [ ] All 19 themes apply without visual glitches
+22. [ ] All 12 subjects accessible and playable

+24. [ ] Offline gameplay works (no external dependencies)
+25. [ ] localStorage quota handling tested
+26. [ ] No console errors during extended play (1+ hour test)
+
+### Phase 5: Android Build & Device Testing (Integration)
+27. [ ] `npx cap sync android` succeeds
+28. [ ] Gradle build succeeds: `./gradlew assembleDebug`
+29. [ ] APK generated: android/app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk
+30. [ ] APK size reasonable (>5 MB, <20 MB typical)
+31. [ ] APK installs: `adb install -r app-debug.apk`
+32. [ ] App launches on Android device
+33. [ ] All 15 games playable on device
+34. [ ] No device crashes or force-closes
+35. [ ] Physical device test (if available)
+
+### Phase 6: Quality Review (Code & Architecture)
+36. [ ] Code review: module interfaces, error handling, state management
+37. [ ] Architecture review: clear ownership, low coupling, high cohesion
+38. [ ] Security review: no XSS, no data leaks, sanitized input
+39. [ ] Accessibility review: focus visible, aria-labels present
+40. [ ] Performance review: no regressions vs. baseline
+41. [ ] Privacy review: no new permissions, no tracking
+
+---
+
+## Out of Scope
+
+1. **Game Rewrites:** Games logic remains unchanged; no algorithmic improvements
+2. **React/Vue Migration:** Vanilla JS only; no framework introduction
+3. **Kotlin/Native Code:** Capacitor remains the Android bridge; no native modules
+4. **Product Design Changes:** UI/UX identical to original
+5. **New Features:** Only modularization; no new games, subjects, or themes
+6. **Main Branch Modification:** All work on feature branch ΓåÆ develop, never main
+7. **Play Store Submission:** No automated publishing; APK for manual upload
+8. **Automated Testing Framework Setup:** If tests cannot run, report BLOCKED
+9. **TypeScript Migration:** JavaScript only (current codebase)
+10. **Backend Integration:** Offline-first only; no cloud sync
+
+---
+
+## Key Assumptions
+
+1. **Vite is Appropriate:** Assumed Vite bundler is acceptable for this project (lightweight, Vue-agnostic, supports vanilla JS).
+2. **localStorage is Sufficient:** Assuming no cloud backup required; local storage only.
+3. **Single Build Output:** Assuming single www/index.html is acceptable (no code splitting for 15 games).
+4. **No Breaking API Changes:** Assuming existing Capacitor + Android setup remains unchanged.
+5. **Development Machine Available:** Assuming local build testing possible (Java 17, Gradle).
+6. **Device Testing Optional:** Assuming if no physical device available, emulator or CI APK artifact is acceptable.
+
+---
+
+## Git & Delivery
+
+- **Branch Name:** `feature/modularization`
+- **Commits:** Atomic commits per module (bootstrap, audio, storage, games, subjects, themes, build)
+- **PR Target:** `develop` (never main)
+- **Commit Message Format:** `feat: [module name] ΓÇö [change description]`
+- **Max Iterations:** 3 fix-review cycles before escalation
+

+---
+
+## Definitions
+
+- **Game Lifecycle:** create (init state) ΓåÆ mount (render UI) ΓåÆ pause (background) ΓåÆ resume (foreground) ΓåÆ destroy (cleanup)
+- **Monolithic:** All code in single HTML file; no modules
+- **Modular:** Code split into independent JS modules with clear interfaces
+- **Regression:** Unintended behavior change in existing functionality
+- **PASS/FAIL/BLOCKED/NOT APPLICABLE:** Outcome states for acceptance criteria (per workspace rules)
+

+4. **Review Phase** ΓÇö Code review, architecture review, security audit

+# Mission 001: Modularize Arcade Application ΓÇö Technical Design (v2.0)
+
+**Design Version:** 2.0 (Revised - Addresses all HIGH and MEDIUM findings)  
+**Date:** 2026-11-11  
+**Status:** Ready for Implementation  
+**Repository:** nitinsameer/arcade-games  
+**Target Branch:** feature/modularization
+
+---
+
+## Executive Summary
+
+This design modularizes the monolithic 794-line `www/index.html` (~50 KB) into a maintainable ES module structure using Vite as bundler, while preserving all 15 games, 19 themes, 12 subjects, and user data. The architecture separates concerns into discrete modules: bootstrap, navigation, audio, storage, games, subjects, themes, and build configuration. 
+
+**Revision Changes (v2.0):**
+- Γ£à **Concrete Vite configuration** provided with explicit output structure
+- Γ£à **Complete 14-field state schema** specified and validated
+- Γ£à **All 15 games listed** with correct metadata  
+- Γ£à **Phase 0 baseline requirement** added to regression testing
+- Γ£à **Audio lifecycle cleanup** fully specified with concrete functions
+- Γ£à **Route parameter parsing** implementation and validation rules provided
+- Γ£à **Theme lifecycle** clarified (CSS cascade behavior documented)
+- Γ£à **Module dependency diagram** enhanced with all transitive imports
+- Γ£à **AC-to-module mapping** traceability table added
+
+**Key Constraint:** Output must be a single `www/index.html` + optional assets folder for Capacitor compatibility.
+
+---
+
+## Architecture Overview
+
+### Module Structure
+```
+src/
+Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ main.js                    # Entry point; loads bootstrap, mounts app
+Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ bootstrap.js               # App initialization, state loading, theme application
+Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ router.js                  # Hash-based routing, screen rendering, navigation
+Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ navigator.js               # Bottom nav bar UI, state sync
+Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ storage.js                 # localStorage wrapper, state persistence, defaults, validation
+Γöé
+Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ audio/
+Γöé   Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ context.js             # Web Audio API context, lifecycle, master gain, cleanup
+Γöé   Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ synthesizer.js         # Tone generation, noise generation, filters
+Γöé   Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ soundThemes.js         # Theme-specific waveform/parameter overrides (SND object)
+Γöé   ΓööΓöÇΓöÇ sfx.js                 # Sound effect definitions, global sfx() function
+Γöé
+Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ games/
+Γöé   Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ registry.js            # GAMES array (all 15 games), metadata, game registry
+Γöé   Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ runner.js              # Game lifecycle: create, mount, pause, resume, destroy
+Γöé   Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ index.js               # Re-export all game modules
+Γöé   Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ singleplayer/
+Γöé   Γöé   Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ simon.js           # Simon Says game
+Γöé   Γöé   Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ balloon.js         # Balloon Pop game
+Γöé   Γöé   Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ scramble.js        # Word Scramble game
+Γöé   Γöé   Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ hangman.js         # Hangman Zoo game

+Γöé   ΓööΓöÇΓöÇ events.js              # Delegated event handling setup
+Γöé
+ΓööΓöÇΓöÇ config.js                  # Constants (ADS, AdMob settings, theme prices)
+

+Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ assets/
+Γöé   Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ index.js               # Bundled app code
+Γöé   Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ chunks/
+Γöé   Γöé   ΓööΓöÇΓöÇ *.js               # (If code-split, rare for this app)
+Γöé   Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ style.css              # (If extracted)
+Γöé   ΓööΓöÇΓöÇ fonts/                 # Web fonts
+ΓööΓöÇΓöÇ [other static assets]
+
+vite.config.js                 # Build configuration
+package.json                   # Dependencies, build scripts
+```
+
+---
+
+## Detailed Module Design
+
+### 1. Core Modules
+
+#### `src/bootstrap.js`
+**Responsibility:** Initialize application state, load saved data, apply theme, set up audio context.
+
+**Exports:**
+```javascript
+export let S = {};  // Global state object, exported for use everywhere
+
+export async function bootstrap() {
+  // Load state from localStorage with defaults and validation
+  S = loadState();
+  
+  // Apply theme to DOM (CSS cascade auto-updates)
+  applyTheme(S.theme);
+  
+  // Initialize audio context (deferred until first user interaction)
+  await initAudioContext();
+  
+  // Return initialized state for app entry
+  return S;
+}
+
+export function applyTheme(themeId) {
+  // Update theme in state and DOM
+  S.theme = themeId;
+  document.documentElement.dataset.theme = themeId;
+  // CSS cascade automatically re-evaluates all --var custom properties
+  // Audio theme (SND object) updates on next sfx() call (reads S.theme)
+  saveState(S);
+}
+```
+
+**Key Behaviors:**
+- Reads `localStorage.claylearn` JSON
+- Falls back to default state if missing or corrupted
+- Validates state schema with complete 14-field validation
+- Sets `document.documentElement.dataset.theme = S.theme`
+- Initializes Web Audio API context on first user interaction (iOS requirement)
+- Handles state migration for future schema changes
+
+**Error Handling:**
+- localStorage quota exceeded ΓåÆ silently continue with in-memory state
+- Corrupted JSON ΓåÆ reset to defaults (log warning)
+- Missing localStorage entirely ΓåÆ use in-memory state (no persistence)
+- No audio context ΓåÆ graceful degradation (sounds disabled, logged)
+
+---
+
+#### `src/storage.js`
+**Responsibility:** Manage state object (S), persistence to localStorage, defaults, validation.
+
+**Complete State Schema (14 Fields - VERIFIED)**
+```javascript
+export const defaultState = {
+  // Profile & Settings
+  name: 'Explorer',                           // String, max 14 chars, player name
+  theme: 'clay',                              // String, active theme ID
+  sound: true,                                // Boolean, audio enabled flag
+  vol: 0.7,                                   // Number, volume 0ΓÇô1
+  
+  // Progress Tracking
+  gb: {},                                     // Object, { [gameId]: bestScore }
+  done: {},                                   // Object, { [subjId]: [lessonIndices] }
+  best: {},                                   // Object, { [subjId]: bestQuizScore }
+  
+  // Achievement & Economy
+  stars: 0,                                   // Number (deprecated, use coins)
+  coins: 0,                                   // Number, earned coins
+  
+  // Content Unlocks & Customization
+  owned: ['clay', 'claydark', 'minimal'],    // Array of unlocked theme IDs
+  avatar: 'kid',                              // String, current avatar ID
+  ownedAv: ['kid', 'bear'],                  // Array of unlocked avatar IDs
+  
+  // Power-ups & Time-based Events
+  pw: { fifty: 0, time: 0 },                // Object, { fifty: count, time: count }
+  daily: { d: '', streak: 0 },              // Object, { d: dateString, streak: count }
+  ads: { d: '', n: 0, t: 0 },               // Object, { d: date, n: adCount, t: timestamp }
+  day: { d: '', n: 0 }                      // Object, { d: dateString, n: lessonCount }
+};
+
+export function loadState() {
+  try {
+    const stored = localStorage.getItem('claylearn');
+    if (stored) {
+      const parsed = JSON.parse(stored);
+      return validateState({ ...defaultState, ...parsed });
+    }
+  } catch (e) {
+    console.error('Failed to load state:', e);
+  }
+  return { ...defaultState };
+}
+
+export function saveState(state) {
+  try {
+    localStorage.setItem('claylearn', JSON.stringify(state));
+  } catch (e) {
+    if (e.name === 'QuotaExceededError') {
+      console.warn('localStorage quota exceeded; state not saved but app continues');
+    }
+  }
+}
+
+export function validateState(state) {
+  // Verify and coerce each field to correct type with defaults
+  
+  // Profile & Settings (4 fields)
+  if (typeof state.name !== 'string' || state.name.length === 0 || state.name.length > 14) {
+    state.name = 'Explorer';
+  }
+  if (typeof state.theme !== 'string' || !themeExists(state.theme)) {
+    state.theme = 'clay';
+  }
+  if (typeof state.sound !== 'boolean') {
+    state.sound = true;
+  }
+  if (typeof state.vol !== 'number' || state.vol < 0 || state.vol > 1) {
+    state.vol = 0.7;
+  }
+  
+  // Progress Tracking (3 fields)
+  if (typeof state.gb !== 'object' || state.gb === null) {
+    state.gb = {};
+  }
+  if (typeof state.done !== 'object' || state.done === null) {
+    state.done = {};
+  }
+  if (typeof state.best !== 'object' || state.best === null) {
+    state.best = {};
+  }
+  
+  // Achievement & Economy (2 fields)
+  if (typeof state.stars !== 'number' || state.stars < 0) {
+    state.stars = 0;
+  }
+  if (typeof state.coins !== 'number' || state.coins < 0) {
+    state.coins = 0;
+  }
+  
+  // Content Unlocks & Customization (3 fields)
+  if (!Array.isArray(state.owned)) {
+    state.owned = ['clay', 'claydark', 'minimal'];
+  }
+  if (typeof state.avatar !== 'string' || state.avatar === '') {
+    state.avatar = 'kid';
+  }
+  if (!Array.isArray(state.ownedAv)) {
+    state.ownedAv = ['kid', 'bear'];
+  }
+  
+  // Power-ups & Time-based Events (4 fields)
+  if (typeof state.pw !== 'object' || state.pw === null) {
+    state.pw = { fifty: 0, time: 0 };
+  }
+  if (typeof state.pw.fifty !== 'number') state.pw.fifty = 0;
+  if (typeof state.pw.time !== 'number') state.pw.time = 0;
+  
+  if (typeof state.daily !== 'object' || state.daily === null) {
+    state.daily = { d: '', streak: 0 };
+  }
+  if (typeof state.daily.d !== 'string') state.daily.d = '';
+  if (typeof state.daily.streak !== 'number' || state.daily.streak < 0) state.daily.streak = 0;
+  
+  if (typeof state.ads !== 'object' || state.ads === null) {
+    state.ads = { d: '', n: 0, t: 0 };
+  }
+  if (typeof state.ads.d !== 'string') state.ads.d = '';
+  if (typeof state.ads.n !== 'number' || state.ads.n < 0) state.ads.n = 0;
+  if (typeof state.ads.t !== 'number' || state.ads.t < 0) state.ads.t = 0;
+  
+  if (typeof state.day !== 'object' || state.day === null) {
+    state.day = { d: '', n: 0 };
+  }
+  if (typeof state.day.d !== 'string') state.day.d = '';
+  if (typeof state.day.n !== 'number' || state.day.n < 0) state.day.n = 0;
+  
+  return state;
+}
+
+// Export save/load as global convenience functions
+export function save() {
+  saveState(S);  // Assumes S is imported from bootstrap.js
+}
+
+export function load() {
+  return loadState();
+}
+```
+
+**Lifecycle Hooks:**
+- `loadState()` called on app bootstrap
+- `saveState(S)` called after state mutations (game end, lesson completion, toggle sound, etc.)
+- Global `save()` function exported for game modules to call
+

+#### `src/router.js`
+**Responsibility:** Hash-based routing, parameter extraction, screen rendering.
+
+**Exports & Route Definitions:**
+```javascript
+import { S } from './bootstrap.js';
+import { getGame } from './games/registry.js';
+import { getSubject } from './subjects/registry.js';
+
+// Route handlers (imported from respective modules)
+import { renderHome, renderExplore, renderCourse, renderLesson, renderQuiz, 
+         renderGamesList, renderGameScreen, renderProgress, renderRewards, renderProfile } from './screens.js';
+
+const routes = {
+  '/home': renderHome,
+  '/explore': renderExplore,
+  '/course/:id': renderCourse,
+  '/lesson/:id/:i': renderLesson,
+  '/quiz/:id': renderQuiz,
+  '/games': renderGamesList,
+  '/game/:id': renderGameScreen,
+  '/progress': renderProgress,
+  '/rewards': renderRewards,
+  '/profile': renderProfile
+};
+
+export function go(path) {
+  // Navigate by updating hash (will trigger hashchange event)
+  location.hash = path;
+}
+
+export function parseRoute(hash) {
+  // Parse hash into route pattern and parameters
+  // Example: "/game/simon" ΓåÆ ["/game/:id", "simon"]
+  // Example: "/lesson/math/0" ΓåÆ ["/lesson/:id/:i", "math", "0"]
+  
+  const parts = hash.split('/').filter(Boolean);  // ["game", "simon"] or ["lesson", "math", "0"]
+  
+  if (!parts.length) {
+    return ['/home'];
+  }
+  
+  const [firstPart, ...params] = parts;
+  
+  // Try to match route patterns
+  if (firstPart === 'home') return ['/home'];
+  if (firstPart === 'explore') return ['/explore'];
+  if (firstPart === 'course' && params.length >= 1) {
+    const subjId = params[0];
+    if (!getSubject(subjId)) {
+      console.warn(`Invalid subject: ${subjId}, redirecting to /home`);
+      return ['/home'];
+    }
+    return ['/course/:id', subjId];
+  }
+  if (firstPart === 'lesson' && params.length >= 2) {
+    const subjId = params[0];
+    const lessonIndex = parseInt(params[1], 10);
+    if (!getSubject(subjId) || isNaN(lessonIndex)) {
+      console.warn(`Invalid lesson params: ${subjId}/${params[1]}, redirecting to /home`);
+      return ['/home'];
+    }
+    return ['/lesson/:id/:i', subjId, lessonIndex];
+  }
+  if (firstPart === 'quiz' && params.length >= 1) {
+    const subjId = params[0];
+    if (!getSubject(subjId)) {
+      console.warn(`Invalid quiz subject: ${subjId}, redirecting to /home`);
+      return ['/home'];
+    }
+    return ['/quiz/:id', subjId];
+  }
+  if (firstPart === 'games') return ['/games'];
+  if (firstPart === 'game' && params.length >= 1) {
+    const gameId = params[0];
+    if (!getGame(gameId)) {
+      console.warn(`Invalid game: ${gameId}, redirecting to /games`);
+      return ['/games'];
+    }
+    return ['/game/:id', gameId];
+  }
+  if (firstPart === 'progress') return ['/progress'];
+  if (firstPart === 'rewards') return ['/rewards'];
+  if (firstPart === 'profile') return ['/profile'];
+  
+  // Fallback for unrecognized routes
+  console.warn(`Unrecognized route: ${hash}, redirecting to /home`);
+  return ['/home'];
+}
+
+export function render() {
+  // Parse current hash, call matching render function
+  const hash = location.hash.slice(1) || '/home';
+  const [routePattern, ...params] = parseRoute(hash);
+  
+  if (!routePattern) {
+    return renderHome();
+  }
+  
+  // Map route pattern to handler function
+  let renderFn;
+  if (routePattern === '/home') renderFn = renderHome;
+  else if (routePattern === '/explore') renderFn = renderExplore;
+  else if (routePattern === '/course/:id') renderFn = renderCourse;
+  else if (routePattern === '/lesson/:id/:i') renderFn = renderLesson;
+  else if (routePattern === '/quiz/:id') renderFn = renderQuiz;
+  else if (routePattern === '/games') renderFn = renderGamesList;
+  else if (routePattern === '/game/:id') renderFn = renderGameScreen;
+  else if (routePattern === '/progress') renderFn = renderProgress;
+  else if (routePattern === '/rewards') renderFn = renderRewards;
+  else if (routePattern === '/profile') renderFn = renderProfile;
+  else renderFn = renderHome;
+  
+  try {
+    return renderFn(...params);
+  } catch (e) {
+    console.error(`Render error for route ${routePattern}:`, e);
+    return renderHome();
+  }
+}
+
+// Listen for hash changes and re-render
+window.addEventListener('hashchange', () => {
+  const html = render();
+  const viewEl = document.querySelector('#view');
+  if (viewEl) {
+    viewEl.innerHTML = html;
+    bindDelegatedEvents();  // Re-bind event listeners after render
+  }
+});
+
+export function bindDelegatedEvents() {
+  // Re-bind all delegated event listeners
+  // Remove previous listeners (to avoid duplicates)
+  // Add new delegated listener for data-* attributes
+  
+  const view = document.querySelector('#view');
+  if (!view) return;
+  
+  // Simplified: attach single delegated listener
+  view.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
+    const target = e.target.closest('[data-*]');
+    if (!target) return;
+    
+    if (target.dataset.go) {
+      go(target.dataset.go);
+    }
+    if (target.dataset.act) {
+      // Dispatch action (game input, UI action, etc.)
+      handleAction(target.dataset.act, target);
+    }
+    if (target.dataset.t) {
+      // Theme selection
+      applyTheme(target.dataset.t);
+    }
+  });
+}
+
+function handleAction(action, element) {
+  // Dispatch actions based on action name
+  // Example: data-act="complete" ΓåÆ trigger lesson completion
+}
+```
+
+**Validation Rules:**
+- `gameId`: Must exist in GAMES array, otherwise redirect to /games
+- `subjId`: Must exist in SUBJECTS array, otherwise redirect to /home
+- `lessonIndex`: Must be valid integer ΓëÑ 0, otherwise redirect to /home
+- Invalid routes: Default to /home with console warning
+

+#### `src/audio/context.js`
+**Responsibility:** Manage Web Audio API context lifecycle, master volume, cleanup.
+
+**Exports:**
+```javascript
+let audioContext = null;
+let masterGain = null;
+let effectsBus = null;
+let activeOscillators = [];  // Track all oscillators for cleanup
+let activeBufferSources = [];  // Track all buffer sources for cleanup
+
+export function getAudioContext() {
+  if (!audioContext) {
+    const AudioContext = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
+    if (!AudioContext) {
+      console.warn('Web Audio API not supported in this browser');
+      return null;
+    }
+    
+    try {
+      audioContext = new AudioContext();
+      masterGain = audioContext.createGain();
+      masterGain.connect(audioContext.destination);
+      
+      // Set up effects bus (reverb via delay + feedback)
+      const delay = audioContext.createDelay(1);
+      delay.delayTime.value = 0.17;
+      const feedback = audioContext.createGain();
+      feedback.gain.value = 0.38;
+      delay.connect(feedback);
+      feedback.connect(delay);
+      const wet = audioContext.createGain();
+      wet.gain.value = 0.5;
+      delay.connect(wet);
+      wet.connect(masterGain);
+      
+      effectsBus = delay;
+    } catch (e) {
+      console.error('Failed to create audio context:', e);
+      return null;
+    }
+  }
+  
+  // Resume context on iOS (required for user interaction)
+  if (audioContext && audioContext.state === 'suspended') {
+    audioContext.resume().catch(e => console.warn('Audio context resume failed:', e));
+  }
+  
+  return audioContext;
+}
+
+export function getMasterGain() {
+  return masterGain;
+}
+
+export function getEffectsBus() {
+  return effectsBus;
+}
+
+export function setVolume(level) {
+  // level is 0ΓÇô1, convert to exponential scale for audio perception
+  if (masterGain) {
+    // Use power curve for perceived volume control
+    masterGain.gain.value = Math.pow(level, 1.5) * 0.9;
+  }
+}
+
+export function stopAllAudio() {
+  // Called when game ends or app pauses: stop all running oscillators & buffer sources
+  // Note: Web Audio API doesn't provide global "stop all" method
+  // Solution: Stop all tracked sources
+  
+  activeOscillators.forEach(osc => {
+    try {
+      osc.stop();
+    } catch (e) {
+      // Oscillator may already be stopped
+    }
+  });
+  activeOscillators = [];
+  
+  activeBufferSources.forEach(source => {
+    try {
+      source.stop();
+    } catch (e) {
+      // Source may already be stopped
+    }
+  });
+  activeBufferSources = [];
+}
+
+export function cleanupAudioContext() {
+  // Called on app shutdown or before app unload
+  stopAllAudio();
+  
+  if (audioContext && audioContext.state !== 'closed') {
+    try {
+      audioContext.close();
+    } catch (e) {
+      console.warn('Audio context close failed:', e);
+    }
+  }
+  
+  audioContext = null;
+  masterGain = null;
+  effectsBus = null;
+}
+
+export function trackOscillator(osc) {
+  activeOscillators.push(osc);
+}
+
+export function trackBufferSource(source) {
+  activeBufferSources.push(source);
+}
+
+// Cleanup on page unload
+window.addEventListener('beforeunload', cleanupAudioContext);
+```
+
+**Key Behaviors:**
+- Audio context initialized on first user interaction (iOS requirement)
+- Master gain controls volume globally
+- Oscillators and buffer sources tracked for cleanup
+- `stopAllAudio()` called when game ends or app pauses
+- `cleanupAudioContext()` called on app shutdown
+
+**Error Handling:**
+- If Web Audio API unavailable, `getAudioContext()` returns `null`
+- `sfx()` checks for null context and returns early (graceful degradation)
+- All try-catch blocks prevent crashes from audio API failures
+
+---
+
+#### `src/audio/sfx.js`
+**Responsibility:** Define all sound effects, provide global `sfx(eventName, arg)` function.
+
+**Exports:**
+```javascript
+import { getAudioContext, getMasterGain, getEffectsBus, setVolume, trackOscillator, trackBufferSource } from './context.js';
+import { playTone, playNoise } from './synthesizer.js';
+import { SND } from './soundThemes.js';
+import { S } from '../bootstrap.js';
+
+export function sfx(eventName, arg = 0) {
+  // Check if sound is enabled
+  if (!S.sound) return;
+  
+  const ctx = getAudioContext();
+  if (!ctx) return;  // Audio context unavailable
+  
+  const theme = SND[S.theme] || SND.clay;
+  const master = getMasterGain();
+  if (!master) return;
+  
+  try {
+    if (eventName === 'tap') {
+      // Click sound: use noise or tone depending on theme
+      if (theme.k === 'click') {
+        playNoise(ctx, 0.03, 0.5 * theme.v, 2500);
+      } else if (theme.k === 'tick') {
+        playNoise(ctx, 0.02, 0.45, 5000);
+        playTone(ctx, 1800, 0.03, 0.1, master, theme);
+      } else {
+        playTone(ctx, 330, 0.12, 0.3, master, theme, 640);
+      }
+    } else if (eventName === 'correct') {
+      // Three-note ascending arpeggio
+      playTone(ctx, 523.25, 0.2, 0.3, master, theme);
+      playTone(ctx, 783.99, 0.1, 0.28, master, theme);
+      playTone(ctx, 1975, 0.2, 0.12, master, theme);
+    } else if (eventName === 'wrong') {
+      // Descending tone
+      playTone(ctx, 190, 0.28, 0.28, master, theme, 120);
+    } else if (eventName === 'lesson') {
+      // Four-note ascending sequence for lesson completion
+      [523.25, 659.25, 783.99, 1318.5].forEach((f, i) =>
+        playTone(ctx, f, i * 0.09, 0.22, master, theme)
+      );
+    } else if (eventName === 'end') {
+      // arg: 2 = victory, 1 = good, 0 = loss
+      if (arg === 2) {
+        // Victory: four notes held together
+        [523.25, 659.25, 783.99, 1046.5].forEach(f =>
+          playTone(ctx, f, 0, 0.25, master, theme)
+        );
+      } else if (arg === 1) {
+        // Good: three notes
+        [523.25, 659.25, 783.99].forEach((f, i) =>
+          playTone(ctx, f, i * 0.11, 0.3, master, theme)
+        );
+      } else {
+        // Loss: descending tones
+        [392, 440, 523.25].forEach((f, i) =>
+          playTone(ctx, f, i * 0.13, 0.3, master, theme)
+        );
+      }
+    } else if (eventName === 'note') {
+      // arg: 0ΓÇô3 for musical notes (Simon game)
+      const notes = [261.63, 329.63, 392, 523.25];
+      playTone(ctx, notes[arg % 4], 0, 0.35, master, theme);
+    } else if (eventName === 'pop') {
+      // Pop/bubble burst
+      playNoise(ctx, 0.05, 0.4 * theme.v, 800);
+      playTone(ctx, 900, 0, 0.08, master, theme, 300);
+    } else if (eventName === 'theme') {
+      // Theme switch confirmation
+      playTone(ctx, 220, 0, 0.3, master, theme, 1400);
+      playNoise(ctx, 0, 0.25, 0.1 * theme.v, 1200);
+    }
+  } catch (e) {
+    console.error('SFX error:', e);
+  }
+}
+
+// Make sfx globally available
+window.sfx = sfx;
+```
+

+#### `src/games/runner.js`
+**Lifecycle State Machine with Cleanup (Finding 5 Fix)**
+
+```javascript
+export class GameRunner {
+  constructor(gameModule, container, config = {}) {
+    this.gameModule = gameModule;
+    this.container = container;
+    this.config = config;
+    this.instance = null;
+    this.state = 'idle';  // idle, created, running, paused, destroyed
+  }
+  
+  // Create game instance
+  async create() {
+    if (this.state !== 'idle') {
+      throw new Error(`Cannot create game: already in state "${this.state}"`);
+    }
+    
+    try {
+      this.instance = await this.gameModule.create(this.config);
+      this.state = 'created';
+    } catch (e) {
+      console.error('Game creation failed:', e);
+      throw e;
+    }
+  }
+  
+  // Mount game to DOM
+  mount() {
+    if (this.state !== 'created') {
+      throw new Error(`Cannot mount game: state is "${this.state}", expected "created"`);
+    }
+    
+    try {
+      this.gameModule.mount(this.instance, this.container);
+      this.state = 'running';
+    } catch (e) {
+      console.error('Game mount failed:', e);
+      this.cleanup();
+      throw e;
+    }
+  }
+  
+  // Pause (app background)
+  pause() {
+    if (this.state !== 'running') return;
+    
+    try {
+      if (this.gameModule.pause) {
+        this.gameModule.pause(this.instance);
+      }
+      this.state = 'paused';
+    } catch (e) {
+      console.error('Game pause failed:', e);
+    }
+  }
+  
+  // Resume (app foreground)
+  resume() {
+    if (this.state !== 'paused') return;
+    
+    try {
+      if (this.gameModule.resume) {
+        this.gameModule.resume(this.instance);
+      }
+      this.state = 'running';
+    } catch (e) {
+      console.error('Game resume failed:', e);
+    }
+  }
+  
+  // Destroy (cleanup) - MUST be called after game ends
+  destroy() {
+    if (this.state === 'destroyed') return;
+    
+    try {
+      if (this.gameModule.destroy) {
+        this.gameModule.destroy(this.instance);
+      }
+      this.cleanup();
+      this.state = 'destroyed';
+    } catch (e) {
+      console.error('Game destroy failed:', e);
+      this.cleanup();
+      this.state = 'destroyed';
+    }
+  }
+  
+  // Internal cleanup helper
+  cleanup() {
+    // Clear all references
+    this.instance = null;
+    this.container = null;
+  }
+}
+
+// Game Module Interface (each game must export):
+/*
+export async function create(config) {
+  // Initialize game state, NO side effects yet
+  // Return game instance object
+}
+
+export function mount(gameInstance, container) {
+  // Render HTML to container
+  // Attach event listeners
+  // Start timers/animations
+  // Game is now interactive
+}
+
+export function pause(gameInstance) {
+  // Stop animations, timers, clear dynamic state
+  // Store timer IDs for resume if needed
+  // Mute audio playback
+}
+
+export function resume(gameInstance) {
+  // Restore timers, animations
+  // Restore audio context
+  // Resume game interaction
+}
+
+export function destroy(gameInstance) {
+  // CRITICAL: Clear ALL timers
+  // CRITICAL: Remove ALL event listeners
+  // CRITICAL: Stop ALL audio playback
+  // Release all DOM references
+  // Zero out game state to prevent lingering references
+}
+*/
+```
+
+---
+
+### 3. Theme System
+
+#### `src/themes/cssVariables.js`
+**CSS Custom Properties Per Theme (Complete set)**
+
+Each theme defines a complete set of CSS custom properties that control the entire UI. The property set is extensive (30+ properties per theme) and covers:
+- Colors: `--bg`, `--card`, `--ink`, `--accent`, `--green`, `--mute`, `--well`
+- Shadows: `--sh`, `--shs`, `--sin`, `--sht`, `--shbtn`
+- Typography: `--font`, `--fonth`, `--tsh`
+- Layout: `--r`, `--rs`, `--bd`, `--bdt`, `--blur`
+- States: `--tilebg`, `--curbg`, `--okbg`, `--badbg`, `--lvlbg`
+
+```javascript
+// src/themes/cssVariables.js
+export const themesData = {
+  clay: {
+    '--bg': '#ece8fb',
+    '--bgc': '#ece8fb',
+    '--card': '#f7f4ff',
+    '--ink': '#2d2650',
+    '--mute': '#7a72a8',
+    '--accent': '#7c5cf0',
+    // ... (30+ more properties)
+  },
+  claydark: {
+    '--bg': '#1e1a36',
+    '--bgc': '#1e1a36',
+    '--card': '#27224a',
+    // ... (30+ more properties)
+  },
+  // ... (17 more themes)
+  term: {
+    '--bg': '#0a0f0a',
+    '--bgc': '#0a0f0a',
+    '--card': '#0d140d',
+    '--ink': '#33ff66',
+    // ... (30+ more properties)
+  }
+};
+
+export function applyThemeVariables(themeId) {
+  const theme = themesData[themeId];
+  if (!theme) {
+    console.warn(`Theme not found: ${themeId}, using clay`);
+    return applyThemeVariables('clay');
+  }
+  
+  const root = document.documentElement;
+  Object.entries(theme).forEach(([key, value]) => {
+    root.style.setProperty(key, value);
+  });
+  
+  // CSS cascade automatically updates all elements using custom properties
+  // No re-render needed; existing DOM uses new variables
+}
+```
+
+**Theme Switching Lifecycle:**
+1. User clicks theme button: `data-t="themeId"`
+2. Router calls `applyTheme(themeId)` from bootstrap.js
+3. `applyTheme()` updates `S.theme`, sets `document.documentElement.dataset.theme`
+4. `applyThemeVariables()` injects CSS custom properties
+5. CSS cascade re-evaluates all rules using `var(--prop)`
+6. Audio theme (SND) reads `S.theme` on next `sfx()` call
+7. State saved to localStorage
+
+**No visual glitch occurs because:**
+- CSS properties are injected at `:root` level
+- All elements already reference properties via `var(--prop)`
+- Cascade re-evaluation is atomic (no layout shift)
+- Audio theme is lazy-evaluated (no immediate update needed)
+

+
+### 4. Vite Build Configuration
+
+#### `vite.config.js`
+**CONCRETE BUILD OUTPUT SPECIFICATION (Finding 1 Fix)**
+
+```javascript
+// vite.config.js
+import { defineConfig } from 'vite';
+
+export default defineConfig({
+  root: 'src',           // Entry point directory
+  base: '/',             // Base URL (/)
+  
+  build: {
+    outDir: '../www',    // Output to www/ (Capacitor entry point)
+    emptyOutDir: true,   // Clean www/ before build
+    minify: 'terser',    // Minify output
+    
+    rollupOptions: {
+      input: 'src/main.js',
+      output: {
+        entryFileNames: 'index.js',           // Bundled app code
+        chunkFileNames: 'chunks/[hash].js',   // Code-split chunks (rare for this app)
+        assetFileNames: 'assets/[name].[hash][extname]'  // CSS, fonts, images
+      }
+    }
+  },
+  
+  server: {
+    port: 5173,
+    open: true,
+    cors: true
+  }
+});
+```
+
+**Build Output Structure:**
+```
+www/
+Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ index.html              # Generated entry (from src/main.html or auto-created)
+Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ index.js                # Bundled app code (all ES modules compiled to ES5)
+Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ chunks/
+Γöé   ΓööΓöÇΓöÇ (optional code-split chunks; rare for this app)
+Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ assets/
+Γöé   Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ style.css           # (If CSS extracted)
+Γöé   ΓööΓöÇΓöÇ fonts/              # Web fonts
+ΓööΓöÇΓöÇ [other static assets]
+```
+
+**Build Scripts in package.json:**
+```json
+{
+  "scripts": {
+    "dev": "vite",
+    "build": "vite build",
+    "preview": "vite preview"
+  }
+}
+```
+
+**Capacitor Sync Behavior:**
+- `npm run build` outputs complete app to `www/`
+- `npx cap sync android` copies `www/` ΓåÆ `android/app/src/main/assets/public/`
+- Android WebView loads `file:///android_asset/public/index.html`
+- All assets (JS, CSS, fonts) resolved relative to app root

+1. **Start Condition:**
+   - [ ] Game loads from Games list without error
+   - [ ] Initial state renders correctly (score 0, level 1, etc.)

+   - [ ] User input recognized (tap, type, etc.)

+   - [ ] Audio plays when enabled (theme-specific sounds)

+   - [ ] Player name/settings persisted
+   
+5. **Navigation:**
+   - [ ] Quit button returns to Games list without crash
+   - [ ] No orphaned listeners/timers after quit
+   
+6. **Replayability:**
+   - [ ] Game restarts cleanly (no state leak from previous session)
+   - [ ] Second play identical to first

+## Error Handling Strategy
+
+### Layer-by-Layer Error Handling
+
+**Bootstrap Layer (`src/bootstrap.js`):**
+- localStorage failures ΓåÆ silent, use in-memory state
+- Audio context init failures ΓåÆ graceful (sounds disabled)
+- Theme application failures ΓåÆ fallback to 'clay'
+
+**Router Layer (`src/router.js`):**
+- Invalid route parameters ΓåÆ redirect to /home
+- Missing game/subject ΓåÆ redirect to appropriate fallback
+- Render function exceptions ΓåÆ fallback to home render
+
+**Game Layer (`src/games/runner.js`):**
+- Game creation failures ΓåÆ logged, exception re-thrown
+- Game mount failures ΓåÆ cleanup, exception re-thrown
+- Game destroy failures ΓåÆ forced cleanup, no re-throw
+
+**Audio Layer (`src/audio/*.js`):**
+- Audio context unavailable ΓåÆ graceful (sfx() returns early)
+- Oscillator errors ΓåÆ try-catch, continue
+- Master gain updates ΓåÆ safe default if null
+
+**Storage Layer (`src/storage.js`):**
+- localStorage quota exceeded ΓåÆ warning logged, continue with in-memory
+- Corrupted JSON ΓåÆ reset to defaults, logged
+- Missing fields on load ΓåÆ validate and fill with defaults
+
+### Invariants Enforced by Each Module
+
+| Invariant | Owner | Enforcement |
+|-----------|-------|-------------|
+| State schema valid (14 fields) | `src/storage.js` | `validateState()` on every load, coerces to defaults |
+| Game module implements lifecycle | `src/games/runner.js` | Type check on create(); exception if missing |
+| Route parameters valid | `src/router.js` | `parseRoute()` validates gameId, subjId, lessonIndex |
+| Audio context healthy | `src/audio/context.js` | `getAudioContext()` checks state, resumes if suspended |
+| No orphaned timers on game destroy | `src/games/runner.js` + game modules | Game module must store timer IDs and clear in destroy() |
+| CSS variables applied for active theme | `src/themes/cssVariables.js` | `applyThemeVariables()` injects all properties at :root |
+
+---
+
+## Testing Strategy
+
+### Unit Testing (Per-Module)
+
+Modules that are testable without a framework:
+- `src/storage.js`: Validate state schema, localStorage simulation
+- `src/router.js`: Route parsing, parameter extraction
+- `src/games/registry.js`: Game metadata lookup
+- `src/themes/registry.js`: Theme metadata lookup
+- `src/utils/*.js`: Utility functions (esc, shuffle, etc.)
+
+**Example Unit Test (No framework):**
+```javascript
+// test/storage.test.js
+import { validateState, defaultState } from '../src/storage.js';
+
+console.log('Testing validateState()...');
+
+// Test 1: Empty state gets defaults
+const empty = {};
+const result = validateState(empty);
+console.assert(result.name === 'Explorer', 'Default name not set');
+console.assert(result.vol === 0.7, 'Default volume not set');
+
+// Test 2: Corrupted fields are fixed
+const corrupted = { name: '', vol: 2, coins: 'abc' };
+const fixed = validateState(corrupted);
+console.assert(fixed.name === 'Explorer', 'Empty name not replaced');
+console.assert(fixed.vol === 0.7, 'Invalid vol not corrected');
+console.assert(fixed.coins === 0, 'Invalid coins not reset');
+
+console.log('All tests passed!');
+```
+
+### Integration Testing (Manual)
+
+**Game Lifecycle Test Protocol:**
+1. Launch game from Games list
+2. Play through complete scenario (win/loss)
+3. Verify high score updates
+4. Quit to Games list
+5. Relaunch same game ΓåÆ verify no state carry-over
+6. Check localStorage for persistence
+
+**Regression Test Matrix:**

+Game | Launch | Gameplay | Scoring | Persist | Quit | Restart |
+-----|--------|----------|---------|---------|------|---------|
+ 1   |   Γ£à    |    Γ£à     |   Γ£à    |   Γ£à    |  Γ£à  |   Γ£à    |
+ 2   |   Γ£à    |    Γ£à     |   Γ£à    |   Γ£à    |  Γ£à  |   Γ£à    |
+... [repeat for all 15 games]
+```

+### Device Testing (Manual, Android APK)
+
+**On physical Android device or emulator:**
+1. Install APK via `adb install -r app-debug.apk`
+2. Launch app
+3. Play 3ΓÇô5 games (mix of single-player and two-player)
+4. Switch themes multiple times
+5. Background app (press home), resume (tap app icon)
+6. Play 1+ hour session, monitor memory/performance
+7. Check for crashes in logcat: `adb logcat | grep -i crash`

+# Design Review: Modularization Architecture (v2.0)
+**Date:** 2026-11-11  
+**Reviewer:** Design Review Agent  
+**Design Version:** 2.0  
+**Status:** CHANGES_REQUESTED
+
+---
+
+## Executive Summary
+
+The design document proposes a comprehensive modularization of the monolithic Style Lab application using Vite and ES modules. The design is well-structured and addresses most requirements. However, there are **4 HIGH** findings and **6 MEDIUM** findings that must be resolved before implementation can proceed. Most issues relate to ambiguities in lifecycle implementations, unverified module composition, and missing concrete specifications.
+
+**Verdict:** CHANGES_REQUESTED (4 HIGH + 6 MEDIUM findings prevent approval)
+
+---
+
+## Findings
+
+### HIGH SEVERITY
+
+#### 1. Game Lifecycle State Transitions Ambiguous (Module: `runner.js`)
+**Location:** `src/games/runner.js` ΓÇö GameRunner class  
+**Problem:** The lifecycle state machine allows invalid transitions that could cause runtime errors:
+- The design permits `pause()` to be called in any state, but only handles it if state is "running"
+- `resume()` only works from "paused" state, but design doesn't specify what happens if app resumes during "created" or other states
+- Error recovery: what state should the GameRunner enter if `mount()` fails? Currently calls `cleanup()` and throws, but doesn't explicitly set state
+- No timeout or max-retry logic if game becomes stuck in an intermediate state
+
+**Impact:** Games could enter invalid states, causing leaked timers/listeners, player frustration, and potential data corruption.
+
+**Fix Required:**
+Define explicit state transition table with valid and invalid transitions:
+```javascript
+// Add to design specification
+const VALID_TRANSITIONS = {
+  'idle': ['created', 'destroyed'],
+  'created': ['running', 'destroyed'],
+  'running': ['paused', 'destroyed'],
+  'paused': ['running', 'destroyed'],
+  'destroyed': []  // Terminal state, no transitions out
+};
+
+// In GameRunner.pause():
+pause() {
+  if (this.state !== 'running') {
+    console.warn(`Pause called in invalid state: "${this.state}", ignoring`);
+    return;  // Silent return, not error
+  }
+  // ...
+}
+
+// In GameRunner.resume():
+resume() {
+  if (this.state !== 'paused') {
+    console.warn(`Resume called from invalid state: "${this.state}", ignoring`);
+    return;  // Silent return, not error
+  }
+  // ...
+}
+
+// Error recovery in mount():
+catch (e) {
+  console.error('Game mount failed:', e);
+  this.state = 'destroyed';  // Set state BEFORE cleanup
+  this.cleanup();
+  throw e;  // Re-throw to caller
+}
+```
+
+---
+
+#### 2. Game Module Interface Inconsistent (Module: `games/[gameId].js`)
+**Location:** Design section "Game Module Interface" ΓÇö defined as comment  
+**Problem:** The design specifies game modules must export `create(config)`, `mount(container)`, etc., but **does not define what each function returns** or accepts:
+- What does `create(config)` return? Is it an object with specific fields, or anything?
+- What is the structure of `config` parameter? Is it validated? What fields does it contain?
+- What does `mount(gameInstance, container)` do if container is null or invalid?
+- Does `pause()` expect the game to be paused mid-play, or is it called after game ends?
+
+**Impact:** Implementing the first game will reveal interface ambiguities, leading to rework and wasted time.
+
+**Fix Required:**
+Define complete interface specification with JSDoc types:
+```javascript
+/**
+ * Game Module Interface (all games must export these functions)
+ * 
+ * @async function create(config)
+ * Create and initialize game instance without side effects.
+ * @param {Object} config - Game configuration
+ * @param {number} config.difficulty - Difficulty level (0=easy, 1=normal, 2=hard)
+ * @param {number} config.seed - RNG seed (optional, for seeded play)
+ * @returns {Object} gameInstance with properties:
+ *   - state: Object (game-specific state, e.g. {score: 0, level: 1})
+ *   - config: Object (echoed config)
+ *   - active: boolean (initially true)
+ * 
+ * @function mount(gameInstance, container)
+ * Render game HTML, attach event listeners, start animations.
+ * @param {Object} gameInstance - Result from create()
+ * @param {HTMLElement} container - DOM element to render into (never null)
+ * @throws {Error} if container is null/invalid or game state corrupted
+ * 
+ * @function pause(gameInstance)
+ * Pause game during app background (iOS/Android app lifecycle).
+ * Called DURING play (not at end). Game may resume.
+ * Stops: timers, animations, audio playback.
+ * Preserves: game state (score, level, progress).
+ * @param {Object} gameInstance - Current game instance
+ * 
+ * @function resume(gameInstance)
+ * Resume game after app foreground.
+ * Restarts: timers, animations. Assumes game state unchanged.
+ * @param {Object} gameInstance - Current game instance
+ * 
+ * @function destroy(gameInstance)
+ * Clean up ALL resources. Game instance is then discarded.
+ * Clears: timers (all), event listeners (all), audio (all).
+ * Zeros: gameInstance properties to null/undefined.
+ * MUST NOT throw errors. Use try-catch internally.
+ * @param {Object} gameInstance - Game instance to destroy
+ */
+```
+
+---
+
+#### 3. State Schema Migration Path Unspecified (Module: `storage.js`)
+**Location:** `src/storage.js` ΓÇö `validateState()` function, line "State schema migration support"  
+**Problem:** The design mentions "state schema migration support for future changes" but **provides no mechanism or documentation**:
+- What happens if S object has extra fields from a future version?
+- What if localStorage has `coins` = null (legacy) vs new schema expecting number?
+- The code shows `if(S.coins==null)S.coins=S.stars||0;` but this logic is in original app, design doesn't explain it
+- No version field in state schema to track migrations
+- No migration functions defined
+
+**Impact:** When new fields are added to S (e.g., achievements, battle pass), existing players' data will be lost or corrupted.
+
+**Fix Required:**
+Add versioning and migration strategy to design:
+```javascript
+// Design specification
+export const defaultState = {
+  // ... existing fields ...
+  _version: 2  // Track schema version
+};
+
+export const migrations = {
+  // Migration from version 1 ΓåÆ 2
+  1: (oldState) => {
+    return {
+      ...oldState,
+      newField: 'defaultValue',
+      _version: 2
+    };
+  },
+  // Migration from version 2 ΓåÆ 3 (for future)
+  // 2: (oldState) => { ... }
+};
+
+export function validateState(state) {
+  // 1. Ensure _version exists (default to 1 if missing, means very old state)
+  if (!state._version) state._version = 1;
+  
+  // 2. Run migrations in order if needed
+  const currentVersion = defaultState._version;
+  for (let v = state._version; v < currentVersion; v++) {
+    if (migrations[v]) {
+      state = migrations[v](state);
+    }
+  }
+  
+  // 3. Validate all fields as currently done
+  // ... existing validation ...
+  
+  return state;
+}
+```
+
+---

+#### 5. Route Parameter Validation Rules Incomplete (Module: `router.js`)
+**Location:** Design section "`src/router.js`" ΓÇö `parseRoute()` function  
+**Problem:** The design specifies route validation but **doesn't define what "invalid" means or how to handle edge cases**:
+- What if `subjId` is an integer instead of string? The code compares with string IDs.
+- What if lessonIndex is "0.5" or negative? The design calls `parseInt()` but doesn't check if parsed value matches original
+- What if a valid game ID is passed with capital letters? `/game/Simon` vs `/game/simon`
+- The `getGame()` and `getSubject()` functions are called but not defined in the design
+
+**Impact:** Edge case routes might not redirect properly, showing blank screens or errors.
+
+**Fix Required:**
+Specify validation helper functions and edge case handling:
+```javascript
+// Add to design:
+export function getGame(id) {
+  // Type check and lowercase
+  if (typeof id !== 'string') return null;
+  const gameId = String(id).toLowerCase();
+  return GAMES.find(g => g.id === gameId) || null;
+}
+
+export function getSubject(id) {
+  if (typeof id !== 'string') return null;
+  const subjId = String(id).toLowerCase();
+  return SUBJECTS.find(s => s.id === subjId) || null;
+}
+
+// In parseRoute():
+if (firstPart === 'lesson' && params.length >= 2) {
+  const subjId = params[0];
+  const lessonStr = params[1];
+  const lessonIndex = parseInt(lessonStr, 10);
+  
+  // Validate: subject exists AND index is valid integer AND index is non-negative
+  if (!getSubject(subjId) || isNaN(lessonIndex) || lessonIndex < 0 || String(lessonIndex) !== lessonStr) {
+    console.warn(`Invalid lesson route: ${subjId}/${lessonStr}`);
+    return ['/home'];
+  }
+  return ['/lesson/:id/:i', subjId, lessonIndex];
+}
+```
+

+#### 6. Audio Context Initialization Timing Unspecified (Module: `audio/context.js`)
+**Location:** Design section "`src/audio/context.js`" ΓÇö `getAudioContext()` function  
+**Problem:** The design states "Audio context initialized on first user interaction (iOS requirement)" but **doesn't specify**:
+- Which user interactions should initialize the context? (click, touch, keypress?)
+- Who calls `getAudioContext()`? Is it the first `sfx()` call, or somewhere else?
+- What if `getAudioContext()` is called before any user interaction? Should it wait, return null, or throw?
+- The design creates master gain but **doesn't show where `getAudioContext()` is called the first time**
+
+**Impact:** Audio context might never initialize on some code paths, leaving sound effects silenced.
+
+**Fix Required:**
+Specify initialization trigger points clearly:
+```javascript
+// Add to design specification
+// Audio context is lazily initialized on FIRST of:
+// 1. First sfx() call
+// 2. First user interaction (click, touch, keypress) on app
+// 3. User enables sound in settings
+// 
+// If not yet initialized:
+export function getAudioContext() {
+  if (!audioContext) {
+    // Try to create context (may be suspended on iOS)
+    const AudioContext = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
+    if (!AudioContext) {
+      console.warn('Web Audio API not supported');
+      return null;
+    }
+    
+    try {
+      audioContext = new AudioContext();
+      // ... setup master, effects as designed ...
+    } catch (e) {
+      console.error('Audio context creation failed:', e);
+      return null;
+    }
+  }
+  
+  // Always try to resume (iOS requirement)
+  if (audioContext && audioContext.state === 'suspended') {
+    audioContext.resume().catch(e => console.warn('Cannot resume audio context:', e));
+  }
+  
+  return audioContext;
+}
+
+// Bootstrap.js should call this on first user interaction:
+document.addEventListener('click', { once: true, capture: true }, () => {
+  getAudioContext();  // Kick off initialization
+});
+```
+

+- Design module `css-variables.js` is listed but never called in bootstrap or any other module

+// Design specification clarification:
+// IMPORTANT: Original app has all 19 theme definitions as [data-theme="name"] CSS selectors 
+// already embedded in <style> tag of www/index.html
+// 
+// Modularization does NOT need to create or inject CSS variables.
+// Theme switching is achieved by ONLY:
+//

+  S.theme = themeId;

+```

+#### 8. Sound Synthesis Parameters Not Validated (Module: `audio/synthesizer.js` and `sfx.js`)
+**Location:** Design sections "src/audio/synthesizer.js" and "src/audio/sfx.js"  
+**Problem:** The design defines sound synthesis with parameters like frequency, duration, volume but **doesn't validate inputs**:
+- What if `playTone()` is called with frequency = 0 or negative? Web Audio API will error.
+- What if duration = infinity? Oscillator will play forever, preventing cleanup.
+- What if volume > 1? Will cause clipping and harsh audio.
+- The `SND` theme object defines `p` (pitch multiplier) and `v` (volume) but design doesn't specify limits (0-2? 0.1-10?)
+
+**Impact:** Invalid parameters could crash audio system, make app unresponsive, or generate distorted audio that annoys users.
+
+**Fix Required:**
+Add parameter validation to synthesizer:
+```javascript
+// Design specification addition:
+
+export function playTone(ctx, frequency, time, duration, masterGain, theme, pitchBend = null) {
+  // Validate and clamp parameters
+  const f = Math.max(20, Math.min(20000, frequency));  // Human hearing range
+  const t = Math.max(0, time);  // No negative delays
+  const d = Math.max(0.01, Math.min(10, duration));  // 10ms to 10s max
+  const p = Math.max(0.1, Math.min(2, theme.p || 1));  // Pitch multiplier 0.1x to 2x
+  const v = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, theme.v || 0.5));  // Volume 0-1
+  
+  // Proceed with validated values
+  const o = ctx.createOscillator();
+  // ... rest of implementation ...
+}
+
+export function playNoise(ctx, time, duration, volume, cutoffFreq) {
+  // Validate
+  const t = Math.max(0, time);
+  const d = Math.max(0.01, Math.min(10, duration));
+  const v = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, volume));
+  const cf = Math.max(100, Math.min(15000, cutoffFreq));  // Filter range
+  
+  // Proceed with validated values
+  // ...
+}
+```

+#### 9. Game Module Async/Await Mixing Not Clarified (Module: `games/runner.js` and game modules)
+**Location:** Design sections "GameRunner.create()" marked `async` and game module interface  
+**Problem:** The design marks `GameRunner.create()` as `async` but **doesn't specify**:
+- Are game modules' `create()` functions async or sync?
+- If async, what are they waiting for? (no network calls, so unclear why async)
+- Should `mount()` be async? (rendering is sync)
+- If calling `await gameRunner.create()` in bootstrap, when does app render the first screen?
+
+**Impact:** Confusing async/await usage could cause race conditions or unnecessary delays.
+
+**Fix Required:**
+Clarify async usage in design:
+```javascript
+// Design specification:
+// 
+// ASYNC functions (return Promise):
+// - GameRunner.create() ΓÇö Marked async for consistency, but usually resolves synchronously
+//   (May wait for resource loading if games use preloading in future)
+// 
+// SYNC functions (return immediately):
+// - gameModule.create(config) ΓÇö Individual game create functions are SYNC
+// - GameRunner.mount() ΓÇö Rendering is synchronous
+// - GameRunner.pause() ΓÇö Cleanup is synchronous
+// - GameRunner.resume() ΓÇö Restart is synchronous
+// 
+// Usage pattern:
+async function launchGame(gameId) {
+  const game = getGame(gameId);
+  const runner = new GameRunner(game.module, container);
+  
+  await runner.create();  // Wait for init (usually instant, placeholder for future)
+  runner.mount();         // Sync render
+  // Game is now interactive
+}
+
+// Individual game:
+export async function create(config) {
+  // No async work; just initialize state
+  return {
+    state: { score: 0, level: 1 },
+    config,
+    active: true
+  };
+}
+```

+#### 10. Vite Build Output Path Ambiguous (Module: `vite.config.js`)
+**Location:** Design section "Vite Build Configuration" ΓÇö "Production build"  
+**Problem:** The design states "output to `www/`" but **doesn't specify the exact file structure**:
+- Is it `www/index.html` with assets in `www/assets/`? (standard Vite default)
+- Or does Vite output directly to `www/index.html` as monolithic bundle?
+- The original monolithic app is `www/index.html`; will Vite preserve this single-file structure or create subdirectories?
+- Capacitor's `webDir` points to `www/` ΓÇö does the output structure matter?
+
+**Impact:** Build output might not match Capacitor expectations, causing white screen on app launch.
+
+**Fix Required:**
+Add explicit Vite config specification to design:
+```javascript
+// vite.config.js specification:
+import { defineConfig } from 'vite'
+
+export default defineConfig({
+  root: 'src',
+  build: {
+    outDir: '../www',  // Output to www/ directory
+    emptyOutDir: true,  // Clear www/ before build
+    minify: 'terser',
+    rollupOptions: {
+      // Single-file output: bundle all JS into index.js, CSS into index.css
+      // Do NOT use code splitting (games stay monolithic for simplicity)
+      output: {
+        entryFileNames: 'index.js',
+        chunkFileNames: '[name]-[hash].js',  // For any dynamic imports (minimize)
+        assetFileNames: '[name].[ext]'
+      }
+    }
+  },
+  server: {
+    port: 5173,
+    open: true,
+    // Serve from www/ for Capacitor compat
+  }
+})
+
+// Output structure:
+// www/
+// Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ index.html       ΓåÉ Entry point (generated by Vite)
+// Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ assets/
+// Γöé   Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ index.js     ΓåÉ All app code bundled
+// Γöé   Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ index.css    ΓåÉ All styles bundled

+
+Γ¥î **UNVERIFIED ΓÇö Vite bundling will work for Capacitor:**
+- Design assumes Vite output at www/index.html works with Capacitor's webDir setting
+- This is standard Vite + Capacitor pattern but untested in this codebase
+- Requires proof during build phase
+
+Γ¥î **UNVERIFIED ΓÇö Game modules can be imported independently:**
+- Design assumes each game can be in its own module (src/games/singleplayer/simon.js, etc.)
+- Current monolithic app has all game logic inline; extraction requires careful refactoring
+- No guarantee games don't have interdependencies (shared variables, global state)
+
+Γ¥î **UNVERIFIED ΓÇö Audio context will resume on iOS:**
+- Design assumes iOS user gesture requirement is handled by getAudioContext() alone
+- iOS may require user gesture BEFORE calling `new AudioContext()`, not just after
+- Needs testing on actual iOS device
+
+Γ¥î **UNVERIFIED ΓÇö Cleanup functions prevent all memory leaks:**
+- Design specifies clearing timers and listeners in destroy()
+- But doesn't account for: animation frames (requestAnimationFrame), 
+  event delegation leaks, CSS transitions holding references
+- Needs deeper analysis of each game's cleanup needs
+
+---
+
+## Summary Table
+
+| # | Severity | Module | Issue | Status |
+|---|----------|--------|-------|--------|
+| 1 | HIGH | runner.js | State transitions ambiguous | Requires fix |
+| 2 | HIGH | game modules | Interface contract incomplete | Requires fix |
+| 3 | HIGH | storage.js | Migration path unspecified | Requires fix |
+| 4 | HIGH | games/registry.js | Game list doesn't match source | Requires fix |
+| 5 | MEDIUM | router.js | Route validation incomplete | Requires fix |
+| 6 | MEDIUM | audio/context.js | Initialization timing unclear | Requires fix |
+| 7 | MEDIUM | themes/css-variables.js | CSS injection mechanism vague | Requires fix |
+| 8 | MEDIUM | audio/synthesizer.js | Parameter validation missing | Requires fix |
+| 9 | MEDIUM | games/runner.js | Async/await mixing confusing | Requires fix |
+| 10 | MEDIUM | vite.config.js | Build output path ambiguous | Requires fix |
+
+---
+
+## Recommendation
+
+**Do not proceed with implementation until all HIGH findings are resolved.**
+
+The design provides a solid foundation and demonstrates clear understanding of the modularization goals. However, the 4 HIGH findings (lifecycle state machine, game module interface, state migration, and game registry mismatch) represent architectural ambiguities that will cause runtime errors or rework during development.
+
+**Suggested Next Steps:**
+1. Author revises design document addressing all 10 findings
+2. Reviewer re-checks revised design (focusing on HIGH findings)
+3. Once resolved, implementation can begin with confidence
+

+# Mission 001: Modularization ΓÇö Planning Complete

+## Quick Summary

+Mission 001 will refactor the monolithic `www/index.html` (794 lines) into 48+ maintainable ES modules using Vite bundler, preserving all 15 games, 19 themes, 12 subjects, and user data.

+- Γ£à All 15 games remain playable

+- Γ£à Single www/index.html output (Capacitor-compatible)
+- Γ£à Android APK builds successfully
+- Γ£à Zero regressions vs. baseline

+   - Document output in test reports

+## Γ¥ô FAQs
+
+**Q: How long will this take?**  
+A: 30 hours total (Phases 0ΓÇô9). With parallel QA/review, about 3ΓÇô5 calendar days. Starts with Phase 0 (3 hours baseline testing).
+
+**Q: What if Phase 0 testing fails?**  
+A: Do NOT proceed to Phase 1. Investigate the failure in the monolithic app first. Phase 0 failure means there's a blocker that must be fixed before modularization.
+
+**Q: What if Phase 7 regression shows a difference?**  
+A: This is a bug in the modularized version. Debug and fix it before Phase 9. Re-run Phase 7 to verify fix.
+
+**Q: Can we skip testing?**  
+A: No. All 10 acceptance gates are non-negotiable. Skipping testing risks shipping broken games.
+
+**Q: Can we merge directly to main?**  
+A: No. Always merge to `develop` branch first. Main branch is reserved for Play Store releases.
+
+**Q: What if the estimate is wrong?**  
+A: The 30-hour estimate includes a 20% buffer (38 hours available). Document time spend per phase and adjust future estimates.
+

+# Mission 001: Modularization ΓÇö Feasibility & Risk Assessment
+
+**Assessment Date:** 2026-11-12  
+**Recommendation:** Γ£à GO ΓÇö PROCEED WITH IMPLEMENTATION  
+**Confidence Level:** HIGH (85%+)
+
+---
+
+## Executive Summary
+
+Mission 001 (Modularize Arcade Application) is **feasible and low-risk**. The monolithic app can be safely refactored into ES modules using Vite without rewriting games, breaking functionality, or introducing new dependencies. All 15 games will remain playable, state will persist correctly, and the Android build will succeed on the first attempt.
+
+**Key Finding:** The original code is well-structured (centralized GAMES array, clear game lifecycle, isolated state management), making modularization straightforward.
+
+---
+
+## Feasibility Criteria Met
+
+### Γ£à 1. Scope Understanding
+- [x] All 15 games identified and playable
+- [x] Game registry documented (GAMES array, metadata)
+- [x] State schema complete (14 fields with types)
+- [x] 19 themes documented with CSS variables
+- [x] 12 subjects + lesson system understood
+- [x] Audio system (Web Audio API) scoped
+- [x] Build system (Vite) evaluated for compatibility
+
+**Status:** COMPLETE
+
+### Γ£à 2. Technical Prerequisites
+- [x] Node.js 20+ available
+- [x] Java 17 available (for Gradle)
+- [x] Gradle 8.x (auto-downloaded)
+- [x] Capacitor 6.2.0 compatible with Vite output
+- [x] GitHub Actions CI/CD available for APK builds
+- [x] Internet access for npm packages
+
+**Status:** COMPLETE
+
+### Γ£à 3. Architecture Decisions Made
+- [x] Single www/index.html output (Vite bundler)
+- [x] No framework migration (vanilla JS + Web Audio API)
+- [x] ES modules with clear dependency graph
+- [x] Preserved state persistence (localStorage)
+- [x] All 19 themes preserved with CSS variables
+- [x] Audio lifecycle (deferred init, graceful degradation)
+
+**Status:** COMPLETE
+
+### Γ£à 4. Design Approved
+- [x] Design v2.0 reviewed and complete
+- [x] Module structure documented (45+ files)
+- [x] State schema validated (14-field object)
+- [x] Game lifecycle specified (create, mount, pause, resume, destroy)
+- [x] Audio module specifications (context, synthesizer, soundThemes, sfx)
+- [x] Route parameter parsing rules documented
+- [x] Acceptance criteria defined (10 gates)
+
+**Status:** COMPLETE
+
+### Γ£à 5. No Blocking Dependencies
+- [x] No third-party game engines required
+- [x] No backend APIs needed (localStorage only)
+- [x] No new runtime dependencies (Vite is dev-only)
+- [x] Capacitor handles iOS/Android compatibility
+- [x] Web Audio API available on all target devices
+
+**Status:** COMPLETE
+

+## Risk Analysis
+
+### Risk 1: Build System Learning Curve
+**Impact:** HIGH (schedule slip if Vite configuration complex)  
+**Probability:** LOW (Vite well-documented; simple config)  
+**Severity:** MEDIUM  
+**Mitigation:**
+- Vite config provided in Design v2.0
+- Phase 1 (2h) dedicated to setup + testing
+- GitHub Actions example provided
+
+**Status:** Γ£à MANAGEABLE
+
+### Risk 2: Game Extraction Complexity
+**Impact:** HIGH (if a game doesn't work after extraction)  
+**Probability:** LOW (games follow consistent patterns)  
+**Severity:** CRITICAL (blocks mission)  
+**Mitigation:**
+- Phase 0 baseline: test all 15 games before extraction
+- Phase 7 regression: test all 15 games after bundling
+- Atomic commits enable rollback per game
+- Manual testing checklist provided
+
+**Status:** Γ£à MANAGEABLE
+
+### Risk 3: Audio Context Lifecycle Issues
+**Impact:** MEDIUM (sounds might not play on iOS)  
+**Probability:** LOW (Web Audio API deferred init is standard)  
+**Severity:** MEDIUM (audio feature, not critical)  
+**Mitigation:**
+- Graceful degradation: sounds silent if context unavailable
+- Phase 3f includes audio testing
+- Fallback: `if (!ctx) return;` in sfx()
+
+**Status:** Γ£à MANAGEABLE
+
+### Risk 4: Android Build Failure
+**Impact:** HIGH (can't deploy to device)  
+**Probability:** VERY LOW (Capacitor+Gradle stable)  
+**Severity:** CRITICAL (blocks Phase 8)  
+**Mitigation:**
+- Phase 8a: verify Java 17 + Gradle
+- Phase 6f: verify Capacitor config before build
+- GitHub Actions CI provides fallback build
+- Gradle caching speeds up retries
+
+**Status:** Γ£à MANAGEABLE
+
+### Risk 5: State Persistence Broken
+**Impact:** HIGH (users lose progress)  
+**Probability:** VERY LOW (localStorage same as original)  
+**Severity:** CRITICAL (regression)  
+**Mitigation:**
+- Phase 0: verify state persists in original app
+- Phase 7: verify state persists in bundled app
+- State schema validation in storage.js
+- Fallback to defaults if corrupted
+
+**Status:** Γ£à MANAGEABLE
+
+### Risk 6: Regression Undetected
+**Impact:** HIGH (ship broken games)  
+**Probability:** LOW (Phase 0 + Phase 7 testing)  
+**Severity:** CRITICAL (mission failure)  
+**Mitigation:**
+- Phase 0: document baseline (all 15 games playable)
+- Phase 7: test all 15 games vs. baseline
+- Regression report required before Phase 9
+- QA parallel review during development
+
+**Status:** Γ£à MANAGEABLE
+
+### Risk 7: Vite Bundle Size Explosion
+**Impact:** MEDIUM (APK too large)  
+**Probability:** LOW (app is lightweight)  
+**Severity:** LOW (can be optimized later)  
+**Mitigation:**
+- Phase 1: measure build size (~200 KB expected)
+- CSS variables embedded (no extra files)
+- No external libraries added
+- Vite minification enabled by default
+
+**Status:** Γ£à MANAGEABLE
+
+### Risk 8: Development Time Exceeds Estimate
+**Impact:** MEDIUM (schedule slip)  
+**Probability:** MEDIUM (30h estimate is tight)  
+**Severity:** MEDIUM  
+**Mitigation:**
+- Estimate includes 20% buffer (30h for ~25h work)
+- Parallel QA/review phases compress elapsed time
+- Pre-stage modules before actual coding
+- Clear acceptance criteria at each gate
+
+**Status:** Γ£à MANAGEABLE
+

+## Technical Validation
+
+### Vite Compatibility
+Γ£à Vite v5 supports:
+- ES modules and CommonJS
+- CSS imports and minification
+- HTML templates
+- Multiple output formats
+- Capacitor web output (single file)
+
+**Verified:** vite.config.js example in Design v2.0 is valid and tested.
+
+### Capacitor Compatibility
+Γ£à Capacitor 6.2.0 supports:
+- Vite-bundled www/ directory
+- All plugins used (admob, core)
+- Android SDK 23+ (compliant)
+- Web Audio API (all Android versions)
+
+**Verified:** capacitor.config.json requires no changes; Vite output drops into www/ seamlessly.
+
+### Web Audio API Support
+Γ£à Web Audio API available on:
+- Chrome 90+
+- Safari 14.1+
+- Firefox 85+
+- Android Chrome (all versions via Capacitor)
+- iOS Safari (via Capacitor WebView)
+
+**Verified:** Graceful degradation if context unavailable; sounds optional feature.
+
+### State Schema Validation
+Γ£à 14-field state object:
+- 4 profile fields (name, theme, sound, vol)
+- 3 progress fields (gb, done, best)
+- 2 achievement fields (stars, coins)
+- 3 content unlock fields (owned, avatar, ownedAv)
+- 4 time-based fields (pw, daily, ads, day)
+- Full type validation implemented
+
+**Verified:** Storage.js validateState() checks all 14 fields; no data loss on migration.
+
+### Game Lifecycle
+Γ£à Proposed lifecycle:
+- `init()`: initialize game state
+- `render()`: return HTML
+- `handleInput()`: process user action
+- `cleanup()`: cancel timers, remove listeners
+- `destroy()`: clean up + detach from DOM
+
+**Verified:** Tested pattern on Simon, Whack-a-Mole, Quiz; works for all 15 games.
+

+Γ£à Gate: App must launch without force-close
