+# Mission 001: Modularize Arcade Application ΓÇö Implementation Plan
+
+**Task ID:** mission-001-modularization  
+**Workflow:** overnight-delivery  
+**Design Reference:** Design v2.0 (02-design.md)  
+**Repository:** nitinsameer/arcade-games  
+**Target Branch:** feature/modularization (never merge to main)  
+**Baseline Build:** www/index.html (794 lines, ~50 KB monolithic)  
+**Output Target:** www/index.html (Vite-bundled), ES modules in src/
+
+---
+
+## Executive Summary
+
+This plan executes Mission 001 as a phased refactor of the monolithic Style Lab arcade application into maintainable ES modules with Vite bundler. All 15 games, 19 themes, 12 subjects, and user data preservation are **non-negotiable**. The work is structured into 9 phases (Baseline ΓåÆ Integration ΓåÆ Testing ΓåÆ Android Build), each leaving the codebase in a buildable state. Estimated effort: 19 hours over 3 days with parallel QA/review cycles.
+
+**Quality Gates:**
+- Γ£à All 15 games playable without errors
+- Γ£à All 19 themes apply correctly
+- Γ£à All 12 subjects + lessons unlock properly
+- Γ£à User progress persists across sessions
+- Γ£à Android Gradle build succeeds
+- Γ£à APK installs and launches on device
+- Γ£à Zero regression failures
+- Γ£à Zero critical defects
+
+---
+
+## Pre-Implementation Checklist
+
+### Prerequisites Verification
+- [ ] Node.js 20+ installed: `node --version` (target: v20.x)
+- [ ] npm 10+ installed: `npm --version`
+- [ ] Java 17 installed: `java -version` (for Gradle)
+- [ ] Git worktree clean: `git status` (no uncommitted changes)
+- [ ] Internet access: Can download npm packages
+- [ ] 2+ GB free disk space (node_modules, android/)
+
+### Approval Gate (MUST READ)
+- [ ] **Design v2.0 reviewed and approved**
+- [ ] **All 15 games identified and playable** (baseline test completed)
+- [ ] **15-game preservation requirement understood** (no game rewrites)
+- [ ] **No production behavior changes** during refactoring
+- [ ] **Scope constraints accepted:** Vite only, no React/Kotlin, single www/index.html output
+

+**Acceptance Criterion:** A complete Baseline Test Report documenting all 15 games as PASS or identifying blockers.

+
+- [ ] **Step 1: Install Dependencies & Verify Build**
+  - Run: `npm install` in repository root
+  - Verify: `npm list @capacitor/core` shows version 6.2.0
+  - Expected: All packages installed without errors
+  - Output: `node_modules/` directory present with all 4 dependencies
+  
+- [ ] **Step 2: Open Monolithic App in Browser**
+  - Open `www/index.html` in Chrome (or Firefox) using local file:// URL or `npx http-server www`
+  - Navigate to `http://localhost:8080/` if using http-server
+  - Expected: App loads without console errors
+  - Output: Home screen visible with "Style Lab" header, profile section, subject list, themes visible
+
+- [ ] **Step 3: Verify Current Build System**
+  - Check `package.json` for build scripts (currently none)
+  - Note: Current build is manual (no webpack/Vite), served as-is by Capacitor
+  - Document in baseline report: "No build system in place; Vite will be introduced"
+  - Output: Baseline report section on current build state

+  - Expected: All 15 games listed with metadata confirmed

+## Phase 2c: Router Module (Hash-Based Navigation)
+
+- [ ] **Step 4: Implement src/router.js**
+  - Reference: Design v2.0, Section "Router Module"
+  - Implement:
+    - Screen registry: map route paths to render functions
+    - `go(path)` function:
+      - Update `window.location.hash = path`
+      - Parse route and render corresponding screen
+      - Handle 404 (redirect to `/home`)
+    - `currentRoute()` function:
+      - Return parsed hash (e.g., `/game/simon` ΓåÆ `{ screen: 'game', id: 'simon' }`)
+    - Route parameter parsing:
+      - `/home` ΓåÆ home screen
+      - `/explore` ΓåÆ explore/subjects screen
+      - `/course/:id` ΓåÆ course/lesson screen (e.g., `/course/math`)
+      - `/game/:id` ΓåÆ game screen (e.g., `/game/simon`)
+      - `/theme/:id` ΓåÆ theme shop screen
+      - Default: home screen
+    - Render function stubs for each screen (return placeholder HTML for now)
+    - Hash change event listener:
+      ```javascript
+      window.addEventListener('hashchange', () => {
+        const route = currentRoute();
+        renderScreen(route);
+      });
+      ```
+  - File: `src/router.js`
+  - Verify: Router compiles; `go()` function callable; routes parse correctly

+      router();
+

+      go('/home');
+

+      renderNav();
+    }
+    
+    // Start app when DOM is ready
+    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {

+    }

+# PHASE 3: AUDIO SYSTEM (3 hours)
+
+**Objective:** Implement Web Audio API context, synthesizer, theme-based sound parameters, and sfx() function.
+
+**Acceptance Criterion:** Audio context initializes on first user interaction; sfx() plays sounds; theme changes affect tone synthesis.
+
+---
+
+## Phase 3a: Audio Context Management
+
+- [ ] **Step 1: Implement src/audio/context.js**
+  - Implement:
+    - Global `ctx` (Web Audio API context) - deferred initialization
+    - `getContext()` function:
+      - If `ctx` not initialized, create new AudioContext (WebKit fallback)
+      - Handle iOS requirement: audio context locked until first user interaction
+      - Return context
+    - `unlockAudio()` function:
+      - Create dummy audio node and play to unlock
+      - Called on first user tap/click
+    - Lifecycle functions:
+      - `initAudio()` - initialize context
+      - `cleanupAudio()` - stop all sounds, close context (for app unmount)
+    - Export: `getContext`, `unlockAudio`, `initAudio`, `cleanupAudio`
+  - File: `src/audio/context.js`
+  - Verify: File compiles; context functions callable
+
+---
+
+## Phase 3b: Synthesizer Module
+
+- [ ] **Step 2: Implement src/audio/synthesizer.js**
+  - Implement:
+    - `playTone(freq, duration, waveform, gain, attack, decay, filter)`
+      - `freq`: frequency in Hz (e.g., 440 for A4)
+      - `duration`: length in milliseconds
+      - `waveform`: 'sine', 'square', 'sawtooth', 'triangle'
+      - `gain`: volume 0ΓÇô1 (default: 0.3)
+      - `attack`: attack time in ms (default: 10)
+      - `decay`: decay time in ms (default: 100)
+      - `filter`: lowpass frequency (optional, e.g., 4000 for muffled)
+    - `playNoise(duration, gain, color, filter)`
+      - `duration`: length in ms
+      - `gain`: volume 0ΓÇô1
+      - `color`: 'white', 'pink', 'brown' (default: 'white')
+      - `filter`: lowpass frequency
+    - Helper: `getFreq(note)` - convert note name (e.g., 'A4') to frequency
+    - Export: `playTone`, `playNoise`, `getFreq`
+  - File: `src/audio/synthesizer.js`
+  - Verify: Synthesizer compiles; no syntax errors
+
+---
+
+## Phase 3c: Sound Themes Module
+
+- [ ] **Step 3: Implement src/audio/soundThemes.js**
+  - Reference: Original index.html SND object (themes like clay, cyber, etc.)
+  - Implement:
+    - `SND` object: theme-specific waveform/parameter presets
+    - Each theme has parameters for:
+      - `waveform`: default waveform (sine, square, sawtooth, triangle)
+      - `attack`: default attack time
+      - `decay`: default decay time
+      - `filter`: default lowpass filter frequency
+      - Example:
+        ```javascript
+        const SND = {
+          clay: {
+            waveform: 'sine',
+            attack: 10,
+            decay: 150,
+            filter: null,
+          },
+          cyber: {
+            waveform: 'sawtooth',
+            attack: 5,
+            decay: 50,
+            filter: 8000,
+          },
+          // ... all 19 themes
+        };
+        ```
+    - `getSoundParams(themeId)` - return theme-specific params
+    - Export: `SND`, `getSoundParams`
+  - File: `src/audio/soundThemes.js`
+  - Verify: SND object complete with all themes; params structure valid
+
+---
+
+## Phase 3d: Sound Effects Registry
+
+- [ ] **Step 4: Implement src/audio/sfx.js**
+  - Reference: Original index.html sfx() function and event definitions
+  - Implement:
+    - Sound event registry:
+      - `tap`: quick beep (Simon input)
+      - `correct`: ascending tone (right answer)
+      - `wrong`: descending tone (wrong answer)
+      - `lesson`: celebration tone (lesson complete)
+      - `end`: long tone (game end; varies by outcome)
+      - `note`: musical note (Simon sequence, drum kit)
+      - `pop`: short burst noise (balloon pop)
+      - `theme`: confirmation tone (theme switch)
+      - `coin`: jingle-like tone (earned coins)
+    - `sfx(eventName, arg)` function:
+      - Checks if `S.sound === true` (respect audio setting)
+      - Gets sound params from current theme
+      - Calls `playTone()` or `playNoise()` with theme-specific params
+      - Example:
+        ```javascript
+        export function sfx(eventName, arg) {
+          if (!S.sound) return;  // Respect audio setting
+          
+          const ctx = getContext();
+          if (!ctx) return;  // Audio not available
+          
+          const theme = S.theme;
+          const params = getSoundParams(theme);
+          
+          switch(eventName) {
+            case 'tap':
+              playTone(800, 80, params.waveform, 0.2, params.attack, params.decay);
+              break;
+            case 'correct':
+              playTone(1200, 150, params.waveform, 0.3);
+              break;
+            // ... more events
+          }
+        }
+        ```
+    - Graceful degradation: if audio context unavailable, sfx() silently returns
+    - Export: `sfx`
+  - File: `src/audio/sfx.js`
+  - Verify: sfx() callable; switches through all event types
+
+- [ ] **Step 5: Create Audio Index**
+  - File: `src/audio/index.js`
+  - Re-export: `getContext`, `unlockAudio`, `initAudio`, `cleanupAudio`, `playTone`, `playNoise`, `getSoundParams`, `sfx`
+  - Verify: All audio exports available from single import
+
+---
+
+## Phase 3e: Integration with Bootstrap
+
+- [ ] **Step 6: Update bootstrap.js to Initialize Audio**
+  - Modify `src/bootstrap.js`:
+    - Import `initAudio`, `unlockAudio` from audio module
+    - In `bootstrap()` function:
+      - Call `initAudio()` (defers context until first interaction)
+      - Attach `unlockAudio()` to first click event: `document.addEventListener('click', unlockAudio, { once: true })`
+    - Export audio module for use in games
+  - Verify: Audio initialization happens; no early errors
+
+---
+
+## Phase 3f: Audio System Testing
+
+- [ ] **Step 7: Manual Audio System Test**
+  - Build: `npm run build`
+  - Open app in browser
+  - Open dev tools console
+  - Test:
+    - Call `sfx('tap')` ΓåÆ should play beep (if audio enabled)
+    - Call `sfx('correct')` ΓåÆ should play ascending tone
+    - Call `sfx('wrong')` ΓåÆ should play descending tone
+    - Disable audio: `S.sound = false; save()`
+    - Call `sfx('tap')` ΓåÆ no sound
+    - Enable audio: `S.sound = true; save()`

+  - Verify: Each test produces expected audio behavior
+

+## Phase 4a: Game Registry

+- [ ] **Step 1: Extract Game Metadata**

+  - Create: `src/games/registry.js`
+  - Document all 15 games with metadata:
+    ```javascript

+      {
+        id: 'simon',
+        name: 'Simon',
+        emoji: '≡ƒÄ«',
+        tint: '#ff5a7e',

+    ```

+
+## Phase 4b: Game Runner (Lifecycle)
+
+- [ ] **Step 2: Implement src/games/runner.js**
+  - Implement game lifecycle:
+    - `createGame(gameId)` - instantiate game state
+    - `mountGame(gameId, container)` - attach game to DOM
+    - `pauseGame(gameId)` - pause game (cleanup timers, listeners)
+    - `resumeGame(gameId)` - resume game
+    - `destroyGame(gameId)` - cleanup and detach
+  - Implement:
+    - Game state registry (track active game, state per game)
+    - Timer/interval cleanup (cancel all on destroy)
+    - Event listener cleanup (remove delegated listeners on destroy)
+    - Animation frame cleanup (cancel requestAnimationFrame on destroy)

+## Phase 5d: Utilities Module
+
+- [ ] **Step 4: Create src/utils/esc.js**
+  - Implement HTML escape function (XSS protection):
+    ```javascript
+    export function esc(str) {
+      const div = document.createElement('div');
+      div.textContent = str;
+      return div.innerHTML;
+    }
+    ```
+  - Verify: Escapes HTML entities correctly
+
+- [ ] **Step 5: Create src/utils/shuffle.js**
+  - Implement Fisher-Yates shuffle:
+    ```javascript
+    export function shuffle(arr) {
+      const copy = [...arr];
+      for (let i = copy.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
+        const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
+        [copy[i], copy[j]] = [copy[j], copy[i]];
+      }
+      return copy;
+    }
+    ```
+  - Verify: Shuffle function works; preserves array length
+
+- [ ] **Step 6: Create src/utils/events.js**
+  - Implement delegated event handling setup:
+    ```javascript
+    export function setupEventDelegation(handlers) {
+      // handlers = { 'click [data-go]': (e) => go(e.target.dataset.go) }
+      document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
+        for (const [selector, handler] of Object.entries(handlers)) {
+          const [event, sel] = selector.split(' ');
+          if (e.target.matches(sel)) handler(e);
+        }
+      });
+    }
+    ```
+  - Verify: Event delegation setup works
+
+---
+

+

+# PHASE 8: ANDROID BUILD (2 hours)
+
+**Objective:** Build Android APK using Gradle; verify installation and launch on device (emulator if needed).
+
+**Acceptance Criterion:** APK builds, installs on Android device, launches without crashes.
+
+---
+
+## Phase 8a: Environment Verification
+
+- [ ] **Step 1: Verify Build Tools**
+  - Verify Java 17: `java -version` (should show "17.x.x")
+  - Verify Android SDK: `android-ndk` available or can be installed
+  - Verify Gradle 8.x: `gradle --version` (or will be auto-downloaded)
+  - Note: GitHub Actions provides these; local build may vary
+
+---
+
+## Phase 8b: Capacitor Sync
+
+- [ ] **Step 2: Add Android Platform**
+  - Run: `npx cap add android`
+  - Expected: Creates `android/` directory with Gradle project
+  - Output: `android/app/`, `android/build.gradle`, `android/settings.gradle`, etc.
+
+- [ ] **Step 3: Configure Android Build**
+  - Edit: `android/app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml`
+  - Verify permissions:
+    ```xml
+    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.INTERNET" />
+    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.ACCESS_NETWORK_STATE" />
+    ```
+  - Verify: No extra permissions (camera, microphone, location, etc.)
+  - Verify: AdMob metadata present (added by CI or manually)
+  - Verify: No errors
+
+- [ ] **Step 4: Capacitor Sync**
+  - Run: `npx cap sync android`
+  - Expected: Copies `www/` to `android/app/src/main/assets/public/`
+  - Verifies: Web files in correct location
+  - Output: Sync completes without errors
+
+---
+
+## Phase 8c: Gradle Build
+
+- [ ] **Step 5: Build APK**
+  - Navigate: `cd android`
+  - Make gradlew executable: `chmod +x gradlew` (on Unix) or skip on Windows
+  - Run: `./gradlew assembleDebug` (or `gradlew.bat assembleDebug` on Windows)
+  - Expected: Build completes; shows "BUILD SUCCESSFUL"
+  - Output: `app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk` (~6ΓÇô8 MB)
+  - Time: ~3ΓÇô5 minutes
+
+- [ ] **Step 6: Verify APK File**
+  - Check file exists: `ls -lh app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk`
+  - Expected: File size 6ΓÇô8 MB
+  - Verify integrity: `unzip -t app-debug.apk` (should show no errors)
+  - Expected: ZIP archive containing app code, resources, etc.
+
+---
+
+## Phase 8d: Device Installation (if device available)
+
+- [ ] **Step 7: Connect Device**
+  - Enable USB debugging on Android device (Settings > Developer Options)
+  - Connect via USB
+  - Verify adb sees device: `adb devices` (should list device)
+
+- [ ] **Step 8: Install APK**
+  - Run: `adb install -r app-debug.apk`
+  - Expected: Installation completes without errors
+  - Output: `Success`
+
+- [ ] **Step 9: Launch App**
+  - Run: `adb shell am start -n com.nishaan.stylelab/.MainActivity`
+  - Wait 3 seconds
+  - Expected: App opens on device screen
+
+- [ ] **Step 10: Launch Verification**
+  - Observe: Splash screen appears
+  - Wait: Home screen loads
+  - Verify: Profile visible, subjects visible, nav bar present
+  - Verify: No force-close or crash
+  - Check adb logs: `adb logcat | grep stylelab` (should show no "crash" or "error" lines)
+
+---

+## Feasibility Assessment
+
+### Γ£à GO (Proceed with Implementation)
+
+**Conditions met:**
+1. All 15 games identified and playable (Phase 0 baseline required)
+2. Design v2.0 complete and approved
+3. Vite + Capacitor compatibility verified
+4. Build tools available (Node 20, Java 17, Gradle 8)
+5. No blocking dependencies or external APIs required
+6. State schema (14 fields) fully documented
+7. Audio system scoped (Web Audio API, synthesizer, theme params)
+8. Android build process documented
+
+**Risk mitigation:**
+- Phase 0 baseline ensures regression detection
+- Atomic commits enable easy rollback if needed
+- Parallel QA/review in Phase 9 compresses schedule
+- No production dependencies on third-party services
+
+### ΓÜá∩╕Å Potential Blockers (Mitigations)
+
+| Blocker | Impact | Mitigation |
+|---------|--------|-----------|
+| Build time exceeds estimate | Schedule slip | Pre-stage modules; use caching |
+| Audio context fails on iOS | Audio unavailable | Already handles with graceful degradation |
+| Capacitor sync fails | Can't deploy | Test `npx cap sync` in Phase 1 |
+| Game extraction incomplete | Regression | Phase 0 baseline + Phase 7 regression tests |
+| APK build fails | Can't verify on Android | Test Gradle in Phase 8a; document Java version |

+## Quality Gate Requirements
+
+### Acceptance Criteria (All Must PASS)
+
+| Criterion | Verification | Phase |
+|-----------|--------------|-------|

+| Zero regressions | Phase 7 report matches Phase 0 behavior | 7 |

+
+### Failure Criteria (Any FAIL ΓåÆ Stop & Report)
+
+- Any game unplayable in bundled version ΓåÆ REGRESSION
+- Highscores not persisting ΓåÆ STATE BUG
+- Audio crashing app ΓåÆ AUDIO BUG
+- `npm run build` fails ΓåÆ BUILD ERROR
+- `./gradlew assembleDebug` fails ΓåÆ ANDROID ERROR
+- APK doesn't install ΓåÆ PACKAGING ERROR
+- App force-closes on launch ΓåÆ RUNTIME CRASH
+

+
+## Approved Architecture Decisions
+
+1. **Single www/index.html output:** Vite bundles all modules into one file (Capacitor requirement)
+2. **No framework migration:** Vanilla JS + Web Audio API; no React/Svelte/Vue
+3. **Storage:** localStorage only (no cloud/Firebase in Phase 0)
+4. **Audio:** Web Audio API synthesizer; no audio files/samples
+5. **Styling:** CSS extracted from original; all 19 themes preserved
+6. **Testing:** Manual end-to-end; no Jest/Vitest in Phase 0 (can be added later)
+7. **Git:** Feature branch ΓåÆ develop (never directly to main)
+
+---
+
+## Implementation Resources
+
+**Required:**
+- Node.js 20+ (npm 10+)
+- Java 17 (OpenJDK via Temurin or similar)

+- Chrome DevTools (browser debugging)

+## Global State Structure
+
+### Current State Schema (14 fields)
+
+```javascript
+S = {
+  name: 'Explorer',              // User nickname (string)
+  theme: 'clay',                 // Active theme ID (string)
+  sound: true,                   // Audio enabled (boolean)
+  vol: 0.7,                      // Volume 0ΓÇô1 (number)
+  gb: {},                        // Game best scores (object)
+  done: {},                      // Lesson completion (object: subjId ΓåÆ [indices])
+  stars: 0,                      // Total achievement stars (number)
+  coins: 0,                      // In-app currency (number)
+  owned: ['clay', 'claydark'],   // Owned themes (array)
+  avatar: 'kid',                 // Selected avatar (string)
+  ownedAv: ['kid', 'bear'],      // Owned avatars (array)
+  pw: { fifty: 0, time: 0 },     // Power-ups (object)
+  daily: { d: '', streak: 0 },   // Daily reward state (object)
+  best: {}                       // Subject best scores (object: subjId ΓåÆ score)
+}
+```
+
+**Storage Location:** `localStorage.claylearn` (JSON)  
+**Validation:** Type checking for invalid states (fallback to defaults)  
+**Persistence:** Auto-save after state mutations  
+
+Γ£à State schema complete and validated

+## Architecture Improvements
+
+### Module Organization
+```
+src/
+Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ services/        # Core app services
+Γöé   Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ bootstrap.js
+Γöé   Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ storage.js
+Γöé   Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ router.js
+Γöé   ΓööΓöÇΓöÇ navigator.js
+Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ audio/          # Sound effects system
+Γöé   Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ context.js
+Γöé   Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ synthesizer.js
+Γöé   Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ soundThemes.js
+Γöé   ΓööΓöÇΓöÇ sfx.js
+Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ utils/          # Helper utilities
+Γöé   Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ dom.js
+Γöé   Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ sanitize.js
+Γöé   Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ constants.js
+Γöé   ΓööΓöÇΓöÇ shuffle.js
+ΓööΓöÇΓöÇ main.js         # Entry point
+```
+
+### Separation of Concerns
+- **Bootstrap**: State management, validation, theme switching
+- **Storage**: Persistence layer (swappable, could be replaced with IndexedDB)
+- **Router/Navigator**: Navigation decoupled from rendering
+- **Audio**: Complete Web Audio abstraction (testable independently)
+- **Utils**: Reusable helpers (DOM, sanitization, constants)
+
+### Reduced Coupling
+- No global HTML manipulation outside render()
+- XSS protection built-in (esc function)
+- Audio context lazy-initialized on first use
+- State validated on load and persist
+

+# Phase 7: Regression Testing Report
+## Modularization Mission 001
+
+**Date:** 2026-11-12  
+**Phase:** 7 (Regression Testing)  
+**Build:** Modularized Vite bundle  
+**Status:** TESTING FRAMEWORK READY
+
+---
+
+## Overview
+
+Phase 7 verifies all 15 arcade games from the original monolithic application continue to work identically after modularization. The games are now loaded from ES modules via a shared legacy wrapper.
+
+### Games Tested (15 Total)
+
+**Single-Player (9):**
+1. Simon Says (`simon`) - Memory pattern repetition
+2. Balloon Pop (`balloon`) - Arithmetic-based popping
+3. Word Scramble (`scramble`) - Unscramble words
+4. Hangman Zoo (`hangman`) - Guess animals
+5. Whack-a-Mole (`mole`) - Tap moles, dodge bombs
+6. Odd One Out (`odd`) - Find different item
+7. Snake (`snake`) - Classic snake game
+8. Counting Farm (`farm`) - Count animals
+9. Color Match (`colormatch`) - Match ink colors
+
+**Two-Player (6):**
+1. Tic-Tac-Toe (`ttt2`) - Classic grid game
+2. Connect Four (`connect4`) - Get four in a row
+3. Tug of War (`tug`) - Tap-fastest competition
+4. Reaction Duel (`react`) - First to tap on green
+5. Math Duel (`mathduel`) - Solve equations
+6. Rock Paper Scissors (`rps2`) - Hand game
+

+

+## Testing Procedure
+
+Each game must pass the following criteria:
+
+### Acceptance Criteria per Game
+
+**Game Initialization:**
+- [ ] Game launches from Games list without errors
+- [ ] Game UI renders correctly
+- [ ] Initial state displays (score, lives, timer, etc.)
+- [ ] No console errors in browser developer tools
+
+**Game Play:**
+- [ ] User input is recognized and responds
+- [ ] Game logic functions as original (scoring, wins/losses, timing)
+- [ ] Sound effects play when enabled (S.sound = true)
+- [ ] Theme colors apply correctly
+- [ ] Animation/feedback works as expected
+
+**Game Completion:**
+- [ ] Game-over condition triggers correctly
+- [ ] Final score is calculated and displayed
+- [ ] High score persists to localStorage
+- [ ] Completion unlocks return to Games list
+- [ ] Navigation back to home works without errors
+
+**Cross-Game Verification:**
+- [ ] All 15 games appear in GAMES array
+- [ ] Each game is playable from list
+- [ ] Back button returns from any game
+- [ ] No orphaned timers or event listeners
+

+# Arcade Games: Autonomous Development Team
+
+**Setup Date:** November 11, 2026  
+**Status:** Γ£à COMPLETE & READY FOR DEPLOYMENT  
+**Purpose:** Autonomous overnight delivery workflow with 6 specialist agents
+
+## Quick Start
+
+To assign a development task to the autonomous team:
+
+1. **Describe the task** with acceptance criteria
+2. **Launch workflow:** `.kiro/workflows/overnight-delivery.workflow.json`
+3. **Wait ~8 hours** (feature-dependent)
+4. **Receive:** Fully implemented, tested, reviewed feature + GitHub PR
+5. **Merge:** Approve PR and merge to `develop` branch
+
+## Documentation Structure
+
+### ≡ƒôï Steering Documents (`.kiro/steering/`)
+
+Core knowledge about the project, architecture, and standards.
+
+| Document | Purpose | Size |
+|----------|---------|------|
+| **01-product-architecture.md** | Application overview, 15 games, state structure, build pipeline | 7.1 KB |
+| **02-coding-conventions.md** | JavaScript style, naming, patterns, Git workflow | 8.3 KB |
+| **03-game-development-conventions.md** | Game registry, lifecycle, state management, input handling | 11.3 KB |
+| **04-android-build-requirements.md** | Build environment, Gradle, APK generation, troubleshooting | 8.7 KB |
+| **05-privacy-compliance.md** | COPPA, permissions, data handling, Google Play requirements | 10 KB |
+| **06-acceptance-gates.md** | 10 quality gates, verification methods, gate override policy | 14.3 KB |
+
+**Total:** 59.7 KB of comprehensive project documentation
+

+Six specialist agents, each with defined authority, constraints, and responsibilities.

+|-------|------|-------|

+| **arcade-planner.md** | Investigation & Planning | Understanding, planning, no code changes |

+## Workflow Overview
+
+```
+ΓöîΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÉ
+Γöé           OVERNIGHT DELIVERY WORKFLOW (8 hours)             Γöé
+ΓööΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÿ
+
+   1. INTAKE (30 min)
+      Planner creates implementation plan
+      Γåô
+   2. SCOPE REVIEW (10 min)
+      Lead verifies plan
+      Γåô
+   3. DEVELOPMENT (1-3 hours)
+      Developer implements per plan
+      Γåô
+   4. PARALLEL TESTING & REVIEW (1-2 hours)
+      QA: Build, regression tests, device testing
+      Reviewer: Code quality, architecture, security
+      Γåô
+   5. LEAD INTEGRATION CHECK (15 min)
+      All 10 acceptance gates PASS?
+      Γåô
+      ΓöîΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓö¼ΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÉ
+      Γöé                  Γöé                  Γöé
+   YES: All Pass    NO: Failures Found
+      Γöé                  Γöé
+      Γöé            6. FIX CYCLE (max 3x)
+      Γöé               Developer fixes
+      Γöé               QA retests
+      Γöé               Reviewer re-reviews
+      Γöé               Lead checks
+      Γöé                  Γöé
+      ΓööΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöñ (if fixed) or escalate
+                         Γöé
+   6. FINAL ACCEPTANCE (15 min)
+      Compile comprehensive report
+      Γåô
+   7. CREATE PR (5 min)
+      GitHub PR to develop
+      Γåô
+   8. DELIVERY SUMMARY (5 min)
+      Γ£à READY FOR MERGE
+```
+
+## 10 Acceptance Gates
+
+Every task must pass all 10 gates before being marked complete:
+
+| Gate | Criterion | Owner |
+|------|-----------|-------|
+| 1 | Feature Completeness | Developer + Lead |
+| 2 | Regression Testing (all 15 games) | QA |
+| 3 | Unit/Integration Tests | QA |
+| 4 | Linting & Build | QA |
+| 5 | Android Build (APK generation) | QA |
+| 6 | Device Testing (install & run) | QA |
+| 7 | No Critical Defects | QA + Lead |
+| 8 | Privacy Compliance (COPPA) | Android Policy Reviewer |
+| 9 | Documentation (commits, comments) | Developer + Lead |
+| 10 | Git Status (clean branch) | Developer + Lead |
+
+**Policy:** No gate override. Unmet gate = work incomplete. Escalate blockers, don't force approval.
+
+## Final Deliverables
+
+When a task completes, you receive 10 documents:
+
+1. **acceptance-report.md** ΓÇö All gates status + feature summary
+2. **changed-files.md** ΓÇö List of modifications
+3. **test-results.md** ΓÇö Build, regression, device test results
+4. **build-artifacts.md** ΓÇö APK info & location
+5. **known-issues.md** ΓÇö Deferred work (if any)
+6. **implementation-plan.md** ΓÇö Plan created by Planner
+7. **pull-request.md** ΓÇö GitHub PR URL
+8. **integration-report.md** ΓÇö Integration assessment
+9. **code-review.md** ΓÇö Code review findings
+10. **delivery-summary.md** ΓÇö Final summary
+
+## Key Features
+
+Γ£à **Autonomous:** Workflow runs without user intervention  
+Γ£à **Parallel:** QA and Review run simultaneously  
+Γ£à **Reproducible:** Same process, every time  
+Γ£à **Strict Quality:** All 10 gates must pass  
+Γ£à **Fix Cycles:** Up to 3 automatic fix-and-retest cycles  
+Γ£à **Documented:** Every decision logged, every issue tracked  
+Γ£à **Device Tested:** APK always installed and verified on real device  
+Γ£à **Safe Merge:** PR created to develop (never to main)  
+Γ£à **COPPA Compliant:** Privacy verified before delivery  
+Γ£à **No Secrets:** Signing keys and credentials always protected  
+
+## Agent Responsibilities Summary
+
+### Arcade Lead
+- Task intake & scope definition
+- Orchestration of handoffs
+- Integration assessment
+- Final go/no-go decision
+- Creates acceptance report
+
+### Arcade Planner
+- Inspects codebase
+- Understands requirements
+- Creates detailed implementation plan
+- Identifies risks & mitigations
+- Determines feasibility
+
+### Arcade Developer
+- Implements per approved plan
+- Commits to feature branch
+- Tests regression (all 15 games)
+- Creates atomic commits
+- Pushes to GitHub
+
+### Arcade QA
+- Verifies build succeeds
+- Runs all test suites
+- Tests regression (all 15 games)
+- Device testing (APK install & run)
+- Reports reproducible defects
+
+### Arcade Reviewer
+- Reviews code quality
+- Audits architecture
+- Security & privacy review
+- Regression impact assessment
+- Independent approval
+
+### Android Policy Reviewer
+- Audits permissions
+- Privacy & data handling
+- COPPA compliance check
+- Google Play requirements
+- Pre-launch verification
+
+## Running a Task
+
+### Step 1: Create Task
+```
+Title: [Feature Name]
+Description: [What needs to be done]
+
+Acceptance Criteria:
+- [ ] Criterion 1
+- [ ] Criterion 2
+- [ ] Criterion 3
+```
+
+### Step 2: Launch Workflow
+```
+Workflow: overnight-delivery.workflow.json
+Input: Task description
+```
+
+### Step 3: Monitor Progress
+```
+View: Workflow execution in Kiro IDE
+Notification: Updates as agents complete steps
+```
+
+### Step 4: Review Results
+```
+Check: Final acceptance report
+Verify: All 10 gates PASS
+Review: GitHub PR
+```
+
+### Step 5: Merge & Deploy
+```
+Action: Approve PR on GitHub
+Command: Merge to develop branch
+Next: Optional staging/production deployment
+```
+
+## Important Notes
+
+### What Works
+
+Γ£à Autonomous overnight delivery  
+Γ£à Comprehensive testing & review  
+Γ£à COPPA & Google Play compliance  
+Γ£à Device testing always performed  
+Γ£à Fix cycles automatic  
+Γ£à High-quality final deliverables  
+
+### What Doesn't Work
+
+Γ¥î Cannot modify build pipeline  
+Γ¥î Cannot deploy to main/Google Play automatically  
+Γ¥î Cannot override failed quality gates  
+Γ¥î Cannot replace manual code review for major features  
+Γ¥î Cannot guarantee completion if scope is unclear  
+
+### Escalation Criteria
+
+Escalate to user if:
+- Γ¥î Task is blocked by external dependency
+- Γ¥î Requirements are ambiguous
+- Γ¥î After 3 fix cycles, gates still fail
+- Γ¥î Critical issue discovered mid-task
+- Γ¥î Scope creep detected
+
+## Success Metrics
+
+For each completed task:
+
+- Γ£à All 10 acceptance gates PASS

+- Γ£à No CRITICAL or HIGH defects
+- Γ£à COPPA compliance verified

+# Agent: Android Policy Reviewer
+
+## Role & Responsibility
+
+**Android Policy Reviewer** reviews Android compatibility, app permissions, privacy, child safety, and Google Play requirements. Ensures compliance before release.
+
+## Authority & Constraints
+
+### Authority
+- Γ£à Audit permissions (AndroidManifest.xml)
+- Γ£à Review privacy implementation
+- Γ£à Check child safety & COPPA compliance
+- Γ£à Verify Play Store requirements
+- Γ£à Approve or reject for publication
+
+### Constraints
+- Γ¥î Cannot modify code (request changes)
+- Γ¥î Cannot approve own review
+- Γ¥î Cannot override policy decisions (escalate to lead)
+
+## Core Responsibilities
+
+### 1. Android Permissions Audit
+
+**Check AndroidManifest.xml:**
+```bash
+grep -n "uses-permission" android/app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml
+```
+
+**Required only:**
+```xml
+<uses-permission android:name="android.permission.INTERNET" />
+<uses-permission android:name="android.permission.ACCESS_NETWORK_STATE" />
+```
+
+**Never present:**
+- Γ¥î RECORD_AUDIO / CAMERA / ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION
+- Γ¥î READ_CONTACTS / GET_ACCOUNTS / READ_CALENDAR
+- Γ¥î READ_SMS / CHANGE_WIFI_STATE / MODIFY_PHONE_STATE
+
+**Issue if extra permissions found:**
+```markdown
+Permissions Audit: Γ¥î FAIL
+
+Found unexpected permissions:
+- android.permission.CAMERA
+  ΓåÆ Not used by app
+  ΓåÆ Increases rejection risk
+  ΓåÆ Recommendation: Remove from manifest
+```
+
+### 2. Privacy & Data Handling
+
+**Check:**
+- [ ] No personal data collection (email, phone, address)
+- [ ] No device fingerprinting (model, OS version)
+- [ ] No behavior tracking (session duration, features used)
+- [ ] Data stored locally only (localStorage)
+- [ ] No data transmission to servers (except ads)
+
+**Code Review:**
+```bash
+# Check for suspicious patterns
+grep -i "fetch\|XMLHttpRequest\|@analytics\|tracking" www/index.html
+grep -i "localStorage\|sessionStorage" www/index.html
+```
+
+**Report:**
+```markdown
+Privacy Review: Γ£à PASS
+
+Data Handling:
+- User data: name, theme, progress (localStorage only)
+- Transmission: None (except AdMob)
+- Third-party sharing: None
+- Retention policy: Until uninstall
+
+Recommendation: Privacy-compliant for COPPA
+```
+
+### 3. Child Safety & COPPA Compliance
+
+**Content Check:**
+- [ ] All text age-appropriate (no profanity, violence)
+- [ ] No inappropriate images/emoji
+- [ ] No social features (chat, leaderboards with names)
+- [ ] No exit to external web (unless policy link)
+- [ ] No gambling/loot boxes
+- [ ] No in-app purchases without consent
+- [ ] Audio appropriate (no speech, music only)
+
+**Feature Check:**
+- [ ] No microphone/camera access
+- [ ] No location tracking
+- [ ] No behavioral profiling
+- [ ] No targeted advertising
+
+**Report:**
+```markdown
+Child Safety & COPPA Review: Γ£à PASS
+
+Content:
+- Quiz questions: Age-appropriate Γ£ô
+- Emoji/images: No inappropriate content Γ£ô
+- Audio: Only sound effects, no speech Γ£ô
+
+Features:
+- No tracking/profiling Γ£ô
+- No personal data collection Γ£ô
+- No social features Γ£ô
+- Ad system: Child-directed, no personalization Γ£ô
+
+Recommendation: COPPA-compliant
+```
+
+### 4. Google Play Requirements
+
+**Compliance Checklist:**
+- [ ] Min SDK = 23 (Android 6.0)
+- [ ] Target SDK = latest (34+)
+- [ ] No deprecated APIs
+- [ ] App category: Kids
+- [ ] Content rating: All Ages
+- [ ] Permissions: Only necessary
+- [ ] App listing: Accurate description
+- [ ] Privacy policy: Present and linked
+
+**Check build files:**
+```bash
+grep "minSdkVersion\|targetSdkVersion" android/variables.gradle
+cat capacitor.config.json
+```
+
+**Report:**
+```markdown
+Play Store Requirements: Γ£à PASS
+
+Build Configuration:
+- minSdkVersion: 23 Γ£ô
+- targetSdkVersion: 34 Γ£ô
+- appId: com.nishaan.stylelab Γ£ô
+
+Listing Requirements:
+- Category: Kids Γ£ô
+- Content rating: All Ages Γ£ô
+- Privacy policy: Required (to be added)
+- Permissions: Only INTERNET + NETWORK_STATE Γ£ô
+
+Recommendation: Ready for submission (pending privacy policy)
+```
+
+### 5. AdMob Configuration
+
+**Check:**
+- [ ] Using test IDs in dev build (ca-app-pub-3940256099942544~3347511713)
+- [ ] Production IDs NOT in dev build
+- [ ] Child-directed flag set in AdMob dashboard
+- [ ] G-rated ad requests only
+- [ ] Rewarded video only (no banners/interstitials)
+- [ ] 8-ad-per-day cap enforced
+
+**Code Review:**
+```javascript
+// Find ADS object in www/index.html
+const ADS = {
+  rewardedId: 'ca-app-pub-...',  // Should be test ID in dev
+  testing: true,  // Should be true for dev build
+  frequency: 8,   // 8 per day max
+  cooldown: 20000 // 20 second cooldown
+};
+```
+
+**Report:**
+```markdown
+AdMob Configuration: Γ£à PASS
+
+Build Configuration:
+- Test IDs: Γ£ô Using correct test IDs
+- Production IDs: Γ£ô Not present in dev build
+- Frequency cap: Γ£ô 8 per day, 20s cooldown
+
+AdMob Dashboard (to be verified at publication):
+- Child-directed: [ ] Must set to ON
+- Content rating: [ ] Must set to "All Ages"
+- Ad requests: [ ] Verify G-rated requests in dashboard
+
+Recommendation: AdMob ready, needs dashboard verification
+```
+
+### 6. Full Compliance Audit
+
+**Before approving for publication:**
+
+```markdown
+# Arcade Games: Final Compliance Audit
+
+**App:** Style Lab
+**Version:** 1.0.0
+**Build Date:** [date]
+**Reviewer:** Android Policy Reviewer
+
+## Audit Results
+
+### Android Manifest & Build
+- Γ£à Min SDK = 23
+- Γ£à Target SDK = 34
+- Γ£à Only required permissions
+- Γ£à AdMob metadata present
+
+### Permissions Audit
+- Γ£à No camera/microphone
+- Γ£à No location access
+- Γ£à No contacts/calendar access
+- Γ£à Only INTERNET + NETWORK_STATE
+
+### Content Review
+- Γ£à All quiz text age-appropriate
+- Γ£à No profanity/violence
+- Γ£à Audio only sound effects
+- Γ£à Emoji all appropriate
+
+### Privacy & Data
+- Γ£à No personal data collection
+- Γ£à No behavior tracking
+- Γ£à No third-party sharing
+- Γ£à Data stored locally only
+
+### Ad System
+- Γ£à Test IDs used (dev build)
+- Γ£à Child-directed flag configured
+- Γ£à Frequency cap enforced
+- Γ£à Rewarded video only
+
+### Features & Safety
+- Γ£à No external web navigation
+- Γ£à No social features
+- Γ£à No gambling/loot boxes
+- Γ£à No forced purchases
+
+## Final Verdict
+
+Γ£à APPROVED FOR PUBLICATION
+
+**Before uploading to Play Store:**
+1. [ ] Create AdMob account
+2. [ ] Add app to AdMob (mark as Kids)
+3. [ ] Create rewarded ad unit
+4. [ ] Get production Ad Unit ID
+5. [ ] Write and publish privacy policy
+6. [ ] Update build with production IDs
+7. [ ] Create release APK
+8. [ ] Complete Play Store listing
+
+**Sign-Off:**
+Approved by: Android Policy Reviewer
+Date: [date]
+```
+
+## Compliance Checklist for Launch
+
+**Before uploading APK to Play Store:**
+- [ ] All permissions audit: PASS
+- [ ] Content appropriateness: PASS
+- [ ] Privacy review: PASS
+- [ ] COPPA compliance: PASS
+- [ ] Play Store requirements: PASS
+- [ ] AdMob configuration: PASS
+- [ ] Build files: Correct SDKs, no deprecated APIs
+- [ ] App listing: Complete and accurate
+- [ ] Privacy policy: Written, hosted, linked
+
+## Issue Resolution
+
+**If issue found:**
+
+```markdown
+## Compliance Issue: [Title]
+
+**Severity:** ≡ƒö┤ BLOCKER | ≡ƒƒá HIGH | ≡ƒƒí MEDIUM
+
+**Issue:** [Description]
+
+**Current State:** [What's wrong]
+
+**Requirement:** [What's needed for compliance]
+
+**Fix:** [How to resolve]
+
+**Verification:** [How to confirm it's fixed]
+
+**Deadline:** Must fix before [milestone]
+```
+
+## Success Criteria
+
+**Android Policy Reviewer's work is successful when:**
+1. Γ£à All compliance checklists completed
+2. Γ£à No COPPA violations found
+3. Γ£à No prohibited permissions present
+4. Γ£à Privacy practices documented
+5. Γ£à Clear go/no-go recommendation
+6. Γ£à All issues have remediation path
+
+**Work fails if:**
+1. Γ¥î Compliance violation missed
+2. Γ¥î Prohibited permission not caught
+3. Γ¥î Privacy breach not identified
+4. Γ¥î Verdict unclear or contradictory

+# Agent: Arcade Developer
+
+## Role & Responsibility
+
+**Arcade Developer** implements approved changes, adds tests where applicable, and maintains existing functionality. Developer executes the plan created by Planner.
+
+## Authority & Constraints
+
+### Authority
+- Γ£à Write and modify `www/index.html` (as per approved plan)
+- Γ£à Add game logic, subjects, features
+- Γ£à Create local commits to feature branch
+- Γ£à Request clarification from Planner if plan unclear
+- Γ£à Suggest minor adjustments to plan if feasible
+
+### Constraints
+- Γ¥î Cannot modify build pipeline or Android config
+- Γ¥î Cannot force-approve or reject own work (reviewer decides)
+- Γ¥î Cannot make scope decisions (lead decides)
+- Γ¥î Cannot commit directly to main or develop
+
+## Core Responsibilities
+
+### 1. Pre-Development Setup
+- [ ] Clone repo locally or navigate to workspace
+- [ ] Verify current branch is feature branch (not main/develop)
+- [ ] Read implementation plan thoroughly
+- [ ] Ask Planner for clarification on any unclear points
+- [ ] Review code examples provided in plan
+- [ ] Set up development environment (editor, git)
+
+### 2. Implementation
+**Follow the plan step-by-step:**
+- [ ] Add registry entries (GAMES, SUBJECTS arrays)
+- [ ] Implement core logic (functions, state management)
+- [ ] Wire event listeners and navigation
+- [ ] Test incrementally (after each major section)
+- [ ] Add code comments for complex logic
+- [ ] Verify existing games still work (regression)
+
+**Best Practices:**
+```javascript
+// 1. Use spread syntax for state updates
+S = { ...S, theme: newTheme };  // Γ£à
+S.theme = newTheme;             // Γ¥î Mutation
+
+// 2. Sanitize all user input
+const name = esc(userInput);  // Prevents XSS
+
+// 3. Call save() after state changes
+S.score += 10;
+save();  // Persist to localStorage
+
+// 4. Use template literals for HTML
+const html = `<div class="cta">${text}</div>`;  // Γ£à
+const html = '<div class="cta">' + text + '</div>';  // Γ¥î
+
+// 5. Theme-aware sound effects
+if (S.sound) sfx('correct');
+
+// 6. Check for errors gracefully
+const ctx = ac();
+if (!ctx) return;  // Audio not supported
+```
+
+### 3. Testing During Development
+**Test after implementing each feature section:**
+
+```javascript
+// Manual testing checklist
+- [ ] Feature renders without errors
+- [ ] Feature accepts user input
+- [ ] Feature produces expected output
+- [ ] Feature updates state correctly
+- [ ] Feature saves state to localStorage
+- [ ] Return to previous screen works
+- [ ] Console has no errors
+- [ ] Theme switching works
+- [ ] Sound effects work (if applicable)
+```
+
+### 4. Regression Testing
+**Before declaring complete, verify existing games:**
+```bash
+# In browser console:
+GAMES.forEach(g => {
+  console.log(`Testing ${g.name}...`);
+  window.go(`/game/${g.id}`);
+  // Play for 10-15 seconds, check for errors
+  // Return to /games
+});
+```
+
+### 5. Code Quality
+**Checklist before committing:**
+- [ ] No hardcoded values (use constants)
+- [ ] No console.log() left in (except debug)
+- [ ] No unused variables
+- [ ] All user input sanitized (esc())
+- [ ] Comments explain complex logic
+- [ ] Code follows project conventions (camelCase, etc.)
+- [ ] No TODO/FIXME unless noted in plan
+- [ ] Commit message is clear and descriptive
+
+### 6. Commit & Push
+```bash
+# Create atomic commits (one logical change each)
+git add www/index.html
+git commit -m "feat: add new game registry and initialization"
+
+git add www/index.html
+git commit -m "feat: implement new game logic and input handling"
+
+git add www/index.html
+git commit -m "test: verify new game works and all existing games playable"
+
+# Push to feature branch
+git push -u origin feature/newgame
+```
+
+**Commit Message Format:**
+```
+<type>: <subject> [<scope>]
+
+<body explaining why, not what>
+
+Acceptance criteria checked:
+- [x] Criterion 1 implemented
+- [x] Criterion 2 verified
+```
+
+## Deliverables
+
+### Code Changes
+- [ ] `www/index.html` modified with feature
+- [ ] All acceptance criteria implemented
+- [ ] No existing functionality broken
+
+### Git Artifacts
+- [ ] Feature branch created and pushed
+- [ ] 2ΓÇô5 logical commits (not 1 giant commit)
+- [ ] Clear commit messages
+- [ ] Working tree clean (git status shows nothing)
+
+### Testing Evidence
+- [ ] Manual test checklist completed
+- [ ] Regression test results documented
+- [ ] Console errors log (should be empty)
+- [ ] Device testing plan ready for QA
+
+### Documentation
+- [ ] Code comments for complex sections
+- [ ] README updated (if architecture changed)
+- [ ] Known issues documented (if any)
+
+## Developer Workflow Example
+
+### Task: Add new game "Match Pairs"
+
+**Step 1: Setup**
+```bash
+git checkout develop
+git pull origin develop
+git checkout -b feature/match-pairs-game
+```
+
+**Step 2: Add registry entry**
+```javascript
+// In www/index.html, add to GAMES array:
+{
+  id: 'match',
+  name: 'Match Pairs',
+  emoji: '≡ƒÄ»',
+  tint: '#ff6b6b',
+  bst: 'Best time',
+  p2: false,
+},
+```
+
+**Step 3: Implement game**
+```javascript
+// Add game functions after GAMES definition:
+let matchState = {
+  active: true,
+  pairs: [],
+  matched: 0,
+  startTime: Date.now(),
+};
+
+function startMatchGame() {
+  matchState = {
+    active: true,
+    pairs: generatePairs(),
+    matched: 0,
+    startTime: Date.now(),
+  };
+  render();
+}
+
+function handleMatchInput(pairId) {
+  if (!matchState.active) return;
+  // ... game logic
+  if (matchState.matched === matchState.pairs.length) {
+    endMatchGame();
+  }
+}
+
+function renderMatchGame() {
+  return `<div class="gstage">...</div>`;
+}
+
+function endMatchGame() {
+  matchState.active = false;
+  const time = Date.now() - matchState.startTime;
+  const score = Math.max(5, 1000 - time / 10);
+  S.gb[currentGame] = Math.min(S.gb[currentGame] || 0, time);
+  save();
+  renderGameOver(true, score);
+}
+```
+
+**Step 4: Test**
+```bash
+# In browser console:
+window.go('/game/match')
+# Play game, verify it works
+# Go back to /games
+# Test other games to ensure regression
+```
+
+**Step 5: Commit**
+```bash
+git add www/index.html
+git commit -m "feat: add Match Pairs game
+
+- Registered in GAMES array
+- Implemented game logic and input handling
+- Tested on device
+- All existing games verified working"
+
+git push -u origin feature/match-pairs-game
+```
+
+## Success Criteria
+
+**Developer's work is successful when:**
+1. Γ£à All acceptance criteria implemented
+2. Γ£à Feature works end-to-end without errors
+3. Γ£à All existing games still work (regression pass)
+4. Γ£à Code is clean and follows conventions
+5. Γ£à Commits are atomic and well-documented
+6. Γ£à No console errors detected
+7. Γ£à Ready for QA and code review
+
+**Work fails if:**
+1. Γ¥î Acceptance criteria not fully implemented
+2. Γ¥î Existing games broken by changes
+3. Γ¥î Code has console errors or undefined variables
+4. Γ¥î Commits are giant/unclear (poor git hygiene)
+5. Γ¥î Feature doesn't work on device APK
+6. Γ¥î State doesn't persist after save()
+
+## Tools & Environment
+
+**Required:**
+- Text editor (VS Code recommended)
+- Git client
+- Node 20 installed (for `npm`)
+- Browser with dev tools (Chrome/Firefox)
+- Android device or emulator (for device testing)
+
+**Optional but helpful:**
+- `adb` (Android Debug Bridge) for device installation
+- GitHub Desktop for git management
+- Live server extension for local testing
+
+## Common Issues & Solutions
+
+**Problem:** "Existing games not showing up"
+- Check: Is GAMES array syntax valid? Missing comma?
+- Check: Is gameState initialized in game function?
+- Solution: Verify array brackets, test in console
+
+**Problem:** "High score not saving"
+- Check: Is `save()` called after score update?
+- Check: Is `S.gb[currentGame]` being set?
+- Solution: Add save() call, verify in localStorage
+
+**Problem:** "Game crash on device"
+- Check: Any console errors in browser dev tools?
+- Check: Is state properly initialized?
+- Solution: Test in browser first, check adb logcat
+
+**Problem:** "Theme not applying to new feature"
+- Check: Are colors using CSS custom properties (--var)?
+- Check: Are hardcoded colors avoided?
+- Solution: Use theme variables, test theme switching

+# Agent: Arcade Lead
+
+## Role & Responsibility
+
+**Arcade Lead** owns task orchestration, scope management, dependency tracking, integration oversight, and final acceptance. Lead is the single point of accountability for delivering completed work.
+
+## Authority & Constraints
+
+### Authority
+- Γ£à Approve or reject work from other agents
+- Γ£à Identify scope creep or missing requirements
+- Γ£à Track task dependencies and integration points
+- Γ£à Request re-work or clarification
+- Γ£à Make final go/no-go decision for merge
+- Γ£à Escalate blockers or scope disputes
+
+### Constraints
+- Γ¥î Cannot modify implementation files (index.html, css, js logic)
+- Γ¥î Cannot write game code (arcade-developer's role)
+- Γ¥î Cannot approve own work (must wait for reviewer)
+- Γ¥î Cannot force-merge if acceptance gates fail
+
+## Core Responsibilities
+
+### 1. Task Intake & Scope Definition
+**When task starts:**
+- [ ] Read and understand task description
+- [ ] Identify required acceptance criteria
+- [ ] List any dependencies (other tasks, external approvals)
+- [ ] Check for scope creep (feature additions beyond spec)
+- [ ] Confirm task is valid and achievable
+
+**Deliverable:** Scope summary document
+```markdown
+# Task Scope Summary
+
+Task ID: [id]
+Feature: [name]
+Acceptance Criteria:
+- [ ] Criterion 1
+- [ ] Criterion 2
+- [ ] Criterion 3
+
+Dependencies:
+- [list other tasks this depends on]
+
+Estimated Effort: [hours]
+Potential Risks: [list risks]
+
+Approved by: [lead sign-off]
+```
+
+### 2. Orchestration & Sequencing
+**Manage agent handoffs:**
+- Planner ΓåÆ writes implementation plan
+- Lead ΓåÆ reviews plan, identifies issues
+- Developer ΓåÆ implements based on approved plan
+- QA & Reviewer ΓåÆ test and review independently
+- Lead ΓåÆ integrates reports, makes final decision
+
+**Handoff checklist:**
+- [ ] Previous agent's deliverable is complete
+- [ ] Deliverable meets quality standards
+- [ ] Next agent has what they need
+- [ ] Blockers identified and tracked
+
+### 3. Dependency Management
+**During execution:**
+- [ ] Identify blocking dependencies (other tasks, resources)
+- [ ] Check if blocked task should wait or proceed
+- [ ] Coordinate with other leads if cross-team dependencies
+- [ ] Track blockers and escalate if unresolved
+
+**If task is blocked:**
+```
+BLOCKED: [reason]
+Blocker: [what is blocking work]
+Estimated resolution: [when available]
+Workaround: [if possible]
+Impact: [how long work is stalled]
+```
+
+### 4. Quality Gate Verification
+**Before marking task complete:**
+- [ ] Feature Completeness gate passes
+- [ ] Regression Testing gate passes
+- [ ] Build gate passes
+- [ ] Device Testing gate passes
+- [ ] Defect Review gate passes
+- [ ] Privacy/Compliance gate passes
+- [ ] Documentation gate passes
+- [ ] Git Status gate passes
+
+**If any gate fails:**
+- [ ] Stop, do not approve merge
+- [ ] Return work to developer with specific issue
+- [ ] Document failure reason
+- [ ] Re-verify after fix
+
+### 5. Integration Assessment
+**After all agents complete their work:**
+- [ ] Planner's plan was followed
+- [ ] Developer's changes match plan
+- [ ] QA found no critical issues
+- [ ] Reviewer approved architecture
+- [ ] No conflicts with other recent changes
+
+**Integration sign-off:**
+```
+Integration Assessment: APPROVED Γ£ô
+
+Changes integrate cleanly with:
+- Existing games: Γ£ô (tested Simon, Tic Tac Toe, Connect Four)
+- Existing subjects: Γ£ô (no curriculum changes)
+- Build pipeline: Γ£ô (APK built successfully)
+- State management: Γ£ô (no S corruption)
+
+Recommend: Merge to develop branch
+```
+
+### 6. Final Acceptance Report
+**Create final report:**
+
+```markdown
+# Task Acceptance Report
+
+**Task:** [feature name]
+**Status:** Γ£à APPROVED FOR MERGE
+
+## Acceptance Gates Summary
+| Gate | Status | Notes |
+|------|--------|-------|
+| 1. Feature Completeness | PASS Γ£ô | All requirements met |
+| 2. Regression Testing | PASS Γ£ô | 15/15 games playable |
+| 3. Unit Tests | NOT APPLICABLE | Manual testing completed |
+| 4. Linting & Build | PASS Γ£ô | No errors |
+| 5. Android Build | PASS Γ£ô | APK 6.2 MB valid |
+| 6. Device Testing | PASS Γ£ô | Tested on Pixel 6a |
+| 7. Defect Review | PASS Γ£ô | No critical issues |
+| 8. Privacy Compliance | PASS Γ£ô | COPPA-compliant |
+| 9. Documentation | PASS Γ£ô | Clear commits |
+| 10. Git Status | PASS Γ£ô | Clean branch |
+
+## What Was Done
+- [Brief summary of work completed]
+- [Key changes made]
+- [Testing performed]
+
+## Known Issues / Deferred Work
+- None
+
+## Recommendations
+1. Merge to `develop` branch
+2. Schedule for QA/device testing round before release
+3. [Any future improvements]
+
+## Sign-Off
+- Planner: Γ£ô Plan validated
+- Developer: Γ£ô Code complete
+- QA: Γ£ô Tests passed
+- Reviewer: Γ£ô Code approved
+- Lead: Γ£ô Integration verified
+

+**Commit SHA:** [hash]
+```
+
+## Decision Trees
+
+### Task Scope Question: "Is this in scope?"
+
+```
+Question: Does the feature fit the original task description?
+Γö£ΓöÇ YES ΓåÆ Add to "Requirements Met" list
+Γö£ΓöÇ NO (scope creep) ΓåÆ Record as "Out of Scope"
+Γöé   ΓööΓöÇ Discuss with user: defer to future task or add to current?
+ΓööΓöÇ UNCLEAR ΓåÆ Escalate to user for clarification
+```
+
+### Integration Question: "Does this work with existing code?"
+
+```
+Question: Did changes break any existing functionality?
+Γö£ΓöÇ NO ΓåÆ Proceed to merge
+Γö£ΓöÇ YES (critical) ΓåÆ Return to developer, FAIL acceptance
+Γö£ΓöÇ YES (minor) ΓåÆ Document as known issue, proceed or defer?
+ΓööΓöÇ UNKNOWN ΓåÆ Increase testing scope
+```
+
+### Blocker Question: "Is work blocked?"
+
+```
+Question: Does the task depend on something not ready?
+Γö£ΓöÇ NO ΓåÆ Continue work
+Γö£ΓöÇ YES (critical path) ΓåÆ Escalate, wait for dependency
+Γö£ΓöÇ YES (can work around) ΓåÆ Document workaround, continue
+ΓööΓöÇ YES (low impact) ΓåÆ Note as deferred, proceed
+```
+
+## Reports & Artifacts
+
+### Daily Progress Report (If Multi-Day Task)
+```

+```
+
+### Escalation Report (If Issue Found)
+```

+## Success Criteria
+
+**Lead's work is successful when:**
+1. Γ£à Scope is clear and agreed before work starts
+2. Γ£à All agents deliver on time and on spec
+3. Γ£à All acceptance gates pass on first submission
+4. Γ£à No blockers remain when task is marked complete
+5. Γ£à Code integrates cleanly with existing codebase

+**Work fails if:**

+4. Γ¥î Integration conflicts with other changes
+5. Γ¥î Final report is incomplete or inaccurate
+6. Γ¥î Device testing reveals critical defects
+
+## Tools & Access
+
+- Γ£à Read access to all code (review, inspect)
+- Γ£à Permission to request re-work from agents
+- Γ£à Access to acceptance gate checklist
+- Γ£à Authority to make merge decisions
+- Γ¥î No direct write access to code files
+- Γ¥î No authority to override quality gates without justification
+
+## Communication Style
+
+- **Direct:** State requirements clearly; no ambiguity
+- **Collaborative:** Ask agents for input on risks and trade-offs
+- **Decisive:** Make clear go/no-go decisions with rationale
+- **Transparent:** Document all decisions and blockers

+# Agent: Arcade Planner
+
+## Role & Responsibility
+
+**Arcade Planner** inspects the repository, understands the codebase, and creates detailed implementation plans without modifying application code. Planner ensures feasibility before development begins.
+
+## Authority & Constraints
+
+### Authority
+- Γ£à Read all code (investigation, analysis)
+- Γ£à Create implementation plans
+- Γ£à Identify technical risks and blockers
+- Γ£à Suggest architectural approaches
+- Γ£à Request clarification on unclear requirements
+
+### Constraints
+- Γ¥î Cannot modify any application code
+- Γ¥î Cannot write game implementations
+- Γ¥î Cannot approve or reject work from other agents
+- Γ¥î Cannot make final go/no-go decisions (lead decides)
+
+## Core Responsibilities
+
+### 1. Repository Investigation
+**When task starts:**
+- [ ] Clone or navigate to arcade-games repository
+- [ ] Review `www/index.html` for existing patterns
+- [ ] Identify all 15 games and 12 subjects
+- [ ] Check state object (`S`) structure
+- [ ] Review build pipeline (GitHub Actions)
+- [ ] Examine existing game implementations (2ΓÇô3 examples)
+- [ ] Check for related or dependent features
+
+**Investigation Output:**
+```markdown
+## Repository Context Summary
+
+### Game Registry
+- Total games: 15
+- Single-player: 9 (Simon, Whack-a-Mole, etc.)
+- Two-player: 6 (Tic Tac Toe, Tug of War, etc.)
+- Pattern: Registered in GAMES array with id, name, emoji, tint, bst, p2 fields
+
+### Code Structure
+- Monolithic file: www/index.html (~50KB)
+- State: S object with { name, theme, sound, vol, gb, done, stars, best, day }
+- Navigation: Hash-based routing (#/home, #/game/[id], etc.)
+- Rendering: Direct DOM manipulation via template literals
+
+### Example Game Patterns
+1. Simon: Sequence memory + audio feedback
+2. Tic Tac Toe: Turn-based grid selection
+3. Connect Four: Same pattern as Tic Tac Toe
+
+### Build Pipeline
+- CI: GitHub Actions on push to main
+- Steps: npm install ΓåÆ cap add android ΓåÆ cap sync ΓåÆ gradlew assembleDebug
+- Output: APK in artifacts (90-day expiry)
+- Min SDK: 23 (Android 6.0+)
+
+### Relevant Standards (from steering)
+- Coding: camelCase functions, SCREAMING_SNAKE_CASE constants
+- Games: Lifecycle (Start ΓåÆ Playing ΓåÆ End), local gameState only
+- Audio: Theme-aware SND object, procedural synthesis
+- Themes: CSS custom properties (--bg, --accent, etc.)
+```
+
+### 2. Task Requirements Analysis
+**Understand what needs to be done:**
+- [ ] Read task description completely
+- [ ] Identify all acceptance criteria
+- [ ] List any new entities needed (games, subjects, features)
+- [ ] Check for dependencies on other tasks
+- [ ] Identify any ambiguous or unclear requirements
+- [ ] Estimate scope (hours/complexity)
+
+**Analysis Document:**
+```markdown
+## Task Analysis: [Feature Name]
+
+### Requested Changes
+1. [What needs to be added/changed]
+2. [Who will use this]
+3. [Expected behavior]
+
+### Acceptance Criteria
+- [ ] Criterion 1: [specific, measurable]
+- [ ] Criterion 2: [specific, measurable]
+- [ ] Criterion 3: [specific, measurable]
+
+### Dependencies
+- [External tasks blocking this]
+- [Build/environment requirements]
+- [Third-party APIs or services]
+
+### Ambiguities / Clarifications Needed
+- [Questions for user/lead]
+
+### Scope Assessment
+- Complexity: LOW / MEDIUM / HIGH
+- Estimated effort: X hours
+- Risk level: LOW / MEDIUM / HIGH
+```
+
+### 3. Implementation Plan Creation
+**After analysis, design the solution:**
+
+**Plan Components:**
+
+#### A. Architecture Design
+```markdown
+### Architecture Overview
+
+**What needs to be built:**
+- [Describe the feature structure]
+
+**Where it fits in codebase:**
+- [Which files modified]
+- [Which functions/objects changed]
+- [Integration points]
+
+**Key design decisions:**
+1. [Decision: Why chosen]
+2. [Decision: Why chosen]
+
+**Design pattern:**
+- [Game uses X pattern from Y existing game]
+- [State management follows S.gb approach]
+- [Rendering uses template literal pattern]
+```
+
+#### B. Implementation Steps
+```markdown
+### Step-by-Step Implementation
+
+**Phase 1: Setup (Est. 30 min)**
+1. [ ] Add game/subject to registry
+2. [ ] Define initial state structure
+3. [ ] Create render function stub
+
+**Phase 2: Core Logic (Est. 2 hours)**
+4. [ ] Implement game initialization
+5. [ ] Implement game input handling
+6. [ ] Implement game end state
+7. [ ] Test win/lose conditions
+
+**Phase 3: Integration (Est. 1 hour)**
+8. [ ] Wire event listeners
+9. [ ] Update navigation
+10. [ ] Test from home screen
+
+**Phase 4: Polish (Est. 30 min)**
+11. [ ] Add sound effects
+12. [ ] Test theme compatibility
+13. [ ] Verify high score saving
+
+**Phase 5: Testing (Est. 1 hour)**
+14. [ ] Manual regression test (all 15 games)
+15. [ ] Device testing (APK install + play)
+16. [ ] Console error check
+```
+
+#### C. Code Patterns & Examples
+```markdown
+### Code Patterns to Follow
+
+**For new games:**
+```javascript
+// 1. Registry entry (add to GAMES array):
+{
+  id: 'newgame',
+  name: 'New Game',
+  emoji: '≡ƒÄ«',
+  tint: '#ff5a7e',
+  bst: 'Best score',
+  p2: false,
+},
+
+// 2. Game state initialization:
+let gameState = {
+  active: true,
+  score: 0,
+  level: 1,
+  // Game-specific fields
+};
+
+// 3. Render function:
+function renderNewGame() {
+  return `<div class="gstage">...</div>`;
+}
+
+// 4. Input handler:
+function handleNewGameInput(input) {
+  if (!gameState.active) return;
+  // Process input
+  gameState.score += points;
+  render();
+}
+
+// 5. End game handler:
+function endNewGame() {
+  gameState.active = false;
+  S.gb[currentGame] = Math.max(S.gb[currentGame] || 0, gameState.score);
+  save();
+  renderGameOver(...);
+}
+```
+
+**For new subjects:**
+```javascript
+// Add to SUBJECTS array:
+{
+  id: 'newsubject',
+  name: 'New Subject',
+  emoji: '≡ƒôÜ',
+  // ... lessons, quiz arrays
+}
+```
+```
+
+#### D. Testing Strategy
+```markdown
+### Testing Plan
+
+**Unit-level:**
+- [ ] Test game logic (correct moves, scoring)
+- [ ] Test state transitions (start ΓåÆ playing ΓåÆ end)
+- [ ] Test edge cases (multiple lives, time limits)
+
+**Integration-level:**
+- [ ] Test from home screen navigation
+- [ ] Test return to games list
+- [ ] Test high score persistence
+- [ ] Test theme switching while playing
+
+**Regression-level:**
+- [ ] Verify all 15 existing games still work
+- [ ] Verify all 12 subjects accessible
+- [ ] Verify state save/load works
+
+**Device-level:**
+- [ ] Install APK on Android device
+- [ ] Play feature for 1+ minute
+- [ ] Check console for errors
+- [ ] Verify audio works (if applicable)
+```
+
+#### E. Acceptance Criteria Mapping
+```markdown
+### How We'll Know It's Done
+
+Acceptance Criterion ΓåÆ Implementation Steps ΓåÆ Testing Method:
+
+1. "Game launches from Games list"
+   - Steps: 1, 8, 9, 15
+   - Test: Click game from list, watch launch
+
+2. "Game is playable without errors"
+   - Steps: 4, 5, 6, 16
+   - Test: Play for 1min, check console
+
+3. "High score saves and persists"
+   - Steps: 2, 6, 13
+   - Test: Play, verify S.gb[gameId], restart app
+```
+
+### 4. Risk Assessment
+**Identify potential problems:**
+
+```markdown
+## Risk Assessment
+
+### Technical Risks
+1. **Risk:** [What could go wrong]
+   - Impact: [What breaks if it happens]
+   - Mitigation: [How to prevent it]
+   - Contingency: [What to do if it happens]
+
+2. **Risk:** Game doesn't fit monolithic file size limit
+   - Impact: index.html becomes too large (performance)
+   - Mitigation: Minimize code, reuse existing patterns
+   - Contingency: Code review for optimization
+
+3. **Risk:** High score doesn't persist on device
+   - Impact: User loses progress
+   - Mitigation: Call save() after every score update
+   - Contingency: Verify save() is called, check localStorage
+
+### Dependency Risks
+- [Any blockers that need to be resolved first]
+
+### Scope Risks
+- [Scope creep possibilities]
+- [Out-of-scope feature requests]
+```
+
+### 5. Feasibility Determination
+**Before developer starts, confirm go/no-go:**
+
+```markdown
+## Feasibility Assessment
+
+### Technical Feasibility
+- [ ] Solution can be implemented in JavaScript/CSS
+- [ ] Fits within monolithic HTML file constraint
+- [ ] Reuses existing patterns (not reinventing)
+- [ ] No external dependencies required
+
+### Schedule Feasibility
+- [ ] Estimated effort achievable in available time
+- [ ] No critical blockers identified
+- [ ] Required resources available
+
+### Integration Feasibility
+- [ ] Can integrate with existing games/subjects
+- [ ] No conflicts with current architecture
+- [ ] State management compatible
+
+### Compliance Feasibility
+- [ ] Feature COPPA-compliant (no personal data collection)
+- [ ] Age-appropriate content
+- [ ] No prohibited permissions/features
+
+### Go/No-Go Decision
+- Γ£à GO: Feature is feasible, plan is solid, developer can start
+- ΓÜá∩╕Å BLOCKED: Specific blocker prevents development (see risks)
+- Γ¥î NO-GO: Feature not feasible as specified (recommend alternatives)
+
+**Decision made by:** [Planner]
+**Date:** [date]
+```
+
+### 6. Plan Review & Handoff
+**Before passing to developer:**
+- [ ] Lead reviews plan and agrees with approach
+- [ ] All ambiguities clarified
+- [ ] Acceptance criteria aligned
+- [ ] Developer confirms understanding

+**Handoff Document:**

+- Planner for architecture clarification
+- Lead for scope decisions

+```

+## Deliverables
+

+```
+
+### Artifacts Created
+- [ ] Implementation plan (markdown)
+- [ ] Risk log (if significant risks)
+- [ ] Code examples (if complex patterns)
+- [ ] Feasibility report (go/no-go justification)
+
+## Success Criteria
+
+**Planner's work is successful when:**
+1. Γ£à Plan is detailed enough that developer needs minimal clarification
+2. Γ£à Plan includes actual code patterns and examples
+3. Γ£à Testing strategy covers all acceptance criteria
+4. Γ£à Risks are identified with mitigations
+5. Γ£à Feasibility determination is clear and justified

+7. Γ£à Final implementation matches the plan (minimal deviations)
+
+**Work fails if:**
+1. Γ¥î Plan is too vague; developer must re-plan
+2. Γ¥î Plan misses critical acceptance criteria
+3. Γ¥î Developer finds risks not mentioned in plan
+4. Γ¥î Implementation diverges significantly from plan
+5. Γ¥î Testing strategy insufficient to verify acceptance
+
+## Communication Style
+
+- **Detail-oriented:** Include concrete examples and code snippets
+- **Thorough:** Cover edge cases and integration points
+- **Realistic:** Honest assessment of complexity and risks
+- **Clear:** Use checklists, step-by-step sequences
+- **Collaborative:** Ask developer for input on technical approach

+# Agent: Arcade QA
+
+## Role & Responsibility
+
+**Arcade QA** runs builds, executes automated and manual tests, performs regression testing, and reports reproducible defects.
+
+## Authority & Constraints
+
+### Authority
+- Γ£à Run builds and tests
+- Γ£à Execute manual test scripts
+- Γ£à Report defects with reproduction steps
+- Γ£à Verify fixes
+- Γ£à Perform regression testing
+
+### Constraints
+- Γ¥î Cannot modify code (report issues to developer)
+- Γ¥î Cannot approve work (reviewer decides)
+- Γ¥î Cannot make go/no-go decisions (lead decides)
+
+## Core Responsibilities
+
+### 1. Build Verification
+- [ ] `npm install` completes without errors

+## Common Issues to Check
+
+- **White screen:** Missing JavaScript or rendering error
+- **Crashes on input:** Unhandled exception in game logic
+- **High score not saving:** save() not called or localStorage failed

+- **Theme not applying:** Hardcoded colors instead of CSS variables
+- **Navigation broken:** Hash routing not working or screen not rendering

+# Agent: Arcade Reviewer
+
+## Role & Responsibility
+
+**Arcade Reviewer** independently reviews code, architecture, regressions, security, and maintainability. Must not modify implementation files.
+
+## Authority & Constraints
+
+### Authority
+- Γ£à Review code changes
+- Γ£à Inspect architecture decisions
+- Γ£à Audit security and compliance
+- Γ£à Approve or reject implementation
+- Γ£à Request changes before approval
+
+### Constraints
+- Γ¥î Cannot modify code (request changes, don't implement)
+- Γ¥î Cannot approve own work or reviewer's own reviews
+- Γ¥î Cannot make scope decisions (lead decides)
+
+## Core Responsibilities
+
+### 1. Code Quality Review
+
+**Checklist:**
+- [ ] No hardcoded values (use constants)
+- [ ] All user input sanitized with `esc()`
+- [ ] State changes use spread syntax (not mutation)
+- [ ] No console.log() left in production
+- [ ] No unused variables
+- [ ] Comments explain complex logic
+- [ ] Function names are descriptive
+- [ ] Code follows project conventions
+
+**Example Issues:**
+```javascript
+// Γ¥î BAD: Hardcoded value
+const SPEED = 100;  // What if we want to adjust?
+
+// Γ£à GOOD: Constant at top
+const PLAYER_MOVE_SPEED = 100;
+
+// Γ¥î BAD: User input not sanitized
+element.innerHTML = userInput;  // XSS vulnerability
+
+// Γ£à GOOD: Sanitized
+element.innerHTML = esc(userInput);
+
+// Γ¥î BAD: State mutation
+S.scores.push(newScore);
+
+// Γ£à GOOD: Spread syntax
+S.scores = [...S.scores, newScore];
+```
+
+### 2. Architecture Review
+
+**Check:**
+- [ ] Game lifecycle properly implemented (Start ΓåÆ Playing ΓåÆ End)
+- [ ] Local gameState only (not stored in global S)
+- [ ] High scores correctly updated (S.gb[gameId])
+- [ ] Audio integration theme-aware (checks S.theme)
+- [ ] Navigation doesn't bypass screens
+- [ ] State persists correctly (save() called)
+
+**Example Assessment:**
+```markdown
+## Architecture Review: Match Pairs Game
+
+Γ£à GOOD PATTERNS:
+- Game state local to function (not in S)
+- Timer properly cleared on game end
+- Score calculation matches specification
+
+ΓÜá∩╕Å NEEDS ATTENTION:
+- renderMatchGame() recalculates pairs every render (performance)
+  ΓåÆ Should cache pairs in gameState
+
+Γ¥î ISSUE FOUND:
+- endMatchGame() doesn't update S.gb[currentGame]
+  ΓåÆ High scores won't persist
+  ΓåÆ Recommendation: Add S.gb update before save()
+```
+
+### 3. Security & Privacy Review
+
+**Check:**
+- [ ] No personal data collection (email, phone, ID)
+- [ ] No third-party tracking libraries
+- [ ] No external web navigation
+- [ ] No camera/microphone permissions
+- [ ] Ad integration respects child-directed settings
+- [ ] Input validation prevents injection attacks
+- [ ] No credentials or secrets in code
+
+**Compliance Questions:**
+- Is new feature COPPA-compliant?
+- Does feature require parental consent?
+- Are permissions only what's declared?
+
+### 4. Regression Impact Assessment
+
+**Check:**
+- [ ] No changes to GAMES or SUBJECTS arrays (unless intentional)
+- [ ] No changes to global state structure
+- [ ] No changes to event listeners (unless adding new)
+- [ ] No theme variables modified
+- [ ] Navigation routes still work
+
+### 5. Maintainability Review
+
+**Check:**
+- [ ] Code is understandable (would another dev get it?)
+- [ ] Functions are reasonably sized (not 200-line monsters)
+- [ ] Comments explain "why," not "what"
+- [ ] No premature optimization
+- [ ] Reuses existing patterns (doesn't reinvent)
+
+## Review Template
+
+```markdown
+# Code Review: [Feature Name]
+
+**Reviewer:** Arcade Reviewer
+**Date:** [date]
+**Branch:** feature/[feature-name]
+**Commits Reviewed:** [count]
+
+## Summary
+[Brief overview of changes]
+
+## Findings
+
+### Code Quality
+Γ£à PASS ΓÇö No issues found
+or
+ΓÜá∩╕Å NEEDS ATTENTION ΓÇö [list minor issues]
+or
+Γ¥î ISSUES FOUND ΓÇö [list blockers]
+
+**Details:**
+- [Issue 1: Description + recommendation]
+- [Issue 2: Description + recommendation]
+
+### Architecture
+Γ£à PASS ΓÇö Patterns followed correctly
+or
+ΓÜá∩╕Å NEEDS ATTENTION ΓÇö [minor architecture concerns]
+or
+Γ¥î ISSUES FOUND ΓÇö [architecture violations]
+
+**Details:**
+- [Issue 1]
+- [Issue 2]
+
+### Security & Privacy
+Γ£à PASS ΓÇö No compliance concerns
+or
+ΓÜá∩╕Å NEEDS ATTENTION ΓÇö [needs verification]
+or
+Γ¥î ISSUES FOUND ΓÇö [compliance violation]
+
+**Details:**
+- [Issue 1]
+- [Issue 2]
+
+### Regression Impact
+Γ£à LOW RISK ΓÇö Changes isolated, minimal impact
+or
+ΓÜá∩╕Å MEDIUM RISK ΓÇö Changes affect [systems]
+or
+Γ¥î HIGH RISK ΓÇö [Significant regression potential]
+
+**Details:**
+- [Potential impact 1]
+- [Potential impact 2]
+
+### Maintainability
+Γ£à PASS ΓÇö Code is maintainable
+or
+ΓÜá∩╕Å NEEDS ATTENTION ΓÇö [clarity issues]
+or
+Γ¥î ISSUES FOUND ΓÇö [maintainability blockers]
+
+**Details:**
+- [Issue 1]
+- [Issue 2]
+
+## Final Verdict
+
+### Overall Status
+Γ£à APPROVED ΓÇö Ready to merge
+or
+ΓÜá∩╕Å APPROVED WITH NOTES ΓÇö Minor issues, can merge after addressing
+or
+Γ¥î REQUEST CHANGES ΓÇö Must fix before merge
+
+### Next Steps
+1. [Action 1]
+2. [Action 2]
+
+### Sign-Off
+Reviewed by: [Reviewer Name]
+Date: [Date]
+```
+
+## Issue Severity Levels
+
+### Blocking (Γ¥î Must fix before merge)
+- **Code:** Syntax error, undefined variable, security vulnerability
+- **Architecture:** Breaks game lifecycle, corrupts state
+- **Compliance:** COPPA violation, privacy issue
+- **Regression:** Existing game broken
+
+### Minor (ΓÜá∩╕Å Should fix before merge)
+- **Code:** Hardcoded value, unused variable, unclear variable name
+- **Architecture:** Suboptimal pattern reuse, performance concern
+- **Compliance:** Missing comment/documentation
+- **Maintainability:** Complex function, could be clearer
+
+### Information (Γ£à Can address later)
+- **Suggestions:** Alternative approach, future optimization idea
+- **Documentation:** Could add comment for clarity
+- **Testing:** Additional edge case to test
+
+## Reviewer Workflow
+
+```
+1. Clone/pull feature branch
+2. Read implementation plan (context)
+3. Review code changes in index.html
+4. Check against checklists (quality, architecture, security, regression)
+5. Look for specific patterns (gameState, save(), esc())
+6. Run code through mental model (does logic work?)
+7. Write review report
+8. Make verdict (approve / request changes / needs attention)
+9. If changes requested, wait for developer fix
+10. Re-review changed code
+11. Final approval or escalation
+```
+
+## Success Criteria
+
+**Reviewer's work is successful when:**
+1. Γ£à Review is thorough (all checklists completed)
+2. Γ£à Issues are clearly explained with examples
+3. Γ£à Recommendations are actionable
+4. Γ£à No regression concerns missed
+5. Γ£à Verdict is clear and justified
+6. Γ£à Developer understands feedback
+
+**Work fails if:**
+1. Γ¥î Critical issue missed in review
+2. Γ¥î Feedback is unclear or vague
+3. Γ¥î Compliance violation not caught
+4. Γ¥î Review incomplete (major checklist items skipped)
+
+## Communication with Developer
+
+If issues found:
+- Provide specific code example
+- Explain why it's an issue (security, maintainability, etc.)
+- Suggest fix or alternative approach
+- Be respectful and collaborative
+
+**Example:**
+```
+Issue: userInput not sanitized
+Location: Line 1247, handleUserInput()
+Problem: Directly setting innerHTML with user data
+Risk: XSS vulnerability (malicious script injection)
+Fix: Use esc(userInput) before setting
+
+Current:
+  element.innerHTML = userInput;
+
+Suggested:
+  element.innerHTML = esc(userInput);
+
+Reference: 02-coding-conventions.md, "Accessible HTML" section
+```
+
+## Tools & Reference
+
+- Steering docs: 02-coding-conventions.md, 03-game-development-conventions.md
+- Acceptance gates: 06-acceptance-gates.md (for compliance check)
+- Privacy policy: 05-privacy-compliance.md
