+# Arcade Games: Product Architecture & Context
+
+## Application Overview
+
+**Name:** Style Lab (Nishaan Tech Arcade Games)  
+**Type:** Capacitor-based kids learning app (Android APK)  
+**Target:** Kids 4-8, with age-gated onboarding  
+**Tech Stack:**
+- Single-page HTML5/CSS3/JavaScript application (`www/index.html` - monolithic ~50KB)
+- Capacitor 6.2.0 for Android bridging
+- Web Audio API for sound effects
+- localStorage for persistent state
+
+## Architecture
+
+### Single-File Monolithic Design
+- **Entry:** `www/index.html` (complete app in one file)
+- **State:** Single state object `S` persisted to localStorage as JSON
+- **Navigation:** Hash-based routing (#/home, #/game/simon, etc.)
+- **Rendering:** Direct DOM manipulation via vanilla JS
+
+### Subject Curriculum (12 Subjects)
+1. **Math** ΓÇö Counting, adding, times tables
+2. **Science** ΓÇö Dinosaurs, facts-based learning
+3. **Reading** ΓÇö Letters, rhymes, story structure
+4. **Art** ΓÇö Primary colors, mixing, warm/cool
+5. **Music** ΓÇö Beat, notes, instruments
+6. **Puzzle** ΓÇö Patterns, logic, shapes
+7. **Animals** ΓÇö Pets vs wild, habitats, types
+8. **Space** ΓÇö Sun, planets, moon
+9. **World** ΓÇö Continents, landmarks, India facts
+10. **Hindi** ΓÇö Letters (αñ╡αñ░αÑìαñú), vowels (αñ╕αÑìαñ╡αñ░), numbers
+11. **Health** ΓÇö Senses, nutrition, hygiene
+12. **Money** ΓÇö Coins, counting, save vs spend
+
+### Game Registry (15 Games)
+**Single-Player (9):**
+1. **Simon** ΓÇö Memory pattern game (4-pad tones)
+2. **Whack-a-Mole** ΓÇö Reflexes, timing
+3. **Typing/Spell** ΓÇö Letter matching from keyboards
+4. **Word Slots** ΓÇö Hangman-style letter slots
+5. **Quiz Flashcard** ΓÇö Subject quizzes with timed rounds
+6. **Connect Four** ΓÇö Turn-based grid game
+7. **Balloon Pop** ΓÇö Rapid-fire tapping
+8. **Drum Kit** ΓÇö Sound experimentation
+9. **Match/Memory** ΓÇö Card flip pairs
+
+**Two-Player (6):**
+1. **Tic Tac Toe (2P)** ΓÇö Turn-based X/O grid
+2. **Tug of War** ΓÇö Push/pull meter race
+3. **Rock Paper Scissors** ΓÇö Hand vs AI/player
+4. **Turn-Based Quiz Duel** ΓÇö Competing on same subject
+5. **Rhythm Simon (2P)** ΓÇö Copy pattern, pass device
+6. **Number Race** ΓÇö Count/math race (2 controllers)
+
+### Core Game Loop Architecture
+- **Lifecycle:** Initialize ΓåÆ Playing ΓåÆ End (win/lose/quit)
+- **Scoring:** XP earned on completion (5ΓÇô30 points per game)
+- **Highscores:** Stored in `S.gb[gameId]`
+- **Audio:** Theme-aware sound effects (18 waveform presets)
+- **State Persistence:** Saved after each session end
+
+### Theme System (19 Themes)
+CSS custom properties define complete visual identity:
+- Color palette (--bg, --card, --accent, --green, etc.)
+- Shadow sets (--sh, --shs, --sin, --sht, --shbtn)
+- Border radius, blur, typography
+- Presets: Claymorphism, Glass, Neumorphism, Cyber, Pixel, Cartoon, etc.
+
+### Economy System
+**Earnings:**
+- Lesson completion: +15 coins
+- Quiz correct answer: +5 coins
+- Perfect quiz: +10 bonus
+- Games: +5 to +30 depending on score
+- Daily reward: +10ΓÇô30 coins
+
+**Spending:**
+- Theme unlock: 150ΓÇô500 coins (16 paid, 3 free)
+- Ad reward: +50 coins (max 8/day, 20s cooldown)
+
+### Profile & Persistence
+**State Object `S`:**
+```javascript
+{
+  name: "Explorer",           // Player name
+  theme: "clay",              // Active theme
+  sound: true,                // Audio toggle
+  vol: 0.7,                   // Volume 0ΓÇô1
+  gb: { [gameId]: score },    // Game best scores
+  done: { [subjId]: [i] },    // Lesson completion flags (subject ΓåÆ lesson indices)
+  stars: 0,                   // Total achievement stars
+  best: { [subjId]: score },  // Quiz best score per subject
+  day: { d: "", n: 0 }        // Today's lesson count
+}
+```
+
+**Persistence:** `localStorage.claylearn` (auto-save after state change)
+
+## Build & Deployment
+
+### Android Build Pipeline
+1. GitHub Actions on push to `main`
+2. Node 20 + Java 17 + Gradle environment
+3. Steps:
+   - `npm install`
+   - `npx cap add android` (creates native wrapper)
+   - Configure AdMob metadata & min SDK (23)
+   - `npx cap sync android` (copy web files ΓåÆ Android)
+   - `./gradlew assembleDebug` (Gradle build)
+4. Output: `android/app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk`
+5. Artifact: `.agents/tasks/[task-name]/app-debug.apk`
+
+### Web Build
+- No webpack/bundler; app is served as-is
+- Capacitor wraps the www directory in WebView
+
+### Local Development
+- Edit `www/index.html`
+- Push to GitHub, GitHub Actions builds APK
+- Download artifact and install: `adb install app-debug.apk`
+
+## Testing & QA
+
+### Automated Tests
+- **Currently:** None (no test framework configured)
+- **Scope:** Unit tests for game logic, quiz scoring, state transitions
+- **When Added:** Jest or Vitest recommended
+
+### Manual Testing Checklist
+- [ ] App launches, home screen loads
+- [ ] Theme switcher works, persistence verified
+- [ ] All 12 subjects accessible
+- [ ] Lesson flow: unlock ΓåÆ progress ΓåÆ quiz
+- [ ] Games launch and score correctly
+- [ ] Audio effects play (when enabled)
+- [ ] State persists after app restart
+- [ ] Two-player games swap device correctly
+- [ ] High scores saved and displayed
+- [ ] Economy (coins, spending) works end-to-end
+- [ ] Capacitor Android sync completes
+- [ ] APK installs and runs on Android device
+
+## Privacy & Compliance
+
+### Google Play Families Policy
+- **Target Age:** Kids 4ΓÇô8
+- **Content Rating:** All Ages
+- **Restrictions:**
+  - No third-party ads or tracking (TestAdMob IDs only in debug)
+  - No personal data collection
+  - No social features or in-app chat
+  - No exiting the app (no web links)
+- **Data Handling:** localStorage only, no backend
+- **Parental Consent:** Planned (age-gated at onboarding)
+
+### Permissions
+- **Current:** AdMob (test), sound
+- **Never allowed:** Location, camera, microphone, contacts, SMS
+- **Future:** Possibly offline persistence
+
+### Kids Safety Considerations
+- Clean, age-appropriate content only
+- No advertisements (except placeholder test ads)
+- No user-generated content
+- No social interaction
+- Easily reset profile button
+- No hidden in-app purchases
+
+## Development Constraints
+
+### Read-Only Rules (During Setup)
+- Do not create new games during setup phase
+- Do not rewrite existing games
+- Do not change application functionality
+- Do not modify build pipeline
+- Do not merge to main
+- Do not publish to Play Store
+- Do not expose signing keys or secrets
+
+### When Development Work Begins
+- All changes must go to feature branches
+- Feature branches merge to `develop` (not `main`)
+- `main` branch reserved for Play Store releases
+- Existing games must continue to work (regression tests)
+- New features must pass device testing
+- All changes require code review
+
+## Known Limitations & Technical Debt
+- No state validation or schema enforcement
+- No error handling for localStorage quota exceeded
+- No offline sync mechanism
+- Monolithic HTML makes code splitting impossible
+- No TypeScript (runtime safety concerns)
+- Sound effects tied to theme (not independently configurable)
+- No analytics or crash reporting in debug build
+
+## Success Metrics
+- App launches in <2s
+- All games playable without errors
+- State persists across sessions
+- APK builds and installs successfully
+- No lint/build warnings
+- Device testing passes all acceptance gates

+# Arcade Games: Coding Conventions & Standards
+
+## JavaScript Style Guide
+
+### Naming Conventions
+```javascript
+// Variables: camelCase
+let playerScore, currentTheme, isGameActive;
+
+// Constants: SCREAMING_SNAKE_CASE
+const MAX_PLAYERS = 2;
+const GAME_TIMEOUT = 3000;
+
+// Functions: camelCase
+function calculateScore() {}
+const playSound = () => {};
+
+// CSS Custom Properties: --kebab-case
+--bg, --accent, --sh, --barfill
+
+// Classes/Constructors: PascalCase (rarely used in this codebase)
+class GameEngine {}
+
+// Private functions: Prefix with _ (convention only)
+const _validateInput = () => {};
+```
+
+### Code Organization
+1. **Imports & Setup** ΓåÆ HTML meta, fonts
+2. **CSS Variables & Themes** ΓåÆ Complete theme definitions
+3. **Global State** ΓåÆ State object `S`, utility variables
+4. **Audio System** ΓåÆ SND object, audio context, sfx()
+5. **Core Functions** ΓåÆ DOM rendering, navigation
+6. **Page Renderers** ΓåÆ home(), explore(), course(), game screens
+7. **Event Handlers** ΓåÆ Click, input, hash change
+8. **Game Logic** ΓåÆ Individual game implementations
+
+### Syntax Standards
+- **Arrow functions preferred** for callbacks: `const fn = () => {}`
+- **Ternary for simple conditions:** `x ? y : z`
+- **Template literals for strings:** `` `text ${var}` ``
+- **Destructuring where readable:** `const { name, score } = player`
+- **const > let > var** (var is banned)
+- **No semicolons** (project conventionΓÇörely on ASI)
+
+### Comments & Documentation
+```javascript
+// Single-line comments for brief context
+// Multi-line comments for complex logic blocks
+
+/**
+ * Function documentation (if needed)
+ * @param {type} name - Description
+ * @returns {type} Description
+ */
+
+// TODO: Future improvements
+// FIXME: Known issue to address
+// NOTE: Important context for maintainers
+```
+
+## HTML & DOM Conventions
+
+### Data Attributes for Interactivity
+```html
+<button data-go="/path">Navigation</button>
+<button data-act="actionName">Action</button>
+<button data-t="themeId">Theme selector</button>
+<div data-game="gameId">Game container</div>
+```
+
+### CSS Class Naming (BEM-inspired)
+- **Block:** `.tile`, `.row`, `.cta` (standalone reusable)
+- **Element:** `.row .m` (modifier within block)
+- **State:** `.done`, `.cur`, `.sel`, `.lit` (semantic states)
+
+### Accessible HTML
+```html
+<button class="cta">Action</button>
+<div class="toast" id="toast" role="status"></div>
+<nav id="nav" aria-label="Main"></nav>
+<input id="field" aria-label="Input description">
+```
+- Use `id` for unique elements
+- Use `aria-label` for icon-only buttons
+- Use `role` for status/notification regions
+- Focus visible outlines enforced
+
+## State Management
+
+### Immutability Patterns
+```javascript
+// Spread syntax for updates (not mutation)
+S = { ...S, name: "NewName" };
+S.best = { ...S.best, [subjId]: score };
+
+// Avoid mutation
+S.done.push(lesson);  // Γ¥î Bad
+S.done = [...S.done, lesson];  // Γ£à Good
+```
+
+### State Validation
+```javascript
+// Type checking patterns (runtime only)
+if (typeof S.vol !== 'number' || S.vol < 0 || S.vol > 1) {
+  S.vol = 0.7;  // Reset to default
+}
+```
+
+### Persistence
+```javascript
+const save = () => {
+  try {
+    localStorage.setItem('claylearn', JSON.stringify(S));
+  } catch (e) {
+    // Silent fail; app continues with in-memory state
+  }
+};
+```
+
+## Game Implementation Template
+
+### Game Registry Entry
+```javascript
+const GAMES = [
+  {
+    id: 'simon',
+    name: 'Simon',
+    emoji: '≡ƒÄ«',
+    tint: '#ff5a7e',
+    bst: 'Best sequence length',
+    p2: false,  // false = single-player, true = two-player
+    // ... levels/config
+  }
+];
+```
+
+### Game Loop Structure
+```javascript
+let gameState = {
+  active: true,
+  score: 0,
+  level: 1,
+  lives: 3
+};
+
+function startGame() {
+  gameState.active = true;
+  render();
+  // Game logic
+}
+
+function endGame() {
+  gameState.active = false;
+  const finalScore = calculateFinalScore();
+  S.gb[currentGame] = Math.max(S.gb[currentGame] || 0, finalScore);
+  save();
+  // Show result screen
+}
+
+function render() {
+  // Update DOM with gameState
+}
+```
+
+### Game Input Handling
+```javascript
+// Delegated event listeners at top level
+document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
+  if (e.target.dataset.go) go(e.target.dataset.go);
+  if (e.target.dataset.act === 'gameInput') handleGameInput(e);
+});
+```
+
+## CSS Conventions
+
+### Custom Properties (Variables) Priority
+```css
+/* Apply all variables at :root level and theme level [data-theme] */
+:root {
+  --bg: #ece8fb;           /* Background */
+  --card: #f7f4ff;         /* Card surface */
+  --accent: #7c5cf0;       /* Primary action */
+  --green: #5cc46b;        /* Success/positive */
+  --ink: #2d2650;          /* Text color */
+  --mute: #7a72a8;         /* Secondary text */
+  --r: 26px;               /* Border radius */
+  --sh: shadow-value;      /* Shadow effect */
+}
+
+[data-theme="cyber"] {
+  --bg: radial-gradient(...);
+  --accent: #00f5d4;
+  /* All variables MUST be redefined for complete theme coverage */
+}
+```
+
+### Media Queries & Responsive
+```css
+/* Mobile-first; max-width 480px is primary target */
+.phone {
+  max-width: 480px;
+  margin: 0 auto;
+  padding: 18px;
+}
+
+/* No media queries for desktopΓÇöapp is phone-only */
+```
+
+### Animation Standards
+- **Use CSS transitions for state changes:** `.12s` to `.25s`
+- **Reduce motion:** `prefers-reduced-motion:reduce` disables all
+- **No JavaScript animations** (prefer CSS)
+
+## Testing Patterns
+
+### Validation Before Game Start
+```javascript
+// Check state integrity
+if (!GAMES.find(g => g.id === gameId)) return home();
+if (typeof S.vol !== 'number') S.vol = 0.7;
+
+// Sanitize inputs
+const sanitized = esc(userInput);  // XSS protection
+```
+
+### Edge Cases to Handle
+- App launched with corrupted localStorage ΓåÆ Reset to defaults
+- Missing audio context ΓåÆ Graceful audio disable
+- Invalid theme ΓåÆ Fall back to 'clay'
+- Game quit mid-play ΓåÆ Clean up state before returning to home
+- Two-player game with one player ΓåÆ Disable submit button
+
+### Debugging
+```javascript
+// Add to browser console for state inspection
+window.S  // View entire state
+window.save()  // Manually trigger save
+window.go('/game/simon')  // Navigate programmatically
+```
+
+## Error Handling
+
+### Silent Failures Acceptable
+```javascript
+try {
+  const v = localStorage.getItem('claylearn');
+  if (v) S = { ...S, ...JSON.parse(v) };
+} catch (e) {
+  // Silent fail; app runs with defaults
+}
+```
+
+### Audio Context Fallback
+```javascript
+function ac() {
+  if (!ctx) {
+    const A = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
+    if (!A) return null;  // No audio support; sfx() will gracefully skip
+    ctx = new A();
+  }
+  return ctx;
+}
+```
+
+### DOM Not Found
+```javascript
+const el = document.querySelector('#nonexistent');
+if (el) el.textContent = 'text';  // Guard access
+```
+
+## Performance & Optimization
+
+### Monolithic File Constraints
+- **No code splitting:** All 15 games, 12 subjects in single HTML
+- **Lazy rendering:** Only render active screen (hash-based views)
+- **Event delegation:** Single document listener for all clicks
+- **CSS efficiency:** All variables pre-computed; no dynamic calc()
+
+### Memory Management
+```javascript
+// Reuse timers
+clearTimeout(toast.i);  // Clear previous before setting new
+toast.i = setTimeout(() => ..., 1800);
+
+// Avoid closures in loops
+games.forEach((game, i) => {
+  game.onClick = () => handleClick(i);  // Bind i once
+});
+```
+
+### Audio Optimization
+- **Web Audio API** context reused globally
+- **Synthesis:** Procedural tone generation (no samples)
+- **Polyphony:** Max 4 simultaneous notes (managed)
+
+## Version Control & Git
+
+### Commit Message Format
+```
+<type>: <subject> [<scope>]
+
+<body>
+
+Fixes #<issue> | Related <PR>
+```
+
+**Types:** feat, fix, refactor, style, docs, test, chore  
+**Subject:** Present tense, lowercase, <50 chars  
+**Body:** Explain why, not what (wrap at 72 chars)
+
+### Branch Naming
+```
+feature/[feature-name]
+bugfix/[bug-name]
+refactor/[component-name]
+```
+All feature work goes to `develop`, never directly to `main`.
+
+## Code Review Checklist
+
+- [ ] No hardcoded values (use constants)
+- [ ] All user input sanitized with `esc()`
+- [ ] State changes use spread syntax, not mutation
+- [ ] Existing games still playable (regression test)
+- [ ] Audio/theme-aware (respects `S.sound`, `S.theme`)
+- [ ] Mobile-first; responsive on 480px max-width
+- [ ] CSS uses custom properties (no hardcoded colors)
+- [ ] LocalStorage save called after state changes
+- [ ] No console errors or warnings
+- [ ] Accessibility: focus visible, aria-labels present

+# Arcade Games: Game Development Conventions
+
+## Game Registry Structure
+
+All games are registered in the `GAMES` array at the top of `www/index.html`. Each entry defines metadata and configuration:
+
+```javascript
+const GAMES = [
+  {
+    id: 'simon',              // Unique identifier (kebab-case)
+    name: 'Simon',            // Display name
+    emoji: '≡ƒÄ«',              // Icon emoji
+    tint: '#ff5a7e',          // Theme color (hex)
+    bst: 'Best sequence...',  // High score label
+    p2: false,                // Single-player: false, Two-player: true
+    // Game-specific config below
+  },
+  // ... 14 more games
+];
+```
+
+### Required Fields
+- `id`: Unique string, must match game function name
+- `name`: Display name (max 12 chars recommended)
+- `emoji`: Unicode emoji (1ΓÇô2 chars)
+- `tint`: CSS hex color for card background
+- `bst`: High score label (e.g., "Best Score", "Best Time", "Longest Sequence")
+- `p2`: Boolean indicating multiplayer capability
+
+### Optional Fields
+- `levels`: Array of level configurations
+- `difficulty`: Starting difficulty (easy/medium/hard)
+- `minPlayers`, `maxPlayers`: Constraints for multiplayer
+
+## Game Lifecycle
+
+### Initialization
+```javascript
+function [gameId]Start() {
+  // 1. Clear previous game state
+  // 2. Initialize game-specific state
+  // 3. Set up event listeners (if not delegated)
+  // 4. Render initial screen
+  // 5. Start audio context
+  
+  let gameState = {
+    active: true,
+    score: 0,
+    level: 1,
+    startTime: Date.now(),
+    sequence: [],
+    userInput: [],
+  };
+  
+  render();
+}
+```
+
+### Playing
+```javascript
+// Game update loop (event-driven, not tick-based)
+function handleGameInput(input) {
+  if (!gameState.active) return;
+  
+  gameState.userInput.push(input);
+  sfx('tap');  // Play feedback
+  
+  // Validate move
+  if (isValidMove(input)) {
+    gameState.score += points;
+    render();
+  } else {
+    gameState.lives--;
+    sfx('wrong');
+    if (gameState.lives <= 0) endGame(false);
+    else render();
+  }
+}
+```
+
+### Completion
+```javascript
+function endGame(won) {
+  gameState.active = false;
+  
+  // 1. Calculate final score
+  const finalScore = gameState.score;
+  
+  // 2. Update high score if needed
+  if (finalScore > (S.gb[currentGame] || 0)) {
+    S.gb[currentGame] = finalScore;
+  }
+  
+  // 3. Play end sound
+  sfx('end', won ? 2 : 0);  // arg: 2 = victory, 0 = defeat
+  
+  // 4. Persist state
+  save();
+  
+  // 5. Show result screen
+  renderGameOver(won, finalScore);
+  
+  // 6. Provide navigation back to games list
+}
+```
+
+## Rendering Patterns
+
+### Game Screen Template
+```javascript
+function render[GameId]() {
+  return `
+    <div class="top">
+      <button class="back" data-go="/games">ΓåÉ</button>
+      <h1>${GAMES.find(g => g.id === currentGame).name}</h1>
+      <div class="star">${gameState.score}</div>
+    </div>
+    
+    <div class="gstage">
+      <!-- Game-specific content -->
+      <div class="arena">
+        <!-- Game arena (canvas/grid/buttons) -->
+      </div>
+      
+      <!-- Controls -->
+      <div class="row2">
+        <button class="cta" data-act="resume" ${!gameState.active ? 'disabled' : ''}>Continue</button>
+        <button class="cta alt" data-go="/games">Quit</button>
+      </div>
+    </div>
+  `;
+}
+```
+
+### Result Screen Template
+```javascript
+function renderGameOver(won, score) {
+  const message = won 
+    ? `You won! Final score: ${score}` 
+    : `Game over. Score: ${score}`;
+  
+  return `
+    <div class="gstage">
+      <div class="trophy">${won ? '≡ƒÅå' : '≡ƒÿó'}</div>
+      <h2>${message}</h2>
+      <div class="star">${S.gb[currentGame] || 0}</div>
+      <small>Best score</small>
+      <button class="cta" data-go="/game/${currentGame}">Play Again</button>
+      <button class="cta alt" data-go="/games">Back to Games</button>
+    </div>
+  `;
+}
+```
+
+## State Management
+
+### Local Game State
+```javascript
+// Declare at game function scope
+let gameState = {
+  active: true,
+  score: 0,
+  lives: 3,
+  level: 1,
+  moves: [],
+  // Game-specific fields
+};
+
+// Update via spread (if complex)
+gameState = { ...gameState, score: gameState.score + 10 };
+
+// Direct mutation OK for local game state (not global S)
+gameState.lives--;
+```
+
+### High Score Persistence
+```javascript
+// Write to global state after game ends
+S.gb = {
+  ...S.gb,
+  [currentGame]: Math.max(S.gb[currentGame] || 0, finalScore)
+};
+save();
+
+// Read for display
+const bestScore = S.gb[currentGame] || 0;
+```
+
+### Avoid Global State Leakage
+```javascript
+// Γ¥î Bad: Game state persisted to S
+S.currentSequence = [];
+
+// Γ£à Good: Local game state only
+let gameState = { sequence: [] };
+```
+
+## Input Handling
+
+### Delegated Event Listeners
+```javascript
+// Add to top-level click handler
+document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
+  if (e.target.dataset.act === 'gameInput') {
+    const inputType = e.target.dataset.input;
+    handleGameInput(inputType);
+  }
+});
+```
+
+### Two-Player Input Swapping
+```javascript
+function handleTwoPlayerInput(input) {
+  gameState.currentPlayer = gameState.currentPlayer === 0 ? 1 : 0;
+  
+  if (gameState.currentPlayer === 1) {
+    // Show "Pass device to Player 2" toast
+    toast('Pass the device to Player 2');
+    setTimeout(() => continueGame(), 2000);
+  }
+}
+```
+
+### Keyboard Input (for typing games)
+```javascript
+document.addEventListener('keypress', (e) => {
+  if (!currentScreen.startsWith('game/')) return;
+  if (!gameState.active) return;
+  
+  const key = e.key.toLowerCase();
+  handleGameInput(key);
+});
+```
+
+## Audio & Sound Effects
+
+### Theme-Aware Sound Synthesis
+```javascript
+// Define in SND object (already complete)
+const SND = {
+  clay: { w: 'sine', p: 1, d: 1, v: 1 },
+  cyber: { w: 'sawtooth', p: 1, d: 1, v: 0.4, fx: 1 },
+  // ...
+};
+
+// Call sfx() with event name
+sfx('tap');        // Input feedback
+sfx('correct');    // Right answer
+sfx('wrong');      // Wrong answer
+sfx('lesson');     // Lesson complete
+sfx('end', 2);     // Victory (arg: 2=victory, 1=good, 0=loss)
+sfx('note', idx);  // Note (arg: 0ΓÇô3 for musical notes)
+sfx('pop');        // Pop/bubble burst
+sfx('theme');      // Theme change confirmation
+```
+
+### Fallback for Silent Mode
+```javascript
+// sfx() checks S.sound automatically
+if (!S.sound) return;  // No sound effects if disabled
+
+// Graceful audio context failure
+const ctx = ac();
+if (!ctx) return;  // Audio not supported
+```
+
+## Scoring Patterns
+
+### Consistent Point Awards
+```javascript
+const POINTS = {
+  gameWin: 30,      // Winning a game
+  gameLose: 5,      // Losing but participating
+  levelComplete: 10,
+  perfectRound: 15,
+  speedBonus: 5,    // For time-based challenges
+};
+
+gameState.score += POINTS.gameWin;
+```
+
+### Time-Based Scoring
+```javascript
+function calculateTimeBonus(elapsedMs, maxMs) {
+  const ratio = Math.max(0, 1 - (elapsedMs / maxMs));
+  return Math.floor(ratio * 20);  // Max 20 bonus points
+}
+```
+
+## Two-Player Game Patterns
+
+### Turn Switching
+```javascript
+function switchPlayer() {
+  gameState.currentPlayer = gameState.currentPlayer === 0 ? 1 : 0;
+  gameState.players[gameState.currentPlayer].ready = false;
+  toast(`Player ${gameState.currentPlayer + 1}'s turn`);
+  renderTurn();
+}
+```
+
+### Score Tracking Per Player
+```javascript
+let gameState = {
+  players: [
+    { name: 'Player 1', score: 0 },
+    { name: 'Player 2', score: 0 },
+  ],
+  currentPlayer: 0,
+};
+
+function endTwoPlayerGame() {
+  const p1Score = gameState.players[0].score;
+  const p2Score = gameState.players[1].score;
+  
+  if (p1Score > p2Score) {
+    renderGameOver(true, p1Score, 'Player 1 Wins!');
+  } else if (p2Score > p1Score) {
+    renderGameOver(true, p2Score, 'Player 2 Wins!');
+  } else {
+    renderGameOver(false, p1Score, 'Tie!');
+  }
+}
+```
+
+## Common Game Patterns
+
+### Memory/Sequence Game
+```javascript
+function playSequence() {
+  gameState.sequence.push(Math.floor(Math.random() * 4));
+  gameState.userInput = [];
+  
+  gameState.sequence.forEach((note, i) => {
+    setTimeout(() => {
+      highlightPad(note);
+      sfx('note', note);
+    }, (i + 1) * 600);
+  });
+}
+
+function checkUserInput(input) {
+  gameState.userInput.push(input);
+  sfx('note', input);
+  
+  const index = gameState.userInput.length - 1;
+  if (gameState.userInput[index] !== gameState.sequence[index]) {
+    sfx('wrong');
+    endGame(false);
+    return false;
+  }
+  
+  if (gameState.userInput.length === gameState.sequence.length) {
+    gameState.level++;
+    setTimeout(playSequence, 1000);
+  }
+  return true;
+}
+```
+
+### Quiz Game
+```javascript
+function renderQuiz(question, options) {
+  return `
+    <div class="qcard">
+      <div><span class="qe">${question.emoji || '≡ƒñö'}</span></div>
+      <h2>${question.text}</h2>
+    </div>
+    <div class="opts3">
+      ${options.map((opt, i) => `
+        <button class="mb2" data-act="answer" data-idx="${i}">
+          ${opt}
+        </button>
+      `).join('')}
+    </div>
+  `;
+}
+
+function handleAnswer(selectedIdx) {
+  const correct = selectedIdx === 0;  // First option is always correct
+  sfx(correct ? 'correct' : 'wrong');
+  gameState.score += correct ? 10 : 0;
+  
+  if (gameState.questionIndex < gameState.questions.length - 1) {
+    gameState.questionIndex++;
+    render();
+  } else {
+    endGame(gameState.score > gameState.questions.length * 5);
+  }
+}
+```
+
+### Tapping/Reflexes Game
+```javascript
+function startRound() {
+  gameState.targets = generateRandomTargets(5);
+  gameState.tapped = 0;
+  gameState.roundStart = Date.now();
+  gameState.active = true;
+  render();
+}
+
+function handleTap(targetId) {
+  if (!gameState.targets.find(t => t.id === targetId)) {
+    sfx('wrong');
+    return;  // Missed target
+  }
+  
+  gameState.tapped++;
+  sfx('correct');
+  gameState.targets = gameState.targets.filter(t => t.id !== targetId);
+  
+  if (gameState.tapped === 5) {
+    const time = Date.now() - gameState.roundStart;
+    gameState.score += Math.max(5, 100 - time / 10);
+    endGame(true);
+  }
+}
+```
+
+## Testing Guidelines
+
+### Game Logic Unit Tests
+```javascript
+// Test sequence validation
+assert(checkUserInput(0) === true);  // Correct
+assert(checkUserInput(1) === false); // Wrong sequence
+
+// Test scoring
+assert(calculateScore(300) === 20);  // Time bonus
+
+// Test state transitions
+assert(gameState.active === false);  // After end game
+```
+
+### Manual Gameplay Testing
+- [ ] Game starts from "Games" list
+- [ ] Initial state renders correctly
+- [ ] User input is recognized and scored
+- [ ] Sound effects play (when enabled)
+- [ ] High score is saved and persists
+- [ ] Game over screen shows final score
+- [ ] Quit returns to games list without saving
+- [ ] Two-player: turn switching works
+- [ ] Two-player: both players can input
+- [ ] No console errors during play
+
+## Performance Optimization
+
+### Avoid Layout Thrashing
+```javascript
+// Γ¥î Bad: Forces reflow in loop
+for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
+  element.style.left = getComputedStyle(element).left;
+}
+
+// Γ£à Good: Batch DOM reads/writes
+const positions = Array.from(elements).map(el => el.offsetLeft);
+elements.forEach((el, i) => el.style.left = positions[i] + 'px');
+```
+
+### Efficient Rendering
+```javascript
+// For animations, use CSS transitions
+element.style.transition = 'transform 0.2s';
+element.style.transform = 'scale(0.97)';
+
+// Not JS timers for visual effects
+```
+
+### Memory Cleanup
+```javascript
+function endGame() {
+  // Cancel pending timeouts/intervals
+  clearTimeout(gameState.timer);
+  clearInterval(gameState.loop);
+  
+  // Remove dynamic event listeners (if game-specific)
+  document.removeEventListener('keypress', handleGameKeypress);
+  
+  gameState = null;  // Release reference
+}
+```

+# Arcade Games: Android Build Requirements & Process
+
+## Build Environment
+
+### Required Tools
+- **Node.js** 20.x (LTS)
+- **Java** 17 (OpenJDK via Temurin)
+- **Gradle** 8.x (bundled with Capacitor Android project)
+- **Android SDK** (installed via Capacitor, no manual setup)
+- **Git** (for repository access)
+
+### GitHub Actions Workflow
+- Runs on **ubuntu-latest**
+- Auto-triggered on push to `main` branch
+- Can be manually triggered via workflow_dispatch
+- Builds APK in ~5ΓÇô8 minutes
+
+## Build Steps (Automated in CI)
+
+### 1. Checkout Repository
+```bash
+actions/checkout@v4
+```
+Clones the repository and checks out the pushed commit.
+
+### 2. Setup Node
+```bash
+actions/setup-node@v4
+node-version: 20
+```
+Installs Node.js 20 runtime.
+
+### 3. Setup Java
+```bash
+actions/setup-java@v4
+distribution: temurin
+java-version: 17
+```
+Installs OpenJDK 17.
+
+### 4. Setup Gradle Cache
+```bash
+gradle/actions/setup-gradle@v4
+```
+Caches Gradle dependencies to speed up builds.
+
+### 5. Install Dependencies
+```bash
+npm install
+```
+Installs Node packages from `package.json`:
+- @capacitor/core
+- @capacitor/cli
+- @capacitor/android
+- @capacitor-community/admob
+
+### 6. Add Android Platform
+```bash
+npx cap add android
+```
+Creates `android/` directory with Gradle project structure.
+- Idempotent; safe to run multiple times
+- Generates `android/variables.gradle`, `android/build.gradle`, etc.
+
+### 7. Configure Android Manifest & SDK
+```bash
+# Add AdMob metadata
+sed -i "s#</application>#<meta-data android:name=\"com.google.android.gms.ads.APPLICATION_ID\" android:value=\"${ADMOB_APP_ID}\"/></application>#" android/app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml
+
+# Set min SDK to 23
+sed -i 's/minSdkVersion = 22/minSdkVersion = 23/' android/variables.gradle
+
+# Verify changes
+grep -n "APPLICATION_ID" android/app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml
+grep -n "minSdkVersion" android/variables.gradle
+```
+
+**Why min SDK 23?**
+- Google Play requires SDK 23 or higher as of 2024
+- API 23 = Android 6.0 (90%+ device coverage)
+- Required for modern Android features and Play Store compliance
+
+### 8. Sync Web Files
+```bash
+npx cap sync android
+```
+Copies:
+- `www/` ΓåÆ `android/app/src/main/assets/public/`
+- Plugins metadata into Android manifest
+- Ensures web app and native wrapper are in sync
+
+### 9. Build Debug APK
+```bash
+chmod +x android/gradlew
+cd android
+./gradlew assembleDebug
+```
+Outputs: `android/app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk`
+
+**assembleDebug vs. build:**
+- `assembleDebug`: Builds APK only (faster, no testing)
+- `build`: Builds APK + runs unit tests (if configured)
+
+### 10. Upload Artifact
+```bash
+actions/upload-artifact@v4
+name: style-lab-apk
+path: android/app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk
+if-no-files-found: error
+```
+Makes APK available for download from Actions tab (expires in 90 days).
+
+## Local Build (Without GitHub Actions)
+
+### Prerequisites
+```bash
+# Install Java 17 (on Windows)
+# Download from https://adoptium.net/ or use choco
+choco install openjdk17
+
+# Verify
+java -version
+```
+
+### Build Steps
+```bash
+# Clone and navigate to repo
+git clone https://github.com/nitinsameer/arcade-games.git
+cd arcade-games
+
+# Install dependencies
+npm install
+
+# Add Android platform
+npx cap add android
+
+# Configure (manual)
+# 1. Edit android/app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml manually
+# 2. Edit android/variables.gradle (minSdkVersion = 23)
+
+# Sync
+npx cap sync android
+
+# Build
+cd android
+./gradlew assembleDebug
+# or (with tests)
+./gradlew build
+
+# Find APK
+ls android/app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk
+```
+
+## Installation on Android Device
+
+### Prerequisites
+- Android device with USB debugging enabled
+- Android Debug Bridge (adb) installed
+- Device connected via USB
+
+### Install APK
+```bash
+# List connected devices
+adb devices
+
+# Install APK
+adb install -r android/app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk
+
+# Launch app
+adb shell am start -n com.nishaan.stylelab/.MainActivity
+```
+
+### Verify Installation
+```bash
+# Check app package
+adb shell pm list packages | grep stylelab
+
+# Check app info
+adb shell pm dump com.nishaan.stylelab | grep versionName
+```
+
+## Build Troubleshooting
+
+### Problem: `minSdkVersion` too low
+**Error:** `App targets Android API 23, but Google Play requires minimum 23`
+**Fix:** Update `android/variables.gradle` to `minSdkVersion = 23`
+
+### Problem: AdMob metadata missing
+**Error:** `ERROR: Application doesn't declare the required Google Play Services module`
+**Fix:** Ensure `sed` command adds `<meta-data>` to AndroidManifest.xml before `</application>`
+
+### Problem: Java version mismatch
+**Error:** `Failed to apply plugin 'com.android.application'`
+**Fix:** Ensure Java 17 is installed and `JAVA_HOME` is set:
+```bash
+# Windows
+set JAVA_HOME=C:\Program Files\Eclipse Adoptium\jdk-17.0.x
+
+# macOS/Linux
+export JAVA_HOME=$(/usr/libexec/java_home -v 17)
+```
+
+### Problem: Gradle daemon issues
+**Error:** `Daemon vm is locked`
+**Fix:** Kill Gradle processes:
+```bash
+./gradlew --stop
+# or
+pkill -f gradle
+```
+
+### Problem: Out of memory during build
+**Error:** `java.lang.OutOfMemoryError: GC overhead limit exceeded`
+**Fix:** Increase Gradle heap size in `gradle.properties`:
+```properties
+org.gradle.jvmargs=-Xmx4096m
+```
+
+## Capacitor Sync Process
+
+### What `npx cap sync android` Does
+1. **Cleans** previous build artifacts
+2. **Copies** `www/` directory to Android assets
+3. **Updates** native plugins (if any)
+4. **Injects** plugin code into AndroidManifest.xml
+5. **Regenerates** Gradle files
+
+### When to Sync
+- After editing `www/index.html`
+- After changing `capacitor.config.json`
+- After installing a new Capacitor plugin
+- Before every build
+
+### Safe Re-sync
+```bash
+npx cap sync android
+npx cap copy android  # If only copying files
+```
+
+## APK Signing (Production Only)
+
+### During Development (Debug Signing)
+- Built with default debug key
+- Unique per development environment
+- Cannot be installed alongside release APK
+- OK for internal testing
+
+### Before Production Release
+```bash
+# Generate keystore (one-time)
+keytool -genkey -v -keystore my-release-key.jks -keyalg RSA -keysize 2048 -validity 10000 -alias my-key-alias
+
+# Build signed APK (CI/CD only)
+./gradlew assembleRelease -Pandroid.injected.signing.store.file=my-release-key.jks -Pandroid.injected.signing.store.password=... -Pandroid.injected.signing.key.alias=my-key-alias -Pandroid.injected.signing.key.password=...
+```
+
+**ΓÜá∩╕Å Never commit signing keys to Git**
+
+## Build Configuration Files
+
+### `capacitor.config.json`
+Capacitor bridge configuration:
+```json
+{
+  "appId": "com.nishaan.stylelab",
+  "appName": "Style Lab",
+  "webDir": "www",
+  "backgroundColor": "#ece8fb"
+}
+```
+- `appId`: Android package name (reverse domain notation)
+- `appName`: Display name in launcher
+- `webDir`: Location of web assets
+- `backgroundColor`: Splash screen color
+
+### `android/variables.gradle`
+Gradle build variables:
+```gradle
+minSdkVersion = 23          // Min Android API
+targetSdkVersion = 34       // Target API (update yearly)
+compileSdkVersion = 34      // Compile against API
+gradlePluginVersion = 8.2.0
+```
+
+### `android/app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml`
+Android permissions and metadata:
+```xml
+<uses-permission android:name="android.permission.INTERNET" />
+<uses-permission android:name="android.permission.ACCESS_NETWORK_STATE" />
+
+<application>
+  <activity android:name=".MainActivity" ... />
+  <meta-data android:name="com.google.android.gms.ads.APPLICATION_ID" android:value="ca-app-pub-3940256099942544~3347511713" />
+</application>
+```
+
+## Performance & File Size
+
+### APK Size Breakdown (Typical)
+- **Capacitor runtime:** ~2 MB
+- **WebView assets (www/):** ~50 KB
+- **Android framework:** ~1 MB
+- **AdMob library:** ~3 MB
+- **Total:** ~6ΓÇô7 MB (unoptimized)
+
+### Optimization Options (Not Currently Applied)
+- **ProGuard/R8:** Minify Java code
+- **Asset compression:** Reduce image/media size
+- **Code splitting:** Not possible with monolithic HTML
+- **WebView features:** Disable unused APIs
+
+## Continuous Integration (CI) Pipeline
+
+### Current Workflow
+- **Trigger:** Push to `main` or manual dispatch
+- **Duration:** 5ΓÇô8 minutes
+- **Artifact:** APK expires in 90 days
+- **Logs:** Available in GitHub Actions tab
+
+### Recommended Enhancements (Future)
+- Add `develop` branch test builds
+- Create signed APK for release
+- Add Firebase Crashlytics integration
+- Automated Play Store upload
+
+## Testing Before Build
+
+### Pre-Build Validation
+```bash
+# Check for syntax errors
+npm run lint  # (Not configured; could add ESLint)
+
+# Check file structure
+ls www/index.html
+grep -c "const GAMES" www/index.html  # Should be 1
+
+# Verify dependencies
+npm list @capacitor/core  # Should be 6.2.0
+```
+
+### Post-Build Verification
+```bash
+# Check APK exists
+ls -lh android/app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk
+
+# Inspect APK contents (zip archive)
+unzip -l app-debug.apk | head -20
+
+# Check for assets
+unzip -l app-debug.apk | grep "index.html"
+```

+# Arcade Games: Privacy & Compliance Framework
+
+## Google Play Families Policy (COPPA Compliance)
+
+### Age Targeting: Kids 4ΓÇô8
+- **Intended Audience:** Primary school children
+- **Content Rating:** All Ages (Green/Approved)
+- **Special Handling:** COPPA-compliant (no personal data beyond username)
+
+### Prohibited Features
+**Never add without explicit compliance review:**
+- Γ¥î Third-party tracking (Google Analytics, Firebase Analytics with ID tracking)
+- Γ¥î Social features (chat, multiplayer with player IDs, leaderboards with names)
+- Γ¥î Exiting to external web (no web links, no YouTube embeds)
+- Γ¥î Personal data collection (email, phone, address, location)
+- Γ¥î Persistent user IDs shared outside app
+- Γ¥î Behavioral advertising (targeted ads based on activity)
+- Γ¥î User-generated content (comments, profiles, custom avatars from camera)
+- Γ¥î In-app purchases without explicit parent consent
+- Γ¥î Selling or sharing user data
+- Γ¥î Microphone/camera access
+
+### Permitted Features
+- Γ£à Local app state (name, progress, highscores in localStorage)
+- Γ£à First-party ads (Google Test AdMob IDs only, no personalization)
+- Γ£à Theme customization
+- Γ£à Sound/music control
+- Γ£à Offline gameplay (all content pre-loaded)
+- Γ£à Basic error reporting (non-personalized crash logs)
+
+## Parental Consent & Age Gating
+
+### Current Implementation
+- **Age selection:** Part of onboarding flow
+- **Consent model:** Silent feature flag (automatic consent during age selection)
+- **No UI:** Parent/guardian consent is implied by age selection
+- **Documentation:** Built-in privacy notice (non-persistent)
+
+### When Adding Features
+1. **Check:** Does feature collect/use personal data?
+   - If YES ΓåÆ Requires explicit parental consent UI
+   - If NO ΓåÆ Proceed with development
+2. **Store:** Parental consent flag in state: `S.parentConsent = true`
+3. **Verify:** Consent stored before feature activates
+4. **Audit:** Code review must verify compliance
+
+### Example: Hypothetical Cloud Save Feature
+```javascript
+// Γ¥î Bad: Saves data without consent
+const saveToCloud = (data) => {
+  fetch('https://backend.nishaan.com/save', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify(data) });
+};
+
+// Γ£à Good: Checks consent, persists locally only
+const saveState = (data) => {
+  if (S.parentConsent !== true) {
+    console.warn('Parental consent required for cloud sync');
+    return;
+  }
+  // Proceed with sync (but keep local copy as fallback)
+};
+```
+
+## Data Handling & Storage
+
+### Local Storage (Current)
+- **Data:** `localStorage.claylearn` (JSON)
+- **Contains:** Name, theme, sound settings, progress, scores
+- **Lifetime:** Until app uninstall or "Reset Profile" button
+- **Security:** Stored in app sandbox (Android), encrypted at rest by OS
+- **No PII:** No email, phone, address, device ID, or IDFA
+
+### What NOT to Store
+- Email addresses or contact info
+- Device identifiers (IMEI, Android ID, GAID)
+- IP addresses
+- Device model/OS version (for fingerprinting)
+- Behavior analytics (session duration, feature usage)
+- External user IDs or tokens
+
+### Future Cloud Storage (If Implemented)
+1. **Require parental consent UI** (visible, confirmed)
+2. **Encrypt data in transit** (HTTPS only)
+3. **Encrypt data at rest** (server-side encryption)
+4. **Implement data deletion** (user-initiated, 30-day purge)
+5. **Document retention policy** (explicit timeline)
+6. **No data sharing** (internal use only)
+7. **Audit logging** (track all access)
+
+## Ad Management
+
+### Current Implementation
+- **AdMob Test IDs:** `ca-app-pub-3940256099942544~3347511713` (app) and rewarded unit
+- **Ad Format:** Rewarded video only (no banner, interstitial, or native ads)
+- **Behavior:** Opt-in; player initiates ad watch for coins
+- **Personalization:** OFF (Google policy requires G-rated + child-directed flags)
+- **Frequency Cap:** 8 ads max per day, 20-second cooldown
+
+### Before Publishing to Play Store
+1. **Create AdMob Account** ΓåÆ https://admob.google.com
+2. **Add App** ΓåÆ Mark as "Kids" category
+3. **Create Rewarded Unit** ΓåÆ Get production unit ID
+4. **Update Build:**
+   ```javascript
+   // In www/index.html, find: const ADS={...}
+   const ADS = {
+     rewardedId: 'ca-app-pub-XXXX/YYYY',  // Your rewarded unit ID
+     testing: false,  // Set to false for production
+   };
+   ```
+5. **Update CI/CD** ΓåÆ Set `ADMOB_APP_ID` in GitHub Actions secrets
+6. **Configure AdMob:**
+   - Mark app as child-directed in AdMob settings
+   - Enable "Content rating" ΓåÆ All Ages
+   - Ensure G-rated ad requests only
+
+### Ad Request Headers (Automatic)
+App sends Google required headers:
+```javascript
+// Pseudo-code; Web Audio context includes:
+// "is_tagged_for_kids": true
+// "content_rating": "G"
+// "keywords": "educational"
+```
+
+## Permissions (Android Manifest)
+
+### Current Permissions
+```xml
+<!-- Required by Capacitor & AdMob -->
+<uses-permission android:name="android.permission.INTERNET" />
+<uses-permission android:name="android.permission.ACCESS_NETWORK_STATE" />
+```
+
+### Never Request
+- Γ¥î `RECORD_AUDIO` (even if "no microphone used")
+- Γ¥î `CAMERA` (even if "no camera used")
+- Γ¥î `ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION` / `ACCESS_COARSE_LOCATION`
+- Γ¥î `READ_CONTACTS` / `GET_ACCOUNTS`
+- Γ¥î `READ_CALENDAR`
+- Γ¥î `READ_SMS` / `SEND_SMS`
+- Γ¥î `CHANGE_WIFI_STATE` / `MODIFY_PHONE_STATE`
+
+**Note:** Declaring unused permissions violates Google Play policy and increases rejection risk.
+
+## Content Policy Compliance
+
+### Allowed Content
+- Γ£à Educational subjects (math, science, reading, etc.)
+- Γ£à Cartoon/emoji art style
+- Γ£à Sound effects and background music
+- Γ£à Themes/visual customization
+- Γ£à Highscores and progress tracking
+- Γ£à Age-appropriate difficulty levels
+
+### Prohibited Content
+- Γ¥î Profanity or offensive language (any language)
+- Γ¥î Violence or weapons
+- Γ¥î Alcohol, tobacco, drugs
+- Γ¥î Sexual or suggestive content
+- Γ¥î Gambling or loot boxes (randomized paid items)
+- Γ¥î Misleading health/medical claims
+- Γ¥î Hate speech or discrimination
+
+### Content Audit Before Submission
+1. **Quiz questions:** Verify all text is age-appropriate
+2. **Game assets:** Check emoji/images for hidden meanings
+3. **Story content:** Ensure no disturbing themes
+4. **Audio:** Listen to all sound effects for appropriateness
+
+## Crash & Error Reporting
+
+### Current Implementation
+- **No remote reporting** (no crashes sent to servers)
+- **Local logs only** (browser console in dev, silent on device)
+
+### If Adding Crash Reporting (Future)
+```javascript
+// Γ£à Good: Non-personalized crash report
+window.addEventListener('error', (event) => {
+  const report = {
+    message: event.message,
+    stack: event.error?.stack,
+    timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
+    // NO: user ID, device ID, location, or behavior data
+  };
+  
+  // Send to Crash Analytics (Firebase Crashlytics has COPPA settings)
+  // Requires: S.parentConsent === true
+});
+```
+
+## Code Review Checklist for Privacy
+
+- [ ] No personal data collection without explicit consent
+- [ ] All user input sanitized (no PII in error messages)
+- [ ] Ad requests include Google-required headers
+- [ ] No third-party tracking libraries integrated
+- [ ] No social sharing features (Twitter, Facebook links)
+- [ ] No external web navigation (all links, if any, go to policy page)
+- [ ] No persistent user IDs shared outside app
+- [ ] Sound/notifications respect parental controls
+- [ ] No forced data sharing (opt-in only)
+- [ ] Permissions only match declared functionality
+
+## Documentation & Policies
+
+### App Store Description (Play Store)
+```
+Style Lab: Fun learning games for kids 4-8.
+
+Master 12 subjects through interactive games and lessons.
+Earn coins, unlock themes, and build your skills.
+
+- No ads (except optional rewarded videos)
+- No personal data collection
+- No social features
+- 100% offline-capable
+- COPPA-compliant
+```
+
+### Privacy Policy (Required for Play Store)
+Create a simple one-page policy covering:
+1. **Data we collect:** App state only (name, progress, scores)
+2. **How we use it:** To save progress locally
+3. **Third parties:** Google Play, AdMob (test IDs)
+4. **Parental rights:** Easy reset button, data deletion on uninstall
+5. **Contact:** Your contact email
+
+**Hosted at:** GitHub Pages or similar (link in Play Store listing)
+
+### Terms of Service (Optional but Recommended)
+Simple terms covering:
+- App is provided "as-is"
+- No warranty for data loss (user responsible for backups)
+- Reserves right to add features or monetization
+- COPPA compliance statement
+
+## Compliance Checklist for Launch
+
+Before uploading to Play Store:
+
+**Content & Features:**
+- [ ] All quiz questions reviewed (no inappropriate content)
+- [ ] All emoji/images age-appropriate
+- [ ] No exit buttons to external web (unless policy link)
+- [ ] No social sharing (no Twitter/Facebook/email buttons)
+- [ ] Sound effects appropriate for kids
+
+**Data & Privacy:**
+- [ ] No personal data collected beyond username & progress
+- [ ] No tracking libraries (Google Analytics, Facebook SDK)
+- [ ] No remote data transmission (except optional ads)
+- [ ] ParentCare reset button functional
+- [ ] Privacy policy written and linked
+
+**Ads & Monetization:**
+- [ ] AdMob test IDs replaced with production IDs
+- [ ] Rewarded ad only (no banners/interstitials)
+- [ ] Ads marked as child-directed in AdMob
+- [ ] Max 8 ads per day enforced
+
+**Permissions:**
+- [ ] No unused permissions declared
+- [ ] No camera/microphone permissions
+- [ ] Only INTERNET + ACCESS_NETWORK_STATE
+
+**Technical:**
+- [ ] Min SDK = 23
+- [ ] Target SDK = 34 (or latest)
+- [ ] No console errors
+- [ ] All games playable end-to-end
+- [ ] Tested on Android 6.0+ devices
+
+## Reporting Compliance Issues
+
+If you discover a potential compliance violation:
+1. **Stop deployment** (do not release APK)
+2. **Document issue** (screenshot, user flow, code location)
+3. **Review with lead** (arcade-lead agent)
+4. **Fix before resubmission** (do not force through)
+5. **Re-audit** (full compliance checklist)
+
+**Rejection reasons to avoid:**
+- Contains or requests unrequired permissions
+- Collects personal information without notice
+- Not listed as Kids category
+- Exiting to external website
+- Gambling, loot boxes, or randomized purchases
+- Using prohibited SDKs (Google Analytics with user ID)

+# Arcade Games: Acceptance & Quality Gates
+
+## Task Success Criteria Framework
+
+All development tasks must pass these gates before completion. Report each criterion as:
+- **PASS** ΓÇö Criterion met, verified
+- **FAIL** ΓÇö Criterion not met; work incomplete
+- **BLOCKED** ΓÇö Criterion cannot be verified (missing tools/environment)
+- **NOT APPLICABLE** ΓÇö Criterion irrelevant to task scope
+
+## Gate 1: Feature Completeness
+
+### Requirement: All requested functionality implemented
+**Verification:**
+- [ ] Task description requirements are satisfied
+- [ ] Acceptance criteria from task specification met
+- [ ] No features marked TODO or FIXME remain
+- [ ] Feature works end-to-end from user perspective
+
+**If game development:**
+- [ ] Game launches from Games list
+- [ ] Game is playable without errors
+- [ ] High score saves and persists
+- [ ] Quit returns to Games list cleanly
+
+**If subject/lesson development:**
+- [ ] Lesson content displays correctly
+- [ ] Quiz questions appear and answer validation works
+- [ ] Progress tracking updates state
+- [ ] Completion unlocks next lesson
+
+**How to verify:**
+```javascript
+// Manual testing checklist
+- [ ] Navigate to feature (hash routing)
+- [ ] Use feature from start to finish
+- [ ] Verify all interactive elements respond
+- [ ] Check for error messages in console
+- [ ] Confirm state persists after navigation
+```
+
+**Report format:**
+```
+Gate 1: Feature Completeness
+- Requirement 1: PASS Γ£ô
+- Requirement 2: PASS Γ£ô
+- Requirement 3: FAIL ΓÇö [specific issue]
+Status: FAIL (cannot proceed until fixed)
+```
+
+## Gate 2: Existing Games Regression
+
+### Requirement: All 15 existing games continue to work
+**Verification:**
+- [ ] Each game in GAMES array is accessible
+- [ ] Each game launches without errors
+- [ ] Each game accepts user input correctly
+- [ ] Each game ends gracefully
+- [ ] High scores are recordable
+- [ ] No console errors during play
+
+**Manual test all 15 games:**
+1. Simon (memory)
+2. Whack-a-Mole (reflexes)
+3. Typing/Spell (letters)
+4. Word Slots (Hangman)
+5. Quiz (flashcards)
+6. Connect Four (grid)
+7. Balloon Pop (tapping)
+8. Drum Kit (audio)
+9. Memory/Match (pairs)
+10. Tic Tac Toe 2P (turn-based)
+11. Tug of War 2P (meter race)
+12. Rock Paper Scissors 2P (hand game)
+13. Quiz Duel 2P (competition)
+14. Rhythm Simon 2P (pattern pass)
+15. Number Race 2P (counting)
+
+**How to verify:**
+```javascript
+// Automated check
+for (const game of GAMES) {
+  if (!game.id || !game.name || !game.emoji) {
+    console.error(`Game ${game.id} missing metadata`);
+  }
+}
+
+// Manual: Play each for 30 seconds minimum
+GAMES.forEach(g => {
+  console.log(`Testing ${g.name}...`);
+  go(`/game/${g.id}`);
+  // Play, then check console for errors
+});
+```
+
+**Report format:**
+```
+Gate 2: Regression Testing
+- Simon: PASS Γ£ô
+- Whack-a-Mole: PASS Γ£ô
+- ...
+- Number Race 2P: PASS Γ£ô
+Status: PASS (all games playable)
+```
+
+**Failure resolution:**
+- If game fails to load: Check game function exists in index.html
+- If game crashes: Review error message, check game state initialization
+- If high score not saved: Verify `S.gb[gameId]` is updated and `save()` called
+- If console errors: Debug and fix before resubmission
+
+## Gate 3: Unit & Integration Tests
+
+### Requirement: Relevant tests pass; no new test failures
+**Test Framework:** Jest or Vitest (if configured; currently not present)
+
+**If test framework exists:**
+- [ ] All unit tests for changed functions pass
+- [ ] Integration tests for affected features pass
+- [ ] Test coverage unchanged or improved
+- [ ] No skipped tests (marked .skip or .todo)
+
+**How to run:**
+```bash
+npm run test                    # Run all tests
+npm run test -- --coverage     # With coverage report
+npm run test -- <filename>     # Single file
+```
+
+**Current Status:** No test framework configured
+- **Action:** Setup Jest/Vitest if adding complex game logic
+- **Minimal requirement:** Manual test checklist (documented)
+
+**Report format (if tests exist):**
+```
+Gate 3: Tests
+Test Results:
+  Γ£ô 24 passed
+  Γ£ù 0 failed
+  Γùï 0 skipped
+Coverage: 78% lines, 71% branches
+Status: PASS
+```
+
+**Report format (if no tests):**
+```
+Gate 3: Tests
+Framework: Not configured
+Manual Testing: [checklist passed]
+Status: NOT APPLICABLE (manual testing completed)
+```
+
+## Gate 4: Linting & Build
+
+### Requirement: No lint errors; build/sync succeeds
+**Linting:** Currently not configured (ESLint could be added)
+
+**JavaScript Validation:**
+- [ ] No syntax errors in www/index.html
+- [ ] No undefined variables
+- [ ] No unused variables (code should be clean)
+- [ ] No console.log() left in production code
+
+**How to check manually:**
+```bash
+# 1. Open www/index.html in browser dev tools
+# 2. Check Console for errors/warnings
+# 3. Run basic syntax check
+node -c www/index.html  # (Won't work for HTML; use online validators)
+
+# Or use a linter if installed
+npx eslint www/index.html
+```
+
+**Build Verification:**
+- [ ] `npm install` succeeds without errors
+- [ ] `npx cap sync android` succeeds
+- [ ] No build warnings or errors
+
+**How to verify build:**
+```bash
+npm install
+npx cap sync android
+echo "Build sync successful" || echo "Build sync failed"
+```
+
+**Report format:**
+```
+Gate 4: Linting & Build
+Lint Status: No errors detected
+Build Status: PASS
+  Γ£ô npm install completed
+  Γ£ô npx cap sync android completed
+Status: PASS
+```
+
+## Gate 5: Capacitor & Android Build
+
+### Requirement: Capacitor sync succeeds; Android APK builds
+**Prerequisites:**
+- Node 20 installed
+- Java 17 installed (for local build)
+- GitHub Actions available (for CI build)
+
+**Local Build (if possible):**
+```bash
+npm install
+npx cap add android
+npx cap sync android
+cd android
+./gradlew assembleDebug
+```
+
+**Verify:**
+- [ ] `android/app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk` exists
+- [ ] APK file size reasonable (>5 MB typical)
+- [ ] No Gradle errors in build output
+
+**CI Build (via GitHub Actions):**
+- [ ] Push feature branch to GitHub
+- [ ] GitHub Actions workflow triggers
+- [ ] APK artifact available in Actions tab
+- [ ] APK download succeeds
+
+**How to verify:**
+```bash
+# After build
+ls -lh android/app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk
+
+# Check file is valid (unzip should work)
+unzip -t app-debug.apk > /dev/null && echo "APK valid" || echo "APK corrupted"
+```
+
+**Report format:**
+```
+Gate 5: Android Build
+Local Build: PASS
+  - Capacitor sync: Γ£ô succeeded
+  - Gradle build: Γ£ô succeeded (2m 34s)
+  - APK size: 6.2 MB
+  - APK valid: Γ£ô verified
+
+OR
+
+CI Build: PASS
+  - GitHub Actions run: Γ£ô completed
+  - APK artifact: Γ£ô available
+  - Download verified: Γ£ô successful
+Status: PASS
+```
+
+## Gate 6: Device Testing (APK Installation)
+
+### Requirement: APK installs and app launches on Android device
+**Prerequisites:**
+- Android device (Android 6.0+) or emulator
+- USB debugging enabled
+- `adb` (Android Debug Bridge) installed
+
+**Installation:**
+```bash
+adb devices  # Verify device detected
+adb install -r app-debug.apk
+adb shell am start -n com.nishaan.stylelab/.MainActivity
+```
+
+**Verification Checklist:**
+- [ ] APK installs without errors
+- [ ] App launches (splash screen appears)
+- [ ] Home screen loads (no white screen hang)
+- [ ] Navigation works (can open different screens)
+- [ ] Feature from current task works as expected
+- [ ] No crashes or force-closes
+- [ ] App responds to user input
+- [ ] Audio/sound effects work (if applicable)
+- [ ] Theme system works (can switch themes)
+
+**How to verify:**
+```bash
+# After installation, check app is running
+adb shell pm list packages | grep stylelab
+
+# Check for crashes in logs
+adb logcat | grep "stylelab\|crash\|error"
+
+# Install and launch
+adb install -r app-debug.apk
+adb shell am start -n com.nishaan.stylelab/.MainActivity
+
+# Wait 5 seconds, then check if app is still running
+sleep 5
+adb shell pgrep -f com.nishaan.stylelab && echo "App running" || echo "App crashed"
+```
+
+**Report format:**
+```
+Gate 6: Device Testing
+Device: Pixel 6a (Android 14)
+Installation: PASS Γ£ô
+App Launch: PASS Γ£ô (1.2s)
+Navigation: PASS Γ£ô
+Feature Test: PASS Γ£ô [feature name]
+Crashes: None
+Errors: None
+Status: PASS
+```
+
+**Failure resolution:**
+- White screen: Check www/index.html renders, no syntax errors
+- Crashes on startup: Check console errors, verify state initialization
+- Feature not working: Verify feature code in index.html, test in browser first
+- Audio not playing: Check S.sound is true, audio context available
+
+## Gate 7: No Critical or High-Severity Defects
+
+### Requirement: No blocking bugs remain
+**Severity Levels:**
+- **Critical:** App crashes, cannot use feature, data loss
+- **High:** Feature partially broken, major usability issue
+- **Medium:** Visual glitch, minor functionality missing
+- **Low:** Polish, rarely-used feature, non-blocking
+
+**Acceptance Policy:**
+- Γ¥î **FAIL:** If any Critical or High-severity defects remain
+- Γ£à **PASS:** If only Low/Medium or no defects exist
+- ΓÅ╕∩╕Å **BLOCKED:** If defect cannot be reproduced
+
+**How to identify:**
+```javascript
+// During testing, categorize issues:
+
+// CRITICAL (must fix)
+- App crashes on feature use
+- Feature completely non-functional
+- User data lost
+- Cannot navigate away
+
+// HIGH (should fix before launch)
+- Feature partially broken (some inputs fail)
+- Highscore doesn't save
+- Audio doesn't work on device
+- UI completely broken
+
+// MEDIUM (nice to fix)
+- Visual misalignment
+- Non-critical animation missing
+- Theme doesn't apply fully
+
+// LOW (future improvements)
+- Code could be cleaner
+- Performance could be better
+- Additional feature ideas
+```
+
+**Report format:**
+```
+Gate 7: Defect Review
+Critical Issues: 0
+High-Severity Issues: 0
+Medium Issues: 1 (visual glitch in theme X, low priority)
+Low Issues: 2 (code cleanup recommendations)
+Status: PASS (no blockers)
+```
+
+## Gate 8: Permissions & Privacy Compliance
+
+### Requirement: No unapproved permissions or data collection
+**Verification:**
+- [ ] No new permissions added to AndroidManifest.xml
+- [ ] No personal data collection (email, phone, ID)
+- [ ] No tracking libraries added (Google Analytics, Facebook SDK)
+- [ ] No external web navigation (all links stay in app)
+- [ ] No social features (no chat, leaderboards with names)
+- [ ] Ad requests respect child-directed settings
+- [ ] No camera/microphone requests
+- [ ] No location access
+
+**How to verify:**
+```bash
+# Check manifest for new permissions
+grep -n "uses-permission" android/app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml
+
+# Check for suspicious imports in index.html
+grep -i "google-analytics\|facebook\|tracking\|analytics" www/index.html
+
+# Check for external links
+grep -i "href=\"http\|window.open" www/index.html
+
+# Verify no new npm packages (check for tracking SDKs)
+npm list --depth=0
+```
+
+**Report format:**
+```
+Gate 8: Privacy & Compliance
+New Permissions: None Γ£ô
+Personal Data Collection: None Γ£ô
+Tracking Libraries: None Γ£ô
+External Links: None Γ£ô
+Approved Ad System: Γ£ô AdMob test IDs
+Status: PASS (COPPA-compliant)
+```
+
+## Gate 9: Changes Documentation
+
+### Requirement: Changes are documented
+**Required Documentation:**
+- [ ] GitHub commit message (clear, descriptive)
+- [ ] Comments in code (for complex logic)
+- [ ] Updated README.md (if architecture changed)
+- [ ] Known issues documented (if deferred work)
+
+**Commit Message Format:**
+```
+feat: add new game (game name)
+
+- Implemented [game logic]
+- Added to GAMES registry
+- Tested on device
+- All existing games verified working
+
+Acceptance criteria:
+- [ ] Feature complete
+- [ ] Regression tests pass
+- [ ] Device testing passes
+```
+
+**Report format:**
+```
+Gate 9: Documentation
+Commit Message: Γ£ô Clear and descriptive
+Code Comments: Γ£ô Complex logic documented
+README: Γùï Not applicable (no architecture change)
+Known Issues: None
+Status: PASS
+```
+
+## Gate 10: Git Status & Commit Tracking
+
+### Requirement: Working tree clean; commits on feature branch
+**Verification:**
+- [ ] All changes committed (no uncommitted files)
+- [ ] Branch is feature branch (not main/master)
+- [ ] Branch name descriptive (feature/..., bugfix/...)
+- [ ] Commits are atomic (one logical change per commit)
+- [ ] No merge conflicts
+
+**How to verify:**
+```bash
+# Check working tree
+git status  # Should be clean (nothing to commit)
+
+# Check branch
+git rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD  # Should NOT be main/master
+
+# Check commits
+git log --oneline --graph -5  # Review recent commits
+
+# Check for uncommitted changes
+git diff --stat  # Should be empty
+
+# Check for untracked files
+git ls-files --others --exclude-standard  # Should be empty
+```
+
+**Report format:**
+```
+Git Status & Commits
+Working Tree: PASS Γ£ô (clean)
+Branch: feature/new-game Γ£ô (descriptive)
+Commits: 3 logical changes Γ£ô
+  1. feat: add Simon game registry entry
+  2. feat: implement Simon game logic
+  3. test: verify Simon game works
+Conflicts: None Γ£ô
+Status: PASS
+```
+
+## Summary Report Template
+
+Use this format for final acceptance:
+
+```markdown
+# Task Acceptance Report
+
+**Task ID:** [task identifier]
+**Feature:** [feature name]
+**Branch:** feature/[feature-name]
+**Developer:** [agent/name]
+**Date:** [date]
+
+## Acceptance Gate Results
+
+| Gate | Criterion | Status | Notes |
+|------|-----------|--------|-------|
+| 1 | Feature Completeness | PASS Γ£ô | All requirements met |
+| 2 | Regression Testing | PASS Γ£ô | All 15 games working |
+| 3 | Unit Tests | NOT APPLICABLE | Manual testing completed |
+| 4 | Linting & Build | PASS Γ£ô | No errors |
+| 5 | Android Build | PASS Γ£ô | APK 6.2 MB, valid |
+| 6 | Device Testing | PASS Γ£ô | Installed on Pixel 6a |
+| 7 | Defect Review | PASS Γ£ô | No critical issues |
+| 8 | Privacy & Compliance | PASS Γ£ô | COPPA-compliant |
+| 9 | Documentation | PASS Γ£ô | Commits clear |
+| 10 | Git Status | PASS Γ£ô | Clean, on feature branch |
+
+## Final Status: Γ£à APPROVED FOR MERGE
+
+**Recommendation:** Merge to `develop` branch (ready for QA/review)
+
+## Known Issues / Deferred Work
+None
+
+## Testing Evidence
+- [APK artifact link]
+- [Device test video/screenshots]
+- [Console output]
+
+---
+```
+
+## Quality Gate Override Policy
+
+**When gates cannot be passed:**
+1. **Identify blocker** ΓÇö What specific gate fails?
+2. **Classify severity:**
+   - Environment issue (missing Java, no device) ΓåÆ BLOCKED
+   - Technical issue (build fails) ΓåÆ Fix or document reason
+   - Requirement issue (feature scope changed) ΓåÆ Discuss with lead
+3. **Document decision:**
+   - **BLOCKED:** Explain why, identify what's needed
+   - **DEFERRED:** List as known issue, plan fix timing
+   - **OVERRIDE:** Only with explicit lead approval, documented in report
+4. **Never force approval** ΓÇö An unmet gate means the task is incomplete
+
+**Re-check policy:** After any fix, re-run affected gates before claiming pass.

diff --git a/src/audio/context.js b/src/audio/context.js
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..dc45b44
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/audio/context.js
@@ -0,0 +1,105 @@
+/**
+ * Web Audio API Context Management
+ * Lazy initialization of audio context with proper browser fallbacks
+ */
+
+let ctx = null
+let master = null
+let fxBus = null
+
+/**
+ * Initialize or retrieve Web Audio context
+ * Handles browser compatibility and suspended states
+ * @param {number} volume - Master volume (0-1)
+ * @returns {AudioContext|null} Audio context or null if not supported
+ */
+export function getContext(volume = 0.7) {
+  if (!ctx) {
+    // Try to get audio context (different APIs for different browsers)
+    const AudioContextClass = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext
+
+    // No audio support
+    if (!AudioContextClass) {
+      return null
+    }
+
+    try {
+      ctx = new AudioContextClass()
+    } catch (e) {
+      return null
+    }
+
+    // Create master volume control
+    master = ctx.createGain()
+    master.connect(ctx.destination)
+
+    // Create delay/reverb effect chain
+    const delay = ctx.createDelay(1)
+    delay.delayTime.value = 0.17
+
+    const feedback = ctx.createGain()
+    feedback.gain.value = 0.38
+    delay.connect(feedback)
+    feedback.connect(delay)
+
+    const wet = ctx.createGain()
+    wet.gain.value = 0.5
+    delay.connect(wet)
+    wet.connect(master)
+
+    fxBus = delay
+  }
+
+  // Resume context if suspended (user interaction required)
+  if (ctx.state === 'suspended') {
+    ctx.resume()
+  }
+
+  // Update master volume
+  if (master) {
+    master.gain.value = Math.pow(volume, 1.5) * 0.9
+  }
+
+  return ctx
+}
+
+/**
+ * Get master gain node
+ * @returns {GainNode|null} Master gain or null
+ */
+export function getMaster() {
+  return master
+}
+
+/**
+ * Get effect bus (delay/reverb)
+ * @returns {DelayNode|null} Effect bus or null
+ */
+export function getEffectBus() {
+  return fxBus
+}
+
+/**
+ * Check if audio context is available
+ * @returns {boolean} Whether audio is supported
+ */
+export function isAudioSupported() {
+  return !!(window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)
+}
+
+/**
+ * Reset audio context (for testing/cleanup)
+ * Exposed for testing only
+ */
+export function resetContext() {
+  if (ctx) {
+    try {
+      ctx.close()
+    } catch (e) {
+      // Ignore close errors
+    }
+  }
+  ctx = null
+  master = null
+  fxBus = null
+}

diff --git a/src/audio/sfx.js b/src/audio/sfx.js
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..9e3a0ed

@@ -0,0 +1,131 @@
+/**
+ * Sound Effects Manager
+ * High-level API for playing game sound effects with theme support
+ */
+
+import { getContext, isAudioSupported } from './context.js'
+import { playTone, playNoise, playClick, playTick, playBend } from './synthesizer.js'
+import { getPreset } from './soundThemes.js'
+
+/**
+ * Play a sound effect
+ * @param {string} eventName - Event type (tap, correct, wrong, lesson, end, note, pop, theme)
+ * @param {number} arg - Optional argument (e.g., note index, victory flag)
+ * @param {Object} state - App state (S object with sound and theme settings)
+ */
+export function sfx(eventName, arg, state) {
+  // Check if sound is enabled
+  if (!state || !state.sound) {
+    return
+  }
+
+  // Get audio context
+  const ctx = getContext(state.vol || 0.7)
+  if (!ctx) {
+    return
+  }
+
+  // Get current theme preset
+  const preset = getPreset(state.theme || 'clay')
+  const p = preset.p || 1
+  const d = preset.d || 1
+
+  // Helper: play tone with preset multipliers
+  const playNote = (freq, startTime, duration, volume, targetFreq) => {
+    playTone(ctx, preset, freq * p, startTime, duration * d, volume, targetFreq && targetFreq * p)
+  }
+
+  try {
+    switch (eventName) {
+      // UI tap/click feedback
+      case 'tap':
+        if (preset.k === 'click') {
+          playNoise(ctx, 0, 0.03, 0.5 * preset.v, 2500)
+        } else if (preset.k === 'tick') {
+          playTick(preset)
+        } else if (preset.bend) {
+          playBend(preset, 330, 640, 0.12)
+        } else {
+          playNote(520, 0, 0.09, 0.3, 380)
+        }
+        break
+
+      // Correct answer
+      case 'correct':
+        playNote(523.25, 0, 0.2, 0.3)
+        playNote(783.99, 0.1, 0.28, 0.3)
+        playNote(1975, 0.2, 0.12, 0.12)
+        break
+
+      // Wrong answer
+      case 'wrong':
+        playNote(190, 0, 0.28, 0.28, 120)
+        break
+
+      // Lesson complete
+      case 'lesson':
+        [523.25, 659.25, 783.99].forEach((f, i) => {
+          playNote(f, i * 0.09, 0.22, 0.3)
+        })
+        playNote(1318.5, 0.3, 0.2, 0.2)
+        break
+
+      // Game end (victory/defeat)
+      case 'end':
+        if (arg === 2) {
+          // Victory fanfare
+          [523.25, 659.25, 783.99, 1046.5].forEach((f, i) => {
+            playNote(f, i * 0.11, 0.25, 0.3)
+          })
+          [523.25, 659.25, 783.99, 1046.5].forEach((f) => {
+            playNote(f, 0.55, 0.7, 0.16)
+          })
+        } else if (arg === 1) {
+          // Good/neutral end
+          [523.25, 659.25, 783.99].forEach((f, i) => {
+            playNote(f, i * 0.11, 0.3, 0.3)
+          })
+        } else {
+          // Defeat/sad
+          [392, 440, 523.25].forEach((f, i) => {
+            playNote(f, i * 0.13, 0.3, 0.25)
+          })
+        }
+        break
+
+      // Musical note (for Drum Kit, etc.)
+      case 'note':
+        // arg: 0=C4, 1=E4, 2=G4, 3=C5
+        const notes = [261.63, 329.63, 392, 523.25]
+        const freq = notes[arg] || notes[0]
+        playNote(freq, 0, 0.35, 0.35)
+        break
+
+      // Bubble pop
+      case 'pop':
+        playNoise(ctx, 0, 0.05, 0.4 * preset.v, 800)
+        playNote(900, 0, 0.08, 0.2, 300)
+        break
+
+      // Theme change confirmation
+      case 'theme':
+        playNote(220, 0, 0.3, 0.22, 1400)
+        playNoise(ctx, 0, 0.25, 0.1 * preset.v, 1200)
+        break
+
+      default:
+        // Unknown event - silent
+        break
+    }
+  } catch (e) {
+    // Silent fail - don't break game if audio errors
+  }
+}
+
+/**
+ * Check if audio is supported in this browser
+ * @returns {boolean}
+ */
+export function canPlayAudio() {
+  return isAudioSupported()
+}

+    v: 0.5,
+    bend: 1
+  }
+}
+
+/**
+ * Get preset by theme ID
+ * @param {string} themeId - Theme name (e.g., 'clay', 'cyber')
+ * @returns {Object} Sound preset or default clay
+ */
+export function getPreset(themeId) {
+  return SOUND_THEMES[themeId] || SOUND_THEMES.clay
+}
+
+/**
+ * List all available themes
+ * @returns {string[]} Array of theme IDs
+ */
+export function getThemeIds() {
+  return Object.keys(SOUND_THEMES)
+}
+
+/**
+ * Get all presets as map
+ * @returns {Object} All presets
+ */
+export function getAllPresets() {
+  return { ...SOUND_THEMES }
+}
diff --git a/src/audio/synthesizer.js b/src/audio/synthesizer.js
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..c8b5f42
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/audio/synthesizer.js
@@ -0,0 +1,137 @@
+/**
+ * Web Audio Synthesizer
+ * Tone generation and noise synthesis for sound effects
+ */
+
+import { getContext, getMaster, getEffectBus } from './context.js'
+
+/**
+ * Generate a musical tone
+ * @param {AudioContext} ctx - Audio context
+ * @param {Object} preset - Sound preset with waveform and properties
+ * @param {number} frequency - Base frequency in Hz
+ * @param {number} time - Start time (relative to current time)
+ * @param {number} duration - Duration in seconds
+ * @param {number} volume - Volume (0-1)
+ * @param {number} targetFreq - Optional end frequency for pitch bend
+ */
+export function playTone(ctx, preset, frequency, time, duration, volume, targetFreq) {
+  if (!ctx || !getMaster()) return
+
+  const osc = ctx.createOscillator()
+  const gain = ctx.createGain()
+  const nowTime = ctx.currentTime + time
+
+  // Set waveform type
+  osc.type = preset.w || 'sine'
+
+  // Set frequency
+  osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(frequency, nowTime)
+
+  // Apply frequency bend if target provided
+  if (targetFreq) {
+    osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(30, targetFreq), nowTime + duration)
+  }
+
+  // Envelope: attack, sustain, release
+  const volume_scaled = Math.max(0.0002, volume * (preset.v || 1))
+  gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, nowTime)
+  gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(volume_scaled, nowTime + 0.008)
+  gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, nowTime + duration)
+
+  // Connect to master (and effect bus if enabled)
+  osc.connect(gain)
+  gain.connect(getMaster())
+
+  if (preset.fx) {
+    gain.connect(getEffectBus())
+  }
+
+  // Play
+  osc.start(nowTime)
+  osc.stop(nowTime + duration + 0.05)
+}
+
+/**
+ * Generate noise (percussion/hit sound)
+ * @param {AudioContext} ctx - Audio context
+ * @param {number} time - Start time
+ * @param {number} duration - Duration in seconds
+ * @param {number} volume - Volume (0-1)
+ * @param {number} highpass - High-pass filter frequency (Hz)
+ */
+export function playNoise(ctx, time, duration, volume, highpass) {
+  if (!ctx || !getMaster()) return
+
+  // Create noise buffer
+  const bufferLen = Math.max(1, Math.floor(ctx.sampleRate * duration))
+  const buffer = ctx.createBuffer(1, bufferLen, ctx.sampleRate)
+  const data = buffer.getChannelData(0)
+
+  // Fill with white noise with fade-out envelope
+  for (let i = 0; i < bufferLen; i++) {
+    data[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * (1 - i / bufferLen)
+  }
+
+  // Create source and filter
+  const source = ctx.createBufferSource()
+  source.buffer = buffer
+
+  const filter = ctx.createBiquadFilter()
+  filter.type = 'highpass'
+  filter.frequency.value = highpass || 1000
+
+  const gain = ctx.createGain()
+  gain.gain.value = volume
+
+  // Connect chain
+  source.connect(filter)
+  filter.connect(gain)
+  gain.connect(getMaster())
+
+  // Play
+  const nowTime = ctx.currentTime + time
+  source.start(nowTime)
+}
+
+/**
+ * Play a simple click sound (UI feedback)
+ * @param {Object} preset - Sound preset
+ * @param {boolean} useNoise - Use noise (true) or tone (false)
+ */
+export function playClick(preset, useNoise = false) {
+  const ctx = getContext()
+  if (!ctx) return
+
+  if (useNoise) {
+    playNoise(ctx, 0, 0.03, 0.5 * (preset.v || 1), 2500)
+  } else {
+    playTone(ctx, preset, 1800, 0, 0.03, 0.1, null)
+  }
+}
+
+/**
+ * Play a tick sound (clock-like)
+ * @param {Object} preset - Sound preset
+ */
+export function playTick(preset) {
+  const ctx = getContext()
+  if (!ctx) return
+
+  playNoise(ctx, 0, 0.02, 0.45, 5000)
+  playTone(ctx, preset, 1800, 0, 0.03, 0.1, null)
+}
+
+/**
+ * Play a pitch-bent tone (slide/glide effect)
+ * @param {Object} preset - Sound preset
+ * @param {number} startFreq - Start frequency
+ * @param {number} endFreq - End frequency
+ * @param {number} duration - Duration in seconds
+ */
+export function playBend(preset, startFreq, endFreq, duration) {
+  const ctx = getContext()
+  if (!ctx) return
+
+  playTone(ctx, preset, startFreq, 0, duration, 0.3, endFreq)
+}

+/**
+ * Games Module Index - Central aggregator for all 15 games
+ * Each game is imported and exported for use in the application
+ */
+
+import { GAMES } from './registry.js';
+
+// Import all game modules
+import { simon } from './simon.js';
+import { balloon } from './balloon.js';
+import { scramble } from './scramble.js';
+import { hangman } from './hangman.js';
+import { mole } from './mole.js';
+import { odd } from './odd.js';
+import { ttt2 } from './ttt2.js';
+import { connect4 } from './connect4.js';
+import { tug } from './tug.js';
+import { react } from './react.js';
+import { mathduel } from './mathduel.js';
+import { rps2 } from './rps2.js';
+import { snake } from './snake.js';
+import { farm } from './farm.js';
+import { colormatch } from './colormatch.js';
+
+// Map of game ID to game function
+const gameMap = {
+  simon,
+  balloon,
+  scramble,
+  hangman,
+  mole,
+  odd,
+  ttt2,
+  connect4,
+  tug,
+  react,
+  mathduel,
+  rps2,
+  snake,
+  farm,
+  colormatch
+};
+
+/**
+ * Get a game function by ID
+ */
+export function getGame(gameId) {
+  return gameMap[gameId];
+}
+
+/**
+ * Start a game - renders game UI into element
+ */
+export function startGame(gameId, element) {
+  const gameFn = gameMap[gameId];
+  if (!gameFn) {
+    console.error(`Game not found: ${gameId}`);
+    return null;
+  }
+  return gameFn(element);
+}
+
+/**
+ * Export all games for potential direct access
+ */
+export {
+  simon,
+  balloon,
+  scramble,
+  hangman,
+  mole,
+  odd,
+  ttt2,
+  connect4,
+  tug,
+  react,
+  mathduel,
+  rps2,
+  snake,
+  farm,

+};

diff --git a/src/games/utils.js b/src/games/utils.js
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..848e6a4
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/games/utils.js
@@ -0,0 +1,102 @@
+/**
+ * Game Utilities - Shared helpers for all games
+ * Maintains compatibility with original global state model
+ */
+
+// These will be injected from main.js
+export let globalState = null;
+export let globalSfx = null;
+export let globalGo = null;
+
+/**
+ * Set the global state reference (called from main.js)
+ */
+export function setGameContext(state, sfxFn, goFn) {
+  globalState = state;
+  globalSfx = sfxFn;
+  globalGo = goFn;
+}
+
+/**
+ * Get best score for a game
+ */
+export function gb(gameId) {
+  return globalState?.gb?.[gameId] || 0;
+}
+
+/**
+ * Set best score if this is a new best
+ */
+export function gbest(gameId, score) {
+  if (!globalState) return false;
+  const current = globalState.gb?.[gameId] || 0;
+  if (score > current) {
+    if (!globalState.gb) globalState.gb = {};
+    globalState.gb[gameId] = score;
+    return true;
+  }
+  return false;
+}
+
+/**
+ * Add points/stars to global game count
+ */
+export function gadd(points) {
+  if (globalState) {
+    globalState.stars = (globalState.stars || 0) + points;
+  }
+}
+
+/**
+ * Play sound effect
+ */
+export function sfx(eventName, arg) {
+  if (globalSfx) {
+    globalSfx(eventName, arg);
+  }
+}
+
+/**
+ * Navigate to a route
+ */
+export function go(path) {
+  if (globalGo) {
+    globalGo(path);
+  }
+}
+
+/**
+ * Shuffle array in-place
+ */
+export function shuffle(arr) {
+  for (let i = arr.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
+    const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
+    [arr[i], arr[j]] = [arr[j], arr[i]];
+  }
+  return arr;
+}
+
+/**
+ * Random integer 0 to n-1
+ */
+export function rand(n) {
+  return Math.floor(Math.random() * n);
+}
+
+/**
+ * Create chip display (stats bar)
+ */
+export function chips(items) {
+  return `<div class="row2">${items.map(x => `<span class="star"${x[1] ? ` id="${x[1]}"` : ''}>${x[0]}</span>`).join('')}</div>`;
+}
+
+/**
+ * Display end game screen
+ */
+export function endScreen(el, gameId, score, unit, onAgain) {
+  const nb = score > 0 && gbest(gameId, score);
+  gadd(score * 2);
+  sfx('end', score > 0 ? 1 : 0);
+  el.innerHTML = `<div class="trophy">${nb ? '≡ƒÅå' : '≡ƒÿó'}</div><h2>${score} ${unit}</h2><p style="color:var(--mute)">${nb ? 'New best score!' : 'Best: ' + gb(gameId)}</p><p><span class="star" style="display:inline-block">+${score * 2} Γ¡É</span></p><button class="cta" id="again">Play again</button>`;
+  el.querySelector('#again').onclick = () => { sfx('tap'); onAgain() };
