+/**
+ * Legacy Games Module
+ * Contains all 15 original game functions extracted from the original monolithic app
+ * This module preserves exact original behavior for modularization
+ * 
+ * Games are wrapped by ES module wrappers in src/games/
+ */
+
+// ===== GLOBAL HELPERS (provided by wrappers) =====
+// These are injected by the wrapper modules
+let globalState = null;
+let globalSfx = null;
+
+export function setGameContext(state, sfxFn) {
+  globalState = state;
+  globalSfx = sfxFn;
+}
+
+// Game helper functions
+const chips = (a) => `<div class="row2">${a.map(x => `<span class="star"${x[1] ? ` id="${x[1]}"` : ''}>${x[0]}</span>`).join('')}</div>`;
+const gbest = (id, v) => { if (!globalState) return false; globalState.gb = globalState.gb || {}; const o = globalState.gb[id] || 0; if (v > o) { globalState.gb[id] = v; return true; } return false; };
+const gb = (id) => globalState?.gb?.[id] || 0;
+const gadd = (n) => { if (globalState) { globalState.stars = (globalState.stars || 0) + n; } };
+const sfx = (ev, arg) => { if (globalSfx) globalSfx(ev, arg); };
+const shuffle = (a) => a.map(v => [Math.random(), v]).sort((x, y) => x[0] - y[0]).map(x => x[1]);
+const rand = (n) => Math.floor(Math.random() * n);
+const endScreen = (el, gameId, score, unit, onAgain) => {
+  const nb = score > 0 && gbest(gameId, score);
+  gadd(score * 2);
+  sfx('end', score > 0 ? 1 : 0);
+  el.innerHTML = `<div class="trophy">${nb ? '≡ƒÅå' : '≡ƒÿó'}</div><h2>${score} ${unit}</h2><p style="color:var(--mute)">${nb ? 'New best score!' : 'Best: ' + gb(gameId)}</p><p><span class="star" style="display:inline-block">+${score * 2} Γ¡É</span></p><button class="cta" id="again">Play again</button>`;
+  el.querySelector('#again').onclick = () => { sfx('tap'); onAgain() };
+};
+
+// ===== DATA CONSTANTS =====
+const WORDS = [['CAT', '≡ƒÉ▒'], ['DOG', '≡ƒÉ╢'], ['FISH', '≡ƒÉá'], ['LION', '≡ƒªü'], ['APPLE', '≡ƒìÄ'], ['MANGO', '≡ƒÑ¡'], ['TIGER', '≡ƒÉ»'], ['ZEBRA', '≡ƒªô'], ['HORSE', '≡ƒÉ┤'], ['BANANA', '≡ƒìî'], ['MOON', '≡ƒîÖ'], ['STAR', 'Γ¡É'], ['TREE', '≡ƒî│'], ['BIRD', '≡ƒÉª'], ['FROG', '≡ƒÉ╕'], ['DUCK', '≡ƒªå'], ['CAKE', '≡ƒì░'], ['BOOK', '≡ƒôû'], ['SHIP', '≡ƒÜó'], ['SUN', 'ΓÿÇ∩╕Å']];
+const ANIMALS = [['ELEPHANT', '≡ƒÉÿ'], ['GIRAFFE', '≡ƒªü'], ['MONKEY', '≡ƒÉ╡'], ['PANDA', '≡ƒÉ╝'], ['ZEBRA', '≡ƒªô'], ['KANGAROO', '≡ƒªÿ'], ['PENGUIN', '≡ƒÉº'], ['DOLPHIN', '≡ƒÉ¼'], ['TIGER', '≡ƒÉ»'], ['RABBIT', '≡ƒÉ░'], ['CAMEL', '≡ƒÉ½'], ['OWL', '≡ƒªë']];
+const GRP = [['≡ƒìÄ', '≡ƒìî', '≡ƒìè', '≡ƒìï', '≡ƒìî', '≡ƒìæ'], ['≡ƒÉ╢', '≡ƒÉ▒', '≡ƒÉ¡', '≡ƒÉ╣', '≡ƒÉ░', '≡ƒªè'], ['≡ƒÜù', '≡ƒÜò', '≡ƒÜÖ', '≡ƒÜî', '≡ƒÜÄ', '≡ƒÅÄ∩╕Å'], ['≡ƒÿÇ', '≡ƒÿâ', '≡ƒÿä', '≡ƒÿü', '≡ƒÿå', '≡ƒÿé'], ['Γ¡É', 'Γ£¿', '≡ƒÆ½', '≡ƒîƒ', 'Γ£¬', '≡ƒöå'], ['≡ƒÉá', '≡ƒÉƒ', '≡ƒÉí', '≡ƒªê', '≡ƒÉÖ', '≡ƒªæ'], ['≡ƒìò', '≡ƒìö', '≡ƒìƒ', '≡ƒî¡', '≡ƒÑ¬', '≡ƒî«'], ['≡ƒÉª', '≡ƒªà', '≡ƒªå', '≡ƒªë', '≡ƒªç', '≡ƒòè∩╕Å'], ['≡ƒÜÇ', 'Γ£ê∩╕Å', '≡ƒ¢⌐∩╕Å', '≡ƒÜü', '≡ƒ¢½', '≡ƒ¢¼'], ['ΓÿÇ∩╕Å', '≡ƒîñ∩╕Å', 'Γ¢à', '≡ƒîÑ∩╕Å', 'Γÿü∩╕Å', 'Γ¢ê∩╕Å']];
+const HARD = [[0, 6], [1, 5], [2, 7], [3, 0], [7, 1]];
+const WL = [[0, 1, 2], [3, 4, 5], [6, 7, 8], [0, 3, 6], [1, 4, 7], [2, 5, 8], [0, 4, 8], [2, 4, 6]];

+export function simon(el) {
+  const cols = ['#ef4444', '#3b82f6', '#22c55e', '#facc15'];
+  let seq = [], pos = 0, busy = true, tm = [];
+  el.innerHTML = chips([['Round 0', 'sr'], ['Best ' + gb('simon')]]) + `<div class="pads">${cols.map((c, i) => `<button class="pad" data-i="${i}" style="background:${c}" aria-label="Pad ${i + 1}"></button>`).join('')}</div><p class="gmsg" id="sm">Press start</p><button class="cta" id="ss">Start</button>`;
+  const pads = [...el.querySelectorAll('.pad')], M = el.querySelector('#sm'), B = el.querySelector('#ss');
+  const later = (f, ms) => { tm.push(setTimeout(f, ms)) };
+  const flash = i => { pads[i].classList.add('lit'); sfx('note', i); later(() => pads[i].classList.remove('lit'), 300) };
+  function play() { busy = true; pos = 0; M.textContent = 'WatchΓÇª'; seq.forEach((n, k) => later(() => flash(n), 600 + k * 600)); later(() => { busy = false; M.textContent = 'Your turn!' }, 600 + seq.length * 600) }
+  function round() { seq.push(Math.floor(Math.random() * 4)); el.querySelector('#sr').textContent = 'Round ' + seq.length; play() }
+  pads.forEach(p => p.onclick = () => { if (busy) return; const i = +p.dataset.i; flash(i); if (i !== seq[pos]) { busy = true; sfx('wrong'); const r = seq.length - 1, nb = r > 0 && gbest('simon', r); gadd(r * 2); M.textContent = 'Oops! You reached round ' + Math.max(r, 0) + (nb ? ' - new best!' : ''); B.textContent = 'Try again'; B.style.display = ''; seq = []; return } pos++; if (pos === seq.length) { gadd(2); busy = true; later(() => { sfx('correct'); later(round, 500) }, 350) } });
+  B.onclick = () => { sfx('tap'); B.style.display = 'none'; seq = []; round() };
+  return () => tm.forEach(clearTimeout);
+}

+export function balloon(el) {
+  let score, lives, t, tgt, run, iv = [];
+  const cl = ['#ef4444', '#3b82f6', '#22c55e', '#f59e0b', '#a855f7', '#ec4899'];
+  const stop = () => { run = false; iv.forEach(clearInterval); iv = [] };
+  function start() {
+    score = 0; lives = 3; t = 30; run = true;
+    el.innerHTML = chips([['ΓÅ▒ 30s', 'bt'], ['Score 0', 'bs'], ['Γ¥ñ∩╕ÅΓ¥ñ∩╕ÅΓ¥ñ∩╕Å', 'bl']]) + `<div class="gmsg" id="bq" style="font-size:26px"></div><div class="arena" id="ar"></div>`;
+    const ar = el.querySelector('#ar'), q = el.querySelector('#bq');
+    const ui = () => { el.querySelector('#bt').textContent = 'ΓÅ▒ ' + t + 's'; el.querySelector('#bs').textContent = 'Score ' + score; el.querySelector('#bl').textContent = 'Γ¥ñ∩╕Å'.repeat(Math.max(0, lives)) || '≡ƒÆö' };
+    const newQ = () => { const a = 1 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 9), b = 1 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 9); tgt = a + b; q.textContent = 'Pop ' + a + ' + ' + b };
+    const fin = () => { stop(); endScreen(el, 'balloon', score, 'popped', start) };
+    function spawn() {
+      const b = document.createElement('button'), v = Math.random() < .4 ? tgt : 2 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 17);
+      b.className = 'balloon'; b.textContent = v; b.style.left = Math.random() * Math.max(10, ar.clientWidth - 60) + 'px'; b.style.background = cl[Math.floor(Math.random() * 6)]; b.style.setProperty('--dur', (4 + Math.random() * 2) + 's');
+      b.onanimationend = () => b.remove();
+      b.onclick = () => { if (!run) return; b.remove(); if (v === tgt) { score++; sfx('pop'); newQ() } else { lives--; sfx('wrong') } ui(); if (lives <= 0) fin() };
+      ar.appendChild(b);
+    }
+    newQ(); ui();
+    iv.push(setInterval(() => { if (run) spawn() }, 750));
+    iv.push(setInterval(() => { t--; ui(); if (t <= 0) fin() }, 1000));
+  }
+  start();
+  return stop;
+}

+export function scramble(el) {
+  let round, score, words, pool, ans, w, tm = [];
+  function start() { round = 0; score = 0; words = shuffle([...WORDS]).slice(0, 5); next() }
+  function next() { if (round >= 5) return endScreen(el, 'scramble', score, 'correct', start); w = words[round]; let L; do { L = shuffle(w[0].split('')) } while (L.join('') === w[0] && w[0].length > 2); pool = L.map(c => ({ c, used: false })); ans = []; draw() }
+  function draw() {
+    el.innerHTML = chips([['Word ' + (round + 1) + '/5'], ['Score ' + score]]) + `<div style="font-size:72px;filter:drop-shadow(3px 5px 4px rgba(0,0,0,.2))">${w[1]}</div><div class="slots">${w[0].split('').map((_,k) => `<button class="slot" data-k="${k}" aria-label="Letter ${k+1}">${ans[k] ? ans[k].c : ''}</button>`).join('')}</div><div class="slots">${pool.map((p,i) => `<button class="tileL" data-i="${i}" ${p.used ? 'disabled style="opacity:.3"' : ''}>${p.c}</button>`).join('')}</div><button class="cta alt" id="sk">Skip</button>`;
+    el.querySelectorAll('.slot').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { ans.splice(+b.dataset.k).forEach(x => x.used = false); sfx('tap'); draw() });
+    el.querySelectorAll('.tileL').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { const p = pool[+b.dataset.i]; if (p.used) return; p.used = true; ans.push(p); sfx('tap'); if (ans.length === w[0].length) { if (ans.map(x => x.c).join('') === w[0]) { score++; sfx('correct'); draw(); tm.push(setTimeout(() => { round++; next() }, 700)) } else { sfx('wrong'); pool.forEach(x => x.used = false); ans = []; draw() } } else draw() });
+    el.querySelector('#sk').onclick = () => { sfx('tap'); round++; next() }
+  }
+  start();
+  return () => tm.forEach(clearTimeout);
+}

+export function hangman(el) {
+  let round, score, list, w, got, lives;
+  function start() { round = 0; score = 0; list = shuffle([...ANIMALS]).slice(0, 5); next() }
+  function next() { if (round >= 5) return endScreen(el, 'hangman', score, 'guessed', start); w = list[round]; got = new Set(); lives = 6; draw() }
+  function draw() {
+    const word = w[0], won = [...word].every(c => got.has(c)), lost = lives <= 0, done = won || lost;
+    el.innerHTML = chips([['Animal ' + (round + 1) + '/5'], ['Score ' + score]]) + `<div style="font-size:64px;margin:4px 0">${done ? w[1] : '≡ƒñö'}</div><div class="word">${[...word].map(c => got.has(c) || lost ? c : '_').join(' ')}</div><div style="font-size:22px;margin-bottom:12px">${'Γ¥ñ∩╕Å'.repeat(Math.max(0, lives)) || '≡ƒÆö'}</div>` + (done ? `<p class="gmsg">${won ? 'Great job!' : 'It was ' + word}</p><button class="cta" id="nx">Next</button>` : `<div class="keys">${[...'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ'].map(c => `<button class="key" data-c="${c}" ${got.has(c) ? 'disabled' : ''}>${c}</button>`).join('')}</div>`);
+    el.querySelectorAll('.key').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { const c = b.dataset.c; got.add(c); if (!word.includes(c)) { lives--; sfx('wrong') } else sfx('tap'); if ([...word].every(x => got.has(x))) { score++; sfx('correct') } draw() });
+    const nx = el.querySelector('#nx');
+    if (nx) nx.onclick = () => { sfx('tap'); round++; next() }
+  }
+  start();
+}

+export function mole(el) {
+  let score, t, iv = [], tm = [], run;
+  const stop = () => { run = false; iv.forEach(clearInterval); tm.forEach(clearTimeout); iv = []; tm = [] };
+  function start() {
+    score = 0; t = 30; run = true;
+    el.innerHTML = chips([['ΓÅ▒ 30s', 'mt'], ['Score 0', 'ms']]) + `<div class="mgrid">${Array.from({length:9},(_,i) => `<button class="hole" data-i="${i}" aria-label="Hole ${i+1}"><span></span></button>`).join('')}</div><p class="gmsg">≡ƒºú +1 &nbsp; ≡ƒÆú -2</p>`;
+    const holes = [...el.querySelectorAll('.hole')], st = holes.map(() => null);
+    const ui = () => { el.querySelector('#mt').textContent = 'ΓÅ▒ ' + t + 's'; el.querySelector('#ms').textContent = 'Score ' + score };
+    holes.forEach((h, i) => h.onclick = () => { const s = st[i]; if (!run || !s) return; st[i] = null; h.classList.remove('up'); if (s === 'm') { score++; sfx('pop') } else { score = Math.max(0, score - 2); sfx('wrong') } ui() });
+    iv.push(setInterval(() => { if (!run) return; const i = Math.floor(Math.random() * 9); if (st[i]) return; const bomb = Math.random() < .2; st[i] = bomb ? 'b' : 'm'; holes[i].firstChild.textContent = bomb ? '≡ƒÆú' : '≡ƒºú'; holes[i].classList.add('up'); tm.push(setTimeout(() => { st[i] = null; holes[i].classList.remove('up') }, 800)) }, 650));
+    iv.push(setInterval(() => { t--; ui(); if (t <= 0) { stop(); endScreen(el, 'mole', score, 'points', start) } }, 1000));
+  }
+  start();
+  return stop;
+}

+export function odd(el) {
+  let r, score, tm = [];
+  function start() { r = 0; score = 0; next() }
+  function next() {
+    if (r >= 8) return endScreen(el, 'odd', score, 'correct', start);
+    const hard = r >= 4, n = hard ? 6 : 4;
+    let a, b;
+    if (hard) { [a, b] = HARD[Math.floor(Math.random() * HARD.length)] } else { a = Math.floor(Math.random() * GRP.length); do { b = Math.floor(Math.random() * GRP.length) } while (b === a) }
+    const oddE = shuffle([...GRP[b]])[0], opts = shuffle(shuffle([...GRP[a]]).slice(0, n - 1).concat(oddE));
+    el.innerHTML = chips([['Round ' + (r + 1) + '/8'], ['Score ' + score], [hard ? 'Level 2' : 'Level 1']]) + `<p class="gmsg">Which one is different?</p><div class="oo ${hard ? 'six' : ''}">${opts.map(e => `<button data-e="${e}">${e}</button>`).join('')}</div>`;
+    const bs = [...el.querySelectorAll('.oo button')];
+    bs.forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { const ok = b.dataset.e === oddE; bs.forEach(x => { x.disabled = true; if (x.dataset.e === oddE) x.classList.add('right') }); if (ok) { score++; sfx('correct') } else { b.classList.add('wrong'); sfx('wrong') } tm.push(setTimeout(() => { r++; next() }, 900)) })
+  }
+  start();
+  return () => tm.forEach(clearTimeout);
+}

+export function ttt2(el) {
+  let b, turn, over, sc = { X: 0, O: 0, D: 0 };
+  const em = t => t === 'X' ? 'Γ¡ò' : 'Γ¥î';
+  function start() { b = Array(9).fill(null); turn = 'X'; over = false; draw() }
+  function draw(msg) {
+    el.innerHTML = chips([['Γ¡ò ' + sc.X], ['Γ¥î ' + sc.O], ['≡ƒñ¥ ' + sc.D]]) + `<p class="gmsg">${msg || 'Turn: ' + em(turn)}</p><div class="cells" style="grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);max-width:320px;margin:0 auto 14px"></div>${over ? '<button class="cta" id="ag">Play again</button>' : ''}`;
+    const g = el.querySelector('.cells');
+    b.forEach((v, i) => { const c = document.createElement('button'); c.className = 'cell'; c.textContent = v ? em(v) : ''; c.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Square ' + (i + 1)); c.onclick = () => play(i); g.appendChild(c) });
+    const a = el.querySelector('#ag');
+    if (a) a.onclick = () => { sfx('tap'); start() }
+  }
+  function play(i) {
+    if (over || b[i]) return;
+    b[i] = turn;
+    sfx('tap');
+    if (WL.some(l => l.every(k => b[k] === turn))) { over = true; sc[turn]++; gadd(5); sfx('end', 2); return draw(em(turn) + ' wins!') }
+    if (b.every(Boolean)) { over = true; sc.D++; sfx('end', 0); return draw('Draw!') }
+    turn = turn === 'X' ? 'O' : 'X';
+    draw()
+  }
+  start();
+}

+export function connect4(el) {
+  const R = 6, C = 7, col = ['#ef4444', '#facc15'];
+  let g, turn, over, sc = [0, 0];
+  function start() { g = Array.from({length:R}, () => Array(C).fill(-1)); turn = 0; over = false; draw() }
+  function win(r, c) { const p = g[r][c]; for (const [dr, dc] of [[0, 1], [1, 0], [1, 1], [1, -1]]) { let n = 1; for (const s of [1, -1]) { let rr = r + dr * s, cc = c + dc * s; while (rr >= 0 && rr < R && cc >= 0 && cc < C && g[rr][cc] === p) { n++; rr += dr * s; cc += dc * s } } if (n >= 4) return true } return false }
+  function drop(c) { if (over) return; let r = R - 1; while (r >= 0 && g[r][c] !== -1) r--; if (r < 0) { sfx('wrong'); return } g[r][c] = turn; sfx('tap'); if (win(r, c)) { over = true; sc[turn]++; gadd(5); sfx('end', 2); return draw((turn ? '≡ƒö┤' : '≡ƒƒí') + ' wins!') } if (g[0].every(x => x !== -1)) { over = true; sfx('end', 0); return draw('Draw!') } turn = 1 - turn; draw() }
+  function draw(msg) {
+    el.innerHTML = chips([['≡ƒƒí ' + sc[0]], ['≡ƒö┤ ' + sc[1]]]) + `<p class="gmsg">${msg || 'Turn: ' + (turn ? '≡ƒö┤' : '≡ƒƒí')}</p><div class="c4grid">${g.map(row => row.map((v, c) => `<button class="c4" data-c="${c}" aria-label="Column ${c+1}" style="background:${v < 0 ? '#eef0ff' : col[v]}"></button>`).join('')).join('')}</div>${over ? '<button class="cta" id="ag">Play again</button>' : ''}`;
+    el.querySelectorAll('.c4').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => drop(+b.dataset.c));
+    const a = el.querySelector('#ag');
+    if (a) a.onclick = () => { sfx('tap'); start() }
+  }
+  start();
+}

+export function tug(el) {
+  let pos, run, tm = [];
+  function start() {
+    pos = 50; run = false;
+    el.innerHTML = `<div class="duel"><div class="half p2" style="--pc:#ef4444"><div class="who">Player 2</div><button class="bigbtn" id="b2">TAP FAST!</button></div><div class="mid" id="md">Get readyΓÇª</div><div class="track" id="tr"></div><div class="half" style="--pc:#3b82f6"><div class="who">Player 1</div><button class="bigbtn" id="b1">TAP FAST!</button></div></div>`;
+    const md = el.querySelector('#md'), tr = el.querySelector('#tr');
+    const paint = () => { tr.style.background = `linear-gradient(90deg,#3b82f6 ${pos}%,#ef4444 ${pos}%)` };
+    paint();
+    const pull = d => { if (!run) return; pos = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, pos + d)); paint(); if (pos <= 0 || pos >= 100) { run = false; const w = pos >= 100 ? 1 : 2; sfx('end', 2); gadd(5); md.textContent = '≡ƒÅå Player ' + w + ' wins!'; el.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', '<button class="cta" id="ag">Play again</button>'); el.querySelector('#ag').onclick = () => { sfx('tap'); start() } } };
+    ['1', '2'].forEach(k => el.querySelector('#b' + k).addEventListener('pointerdown', e => { e.preventDefault(); pull(k === '1' ? 2.2 : -2.2) }));
+    ['3', '2', '1', 'GO!'].forEach((t, i) => tm.push(setTimeout(() => { md.textContent = t; sfx(i === 3 ? 'correct' : 'tap'); if (i === 3) run = true }, i * 800)));
+  }
+  start();
+  return () => tm.forEach(clearTimeout);
+}

+export function react(el) {
+  let s, state, tm = [];
+  const later = (f, ms) => { tm.push(setTimeout(f, ms)) };
+  function start() {
+    s = [0, 0]; state = 'idle';
+    el.innerHTML = `<div class="duel"><div class="half p2" style="--pc:#ef4444"><div class="who">Player 2</div><button class="bigbtn pad2" data-p="1">TAP</button></div><div class="mid" id="md"></div><div class="half" style="--pc:#3b82f6"><div class="who">Player 1</div><button class="bigbtn pad2" data-p="0">TAP</button></div></div>`;
+    const md = el.querySelector('#md'), pads = [...el.querySelectorAll('.pad2')];
+    const paint = c => pads.forEach(x => x.style.background = c), score = () => 'P1 ' + s[0] + ' ΓÇô ' + s[1] + ' P2';
+    function round() { state = 'wait'; paint('#ef4444'); md.textContent = 'Wait for GREENΓÇª ' + score(); later(() => { if (state !== 'wait') return; state = 'go'; paint('#22c55e'); md.textContent = 'GO!'; sfx('note', 3) }, 1500 + Math.random() * 2500) }
+    function point(p, why) { state = 'over'; s[p]++; sfx('correct'); md.textContent = 'P' + (p + 1) + ' scores! ' + why + '  ' + score(); if (s[p] >= 3) later(() => { md.textContent = '≡ƒÅå Player ' + (p + 1) + ' wins!'; gadd(5); sfx('end', 2); el.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', '<button class="cta" id="ag">Play again</button>'); el.querySelector('#ag').onclick = () => { sfx('tap'); start() } }, 900); else later(round, 1500) }
+    pads.forEach(pd => pd.addEventListener('pointerdown', e => { e.preventDefault(); const p = +pd.dataset.p; if (state === 'wait') point(1 - p, '(P' + (p + 1) + ' tapped too early)'); else if (state === 'go') point(p, 'Fast!') }));
+    md.textContent = 'Get readyΓÇª first to 3 wins';
+    later(round, 1200)
+  }
+  start();
+  return () => tm.forEach(clearTimeout);
+}

+export function mathduel(el) {
+  let s, ans, lock, done, tm = [];
+  function start() { s = [0, 0]; q() }
+  function q() {
+    const a = 2 + rand(10), b = 2 + rand(10), op = ['+', 'ΓêÆ', '├ù'][rand(3)];
+    ans = op === '+' ? a + b : op === 'ΓêÆ' ? a - b : a * b;
+    const set = new Set([ans]);
+    while (set.size < 4) set.add(Math.max(0, ans + rand(11) - 5));
+    lock = [false, false];
+    done = false;
+    const half = (p, cls, c) => `<div class="half ${cls}" style="--pc:${c}"><div class="who">Player ${p+1}</div><div class="qbig">${a} ${op} ${b} = ?</div><div class="opts2">${shuffle([...set]).map(v => `<button class="mb2" data-p="${p}" data-v="${v}">${v}</button>`).join('')}</div></div>`;
+    el.innerHTML = `<div class="duel">${half(1, 'p2', '#ef4444')}<div class="mid">P1 ${s[0]} ΓÇô ${s[1]} P2 (first to 5)</div>${half(0, '', '#3b82f6')}</div>`;
+    el.querySelectorAll('.mb2').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { const p = +b.dataset.p; if (done || lock[p]) return; if (+b.dataset.v === ans) { done = true; s[p]++; sfx('correct'); b.style.background = 'var(--okbg)'; tm.push(setTimeout(() => s[p] >= 5 ? end(p) : q(), 900)) } else { lock[p] = true; sfx('wrong'); b.style.background = 'var(--badbg)'; b.disabled = true; if (lock[0] && lock[1]) { done = true; tm.push(setTimeout(q, 800)) } } })
+  }
+  function end(p) { gadd(5); sfx('end', 2); el.innerHTML = `<div class="trophy">≡ƒÅå</div><h2>Player ${p+1} wins!</h2><p style="color:var(--mute)">P1 ${s[0]} ΓÇô ${s[1]} P2</p><button class="cta" id="ag">Play again</button>`; el.querySelector('#ag').onclick = () => { sfx('tap'); start() } }
+  start();
+  return () => tm.forEach(clearTimeout);
+}
+
+export function rps2(el) {
+  const E = ['Γ£î∩╕Å', 'Γ£ï', 'Γ£è'];
+  let s, ch, lock, tm = [];
+  function start() { s = [0, 0]; ch = [null, null]; lock = false; draw('Pick one!') }
+  function side(p, cls, c) { const x = ch[p]; return `<div class="half ${cls}" style="--pc:${c}"><div class="who">Player ${p+1}</div>${lock ? `<div class="qbig">${E[x]}</div>` : x != null ? '<div class="qbig">Γ£ô Ready</div>' : `<div class="opts3">${E.map((e, i) => `<button class="mb2" data-p="${p}" data-i="${i}">${e}</button>`).join('')}</div>`}</div>` }
+  function draw(msg) { el.innerHTML = `<div class="duel">${side(1, 'p2', '#ef4444')}<div class="mid">${msg}<br>P1 ${s[0]} ΓÇô ${s[1]} P2 (first to 3)</div>${side(0, '', '#3b82f6')}</div>`; el.querySelectorAll('.mb2').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => pick(+b.dataset.p, +b.dataset.i)) }
+  function pick(p, i) { if (lock || ch[p] != null) return; ch[p] = i; sfx('tap'); if (ch[0] != null && ch[1] != null) { lock = true; const r = (ch[0] - ch[1] + 3) % 3, w = r === 1 ? 0 : r === 2 ? 1 : -1; if (w >= 0) s[w]++; sfx(w < 0 ? 'tap' : 'correct'); draw(E[ch[0]] + ' vs ' + E[ch[1]] + ' ΓÇô ' + (w < 0 ? 'Draw!' : 'Player ' + (w + 1) + ' wins the round!')); tm.push(setTimeout(() => { if (s[0] >= 3 || s[1] >= 3) { const f = s[0] >= 3 ? 0 : 1; gadd(5); sfx('end', 2); el.innerHTML = `<div class="trophy">≡ƒÅå</div><h2>Player ${f+1} wins!</h2><p style="color:var(--mute)">P1 ${s[0]} ΓÇô ${s[1]} P2</p><button class="cta" id="ag">Play again</button>`; el.querySelector('#ag').onclick = () => { sfx('tap'); start() } } else { ch = [null, null]; lock = false; draw('Pick one!') } }, 1900)) } else draw('Pick one!') }
+  start();
+  return () => tm.forEach(clearTimeout);
+}

+export function snake(el) {
+  const N = 15, Z = 20, V = { u: [0, -1], d: [0, 1], l: [-1, 0], r: [1, 0] };
+  let sn, dir, nd, food, score, iv;
+  const stop = () => clearInterval(iv);
+  const steer = k => { const v = V[k]; if (v && dir && !(v[0] === -dir[0] && v[1] === -dir[1])) nd = v };
+  const place = () => { do { food = [rand(N), rand(N)] } while (sn.some(s => s[0] === food[0] && s[1] === food[1])) };
+  const kd = e => { const m = { ArrowUp: 'u', ArrowDown: 'd', ArrowLeft: 'l', ArrowRight: 'r' }[e.key]; if (m) { e.preventDefault(); steer(m) } };
+  document.addEventListener('keydown', kd);
+  function build() {
+    el.innerHTML = chips([['Score 0', 'ss'], ['Best ' + gb('snake')]]) + `<canvas id="cv" class="arena" width="300" height="300" style="width:100%;max-width:300px;height:auto;display:block;margin:0 auto 12px;touch-action:none"></canvas><div class="dpad"><button data-d="u" aria-label="Up">Γåæ</button><div><button data-d="l" aria-label="Left">ΓåÉ</button><button data-d="r" aria-label="Right">ΓåÆ</button></div><button data-d="d" aria-label="Down">Γåô</button></div>`;
+    const cv = el.querySelector('#cv'), x = cv.getContext && cv.getContext('2d');
+    el.querySelectorAll('[data-d]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => steer(b.dataset.d));
+    let tx = 0, ty = 0;
+    cv.addEventListener('touchstart', e => { tx = e.touches[0].clientX; ty = e.touches[0].clientY }, { passive: true });
+    cv.addEventListener('touchend', e => { const t = e.changedTouches[0], dx = t.clientX - tx, dy = t.clientY - ty; if (Math.max(Math.abs(dx), Math.abs(dy)) < 20) return; steer(Math.abs(dx) > Math.abs(dy) ? (dx > 0 ? 'r' : 'l') : (dy > 0 ? 'd' : 'u')) });
+    sn = [[7, 7], [6, 7], [5, 7]];
+    dir = [1, 0];
+    nd = dir;
+    score = 0;
+    place();
+    const paint = () => { if (!x) return; x.clearRect(0, 0, 300, 300); sn.forEach((s, i) => { x.fillStyle = i ? '#22c55e' : '#15803d'; x.fillRect(s[0] * Z + 1, s[1] * Z + 1, Z - 2, Z - 2) }); x.font = '17px serif'; x.fillText('≡ƒìÄ', food[0] * Z + 1, food[1] * Z + 16) };
+    const tick = () => { dir = nd; const h = [(sn[0][0] + dir[0] + N) % N, (sn[0][1] + dir[1] + N) % N]; if (sn.some(s => s[0] === h[0] && s[1] === h[1])) { stop(); sfx('wrong'); endScreen(el, 'snake', score, 'apples', build); return } sn.unshift(h); if (h[0] === food[0] && h[1] === food[1]) { score++; sfx('pop'); place(); el.querySelector('#ss').textContent = 'Score ' + score } else sn.pop(); paint() };
+    paint();
+    stop();
+    iv = setInterval(tick, 170);
+  }
+  build();
+  return () => { stop(); document.removeEventListener('keydown', kd) };
+}
+
+export function farm(el) {
+  let r, score, tm = [];
+  const EM = ['≡ƒÉä', '≡ƒÉû', '≡ƒÉæ', '≡ƒÉÉ', '≡ƒÉ┤', '≡ƒªå'];
+  function start() { r = 0; score = 0; next() }
+  function next() {
+    if (r >= 8) return endScreen(el, 'farm', score, 'correct', start);
+    const n = 1 + rand(9), e = EM[rand(6)], set = new Set([n]);
+    while (set.size < 4) set.add(1 + rand(10));
+    el.innerHTML = chips([['Round ' + (r + 1) + '/8'], ['Score ' + score]]) + `<p class="gmsg">How many ${e}?</p><div class="arena" style="height:auto;min-height:160px;display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;justify-content:center;align-items:center;padding:14px;font-size:38px">${Array.from({length:n}, () => `<span style="display:inline-block;transform:rotate(${rand(30)-15}deg)">${e}</span>`).join('')}</div><div class="opts2" style="margin-top:12px">${shuffle([...set]).map(v => `<button class="mb2" data-v="${v}">${v}</button>`).join('')}</div>`;
+    const bs = [...el.querySelectorAll('.mb2')];
+    bs.forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { bs.forEach(x => { x.disabled = true; if (+x.dataset.v === n) x.style.background = 'var(--okbg)' }); if (+b.dataset.v === n) { score++; sfx('correct') } else { b.style.background = 'var(--badbg)'; sfx('wrong') } tm.push(setTimeout(() => { r++; next() }, 900)) })
+  }
+  start();
+  return () => tm.forEach(clearTimeout);
+}
+
+export function colormatch(el) {
+  const C = [['RED', '#ef4444'], ['BLUE', '#3b82f6'], ['GREEN', '#22c55e'], ['YELLOW', '#facc15'], ['PURPLE', '#a855f7'], ['ORANGE', '#f97316']];
+  let score, t, iv, run;
+  const stop = () => { run = false; clearInterval(iv) };
+  function start() {
+    score = 0; t = 30; run = true;
+    el.innerHTML = chips([['ΓÅ▒ 30s', 'ct'], ['Score 0', 'cs']]) + `<p class="gmsg">Tap the COLOR of the word, not what it says</p><div id="cw" style="font-size:54px;font-weight:900;margin:12px 0 18px"></div><div class="opts2" id="co"></div>`;
+    const cw = el.querySelector('#cw'), co = el.querySelector('#co');
+    const ui = () => { el.querySelector('#ct').textContent = 'ΓÅ▒ ' + t + 's'; el.querySelector('#cs').textContent = 'Score ' + score };
+    function next() {
+      const wi = rand(6);
+      let ci;
+      do { ci = rand(6) } while (ci === wi);
+      cw.textContent = C[wi][0];
+      cw.style.color = C[ci][1];
+      const ids = shuffle([ci, ...shuffle([0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5].filter(k => k !== ci)).slice(0, 3)]);
+      co.innerHTML = ids.map(k => `<button class="mb2" data-k="${k}" aria-label="${C[k][0]}" style="height:64px;background:${C[k][1]}"></button>`).join('');
+      co.querySelectorAll('.mb2').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { if (!run) return; if (+b.dataset.k === ci) { score++; sfx('pop') } else sfx('wrong'); ui(); next() })
+    }
+    next();
+    ui();
+    iv = setInterval(() => { t--; ui(); if (t <= 0) { stop(); endScreen(el, 'colormatch', score, 'correct', start) } }, 1000);
+  }
+  start();
+  return stop;
+}

+/**
+ * Bootstrap Service
+ * Application initialization and state setup
+ */
+
+import { load, save } from './storage.js'
+
+/**
+ * Default application state
+ */
+const DEFAULT_STATE = {
+  name: 'Explorer',
+  theme: 'clay',
+  sound: true,
+  vol: 0.7,
+  gb: {}, // game best scores
+  done: {}, // lessons done
+  stars: 0, // total achievements
+  best: {}, // quiz best scores per subject
+  day: { d: '', n: 0 } // today's lesson count
+}
+
+/**
+ * Initialize application state
+ * @returns {Object} Initialized state
+ */
+export function initState() {
+  const state = load(DEFAULT_STATE)
+
+  // Validate and correct invalid state values
+  if (typeof state.vol !== 'number' || state.vol < 0 || state.vol > 1) {
+    state.vol = 0.7
+  }
+  if (typeof state.sound !== 'boolean') {
+    state.sound = true
+  }
+  if (typeof state.stars !== 'number') {
+    state.stars = 0
+  }
+  if (!state.gb) state.gb = {}
+  if (!state.done) state.done = {}
+  if (!state.best) state.best = {}
+  if (!state.day) state.day = { d: '', n: 0 }
+
+  // Persist validated state
+  save(state)
+
+  return state
+}
+
+/**
+ * Update application theme
+ * @param {Object} state - Current state
+ * @param {string} themeName - Theme name to apply
+ */
+export function setTheme(state, themeName) {
+  state.theme = themeName
+  document.documentElement.dataset.theme = themeName
+  save(state)
+}
+
+/**
+ * Toggle sound on/off
+ * @param {Object} state - Current state
+ * @returns {boolean} New sound state
+ */
+export function toggleSound(state) {
+  state.sound = !state.sound
+  save(state)
+  return state.sound
+}
+
+/**
+ * Set volume level
+ * @param {Object} state - Current state
+ * @param {number} volume - Volume 0-1
+ */
+export function setVolume(state, volume) {
+  state.vol = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, volume))
+  save(state)
+}
+
+/**
+ * Update player name
+ * @param {Object} state - Current state
+ * @param {string} name - New player name
+ */
+export function setPlayerName(state, name) {
+  state.name = String(name).slice(0, 14)
+  save(state)
+}
+
+/**
+ * Reset all progress (keeping theme/name/settings)
+ * @param {Object} state - Current state
+ */
+export function resetProgress(state) {
+  state.gb = {}
+  state.done = {}
+  state.stars = 0
+  state.best = {}
+  state.day = { d: '', n: 0 }
+  save(state)
+}
+
+/**
+ * Get default state (for reference)
+ * @returns {Object} Default state template
+ */
+export function getDefaultState() {
+  return { ...DEFAULT_STATE }
+}

+/**
+ * Router Service
+ * Hash-based routing for single-page app navigation
+ */
+
+let currentRoute = '#/home'
+let routeHandlers = {}
+
+/**
+ * Register a route handler
+ * @param {string} route - Route pattern (e.g., '/home', '/game/:id')
+ * @param {Function} handler - Callback function for this route
+ */
+export function registerRoute(route, handler) {
+  routeHandlers[route] = handler
+}
+
+/**
+ * Register multiple routes at once
+ * @param {Object} routes - Map of route -> handler
+ */
+export function registerRoutes(routes) {
+  Object.assign(routeHandlers, routes)
+}
+
+/**
+ * Get current route hash
+ * @returns {string} Current hash path (e.g., '/home')
+ */
+export function getCurrentRoute() {
+  return currentRoute
+}
+
+/**
+ * Navigate to a route
+ * @param {string} path - Path to navigate to
+ */
+export function navigate(path) {
+  location.hash = path
+}
+
+/**
+ * Parse current hash and return route + params
+ * @returns {Object} { route, params }
+ */
+export function parseRoute() {
+  const hash = location.hash.slice(1) || '/home'
+  const [route, ...parts] = hash.split('/')
+  return {
+    path: hash,
+    route: `/${route}`,
+    params: parts
+  }
+}
+
+/**
+ * Initialize router - call on app startup
+ * @param {Function} onRouteChange - Callback when route changes
+ */
+export function initRouter(onRouteChange) {
+  const handleHashChange = () => {
+    currentRoute = location.hash.slice(1) || '/home'
+    onRouteChange(parseRoute())
+  }
+
+  window.addEventListener('hashchange', handleHashChange)
+  // Initial route
+  handleHashChange()
+}
+
+/**
+ * Get all registered routes
+ * @returns {Object} Map of routes
+ */
+export function getRoutes() {
+  return { ...routeHandlers }
+}
diff --git a/src/services/storage.js b/src/services/storage.js

+/**
+ * Storage Service
+ * Handles localStorage persistence of game state
+ */
+
+/**
+ * Save application state to localStorage
+ * @param {Object} state - The state object to persist
+ */
+export function save(state) {
+  try {
+    localStorage.setItem('claylearn', JSON.stringify(state))
+  } catch (e) {
+    // Silent fail; app continues with in-memory state
+  }
+}
+
+/**
+ * Load application state from localStorage
+ * @param {Object} defaultState - Default state if nothing saved
+ * @returns {Object} Merged state from storage + defaults
+ */
+export function load(defaultState) {
+  try {
+    const v = localStorage.getItem('claylearn')
+    if (v) {
+      return { ...defaultState, ...JSON.parse(v) }
+    }
+  } catch (e) {
+    // Silent fail; use default state
+  }
+  return defaultState
+}

+];
+
+export function getSubject(id) {
+  return SUBJECTS.find(s => s.id === id);
+}
+
+export function getSubjects() {
+  return SUBJECTS;
+}
+
+export default { SUBJECTS, getSubject, getSubjects };

+      fonth: "'Share Tech Mono',monospace",
+      tsh: '0 0 8px rgba(51,255,102,.6)'
+    }
+  },
+  cartoon: {
+    id: 'cartoon',
+    name: 'Cartoon',
+    vars: {
+      bg: "radial-gradient(rgba(0,0,0,.13) 2px,transparent 2.5px) 0 0/18px 18px,#7ad7ff",
+      bgc: '#7ad7ff',
+      card: '#fff',
+      ink: '#1b1b1b',
+      mute: '#4a4a4a',
+      accent: '#ff4757',
+      accentg: '#ff4757',
+      onacc: '#fff',
+      greeng: '#2ecc71',
+      green: '#2ecc71',
+      r: '22px',
+      rs: '16px',
+      bd: '4px solid #1b1b1b',
+      bdt: '3px solid #1b1b1b',
+      well: '#fff',
+      barfill: '#ffe14d',
+      sh: '5px 5px 0 #1b1b1b',
+      shs: '3px 3px 0 #1b1b1b',
+      sin: 'none',
+      sht: '4px 4px 0 #1b1b1b',
+      shbtn: '4px 4px 0 #1b1b1b',
+      font: "'Comic Neue','Nunito',cursive",
+      fonth: "'Bangers','Comic Neue',cursive"
+    }
+  }
+};
+
+/**
+ * Get theme by ID
+ */
+export function getTheme(id) {
+  return THEMES[id] || THEMES.clay;
+}
+
+/**
+ * Apply theme to document
+ */
+export function applyTheme(id) {
+  const theme = getTheme(id);
+  if (!theme || !theme.vars) return false;
+  
+  const root = document.documentElement;
+  Object.entries(theme.vars).forEach(([key, value]) => {
+    root.style.setProperty(`--${key}`, value);
+  });
+  root.dataset.theme = id;
+  return true;
+}
+
+export default { THEMES, getTheme, applyTheme };

+/**
+ * DOM Utilities
+ * Helper functions for DOM manipulation and rendering
+ */
+
+/**
+ * Query selector shorthand
+ * @param {string} selector - CSS selector
+ * @returns {Element|null} Selected element or null
+ */
+export function $(selector) {
+  return document.querySelector(selector)
+}
+
+/**
+ * Query all matching elements
+ * @param {string} selector - CSS selector
+ * @returns {NodeList} All matching elements
+ */
+export function $$(selector) {
+  return document.querySelectorAll(selector)
+}
+
+/**
+ * Set HTML content of element (must sanitize input!)
+ * @param {Element|string} el - Element or selector
+ * @param {string} html - HTML content (should be pre-sanitized)
+ */
+export function setHTML(el, html) {
+  const elem = typeof el === 'string' ? $(el) : el
+  if (elem) elem.innerHTML = html
+}
+
+/**
+ * Set text content of element
+ * @param {Element|string} el - Element or selector
+ * @param {string} text - Text content
+ */
+export function setText(el, text) {
+  const elem = typeof el === 'string' ? $(el) : el
+  if (elem) elem.textContent = String(text)
+}
+
+/**
+ * Add class to element
+ * @param {Element|string} el - Element or selector
+ * @param {string} cls - Class name
+ */
+export function addClass(el, cls) {
+  const elem = typeof el === 'string' ? $(el) : el
+  if (elem) elem.classList.add(cls)
+}
+
+/**
+ * Remove class from element
+ * @param {Element|string} el - Element or selector
+ * @param {string} cls - Class name
+ */
+export function removeClass(el, cls) {
+  const elem = typeof el === 'string' ? $(el) : el
+  if (elem) elem.classList.remove(cls)
+}
+
+/**
+ * Toggle class on element
+ * @param {Element|string} el - Element or selector
+ * @param {string} cls - Class name
+ * @returns {boolean} Whether class is now present
+ */
+export function toggleClass(el, cls) {
+  const elem = typeof el === 'string' ? $(el) : el
+  if (elem) return elem.classList.toggle(cls)
+  return false
+}
+
+/**
+ * Render page content to view
+ * @param {string} html - HTML to render
+ */
+export function render(html) {
+  setHTML('#view', html)
+}
+
+/**
+ * Show toast notification
+ * @param {string} message - Message to display
+ * @param {number} duration - Duration in ms (default 1800)
+ */
+export function showToast(message, duration = 1800) {
+  const toast = $('#toast')
+  if (!toast) return
+
+  toast.textContent = message
+  addClass(toast, 'show')
+
+  // Clear any existing timeout
+  if (showToast.timeout) clearTimeout(showToast.timeout)
+
+  showToast.timeout = setTimeout(() => {
+    removeClass(toast, 'show')
+  }, duration)
+}
+
+/**
+ * Update navigation active state
+ * @param {string} activeId - ID of active nav button (e.g., 'nav-home')
+ */
+export function setActiveNav(activeId) {
+  $$('#nav button').forEach(btn => {
+    removeClass(btn, 'on')
+  })
+  if (activeId) {
+    const btn = $(`#${activeId}`)
+    if (btn) addClass(btn, 'on')
+  }
+}
+
+/**
+ * Handle element focus
+ * @param {Element|string} el - Element or selector
+ */
+export function focus(el) {
+  const elem = typeof el === 'string' ? $(el) : el
+  if (elem) elem.focus()
+}
+
+/**
+ * Get input value
+ * @param {Element|string} el - Input element or selector
+ * @returns {string} Input value
+ */
+export function getValue(el) {
+  const elem = typeof el === 'string' ? $(el) : el
+  return elem ? elem.value : ''
+}
+
+/**
+ * Set input value
+ * @param {Element|string} el - Input element or selector
+ * @param {string} value - Value to set
+ */
+export function setValue(el, value) {
+  const elem = typeof el === 'string' ? $(el) : el
+  if (elem) elem.value = String(value)
+}

+

+export const UTILS = {
+  $: (sel) => document.querySelector(sel),
+  $$: (sel) => document.querySelectorAll(sel),
+  esc: (v) => String(v).replace(/[&<>"]/g, c => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c])),
+  sanitizeUrl: (u) => {
+    const l = String(u).toLowerCase().trim()
+    if (l.startsWith('javascript:') || l.startsWith('data:')) return ''
+    if (l.startsWith('http://') || l.startsWith('https://') || l.startsWith('/')) return u
+    if (l.startsWith('#')) return u
+    return ''
+  }
+}
+
+export default { UTILS }
diff --git a/src/utils/sanitize.js b/src/utils/sanitize.js

index 0000000..7b1a433
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/utils/sanitize.js
@@ -0,0 +1,71 @@
+/**
+ * Sanitization Utilities
+ * XSS prevention for user input
+ */
+
+/**
+ * Escape HTML special characters to prevent XSS
+ * @param {*} input - Value to escape (will be converted to string)
+ * @returns {string} Escaped string safe for HTML
+ */
+export function esc(input) {
+  return String(input).replace(/[&<>"]/g, c => {
+    const map = {
+      '&': '&amp;',
+      '<': '&lt;',
+      '>': '&gt;',
+      '"': '&quot;'
+    }
+    return map[c]
+  })
+}
+
+/**
+ * Check if string looks like a safe HTML class name
+ * @param {string} className - Class name to validate
+ * @returns {boolean} Whether it's a safe class name
+ */
+export function isValidClassName(className) {
+  // Only allow alphanumeric, hyphens, underscores
+  return /^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+$/.test(className)
+}
+
+/**
+ * Sanitize class name by filtering
+ * @param {string} className - Class name to sanitize
+ * @returns {string} Sanitized class name or empty string
+ */
+export function sanitizeClassName(className) {
+  return isValidClassName(className) ? className : ''
+}
+
+/**
+ * Sanitize attribute value (common for data attributes)
+ * @param {string} value - Value to sanitize
+ * @returns {string} Sanitized value
+ */
+export function sanitizeAttr(value) {
+  return String(value)
+    .replace(/["'`]/g, '') // Remove quotes
+    .replace(/[<>]/g, '') // Remove angle brackets
+    .slice(0, 255) // Limit length
+}
+
+/**
+ * Sanitize URL to prevent javascript: and data: URIs
+ * @param {string} url - URL to sanitize
+ * @returns {string} Safe URL or empty string
+ */
+export function sanitizeUrl(url) {
+  const lower = String(url).toLowerCase().trim()
+  if (lower.startsWith('javascript:') || lower.startsWith('data:')) {
+    return ''
+  }
+  if (lower.startsWith('http://') || lower.startsWith('https://') || lower.startsWith('/')) {
+    return url
+  }
+  if (lower.startsWith('#')) {
+    return url
+  }
+  return ''
+}
diff --git a/src/utils/shuffle.js b/src/utils/shuffle.js

@@ -0,0 +1,53 @@
+/**
+ * Shuffle Utility
+ * Fisher-Yates shuffle algorithm for randomizing arrays
+ */
+
+/**
+ * Shuffle an array randomly (non-mutating)
+ * @param {Array} array - Array to shuffle
+ * @returns {Array} New shuffled array
+ */
+export function shuffle(array) {
+  return array
+    .map(value => [Math.random(), value])
+    .sort((a, b) => a[0] - b[0])
+    .map(x => x[1])
+}
+
+/**
+ * Shuffle an array in place (mutating)
+ * @param {Array} array - Array to shuffle
+ * @returns {Array} The same array, shuffled
+ */
+export function shuffleInPlace(array) {
+  for (let i = array.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
+    const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
+    [array[i], array[j]] = [array[j], array[i]]
+  }
+  return array
+}
+
+/**
+ * Get random element from array
+ * @param {Array} array - Array to pick from
+ * @returns {*} Random element
+ */
+export function randomElement(array) {
+  if (array.length === 0) return undefined
+  return array[Math.floor(Math.random() * array.length)]
+}
+
+/**
+ * Get N random unique elements from array
+ * @param {Array} array - Array to pick from
+ * @param {number} n - Number of elements to pick
+ * @returns {Array} Array of N random unique elements
+ */
+export function randomUnique(array, n) {
+  if (n > array.length) {
+    throw new Error('Cannot pick more elements than array length')
+  }
+  const shuffled = shuffle(array)
+  return shuffled.slice(0, n)
+}

+(function polyfill() {
+  const relList = document.createElement("link").relList;
+  if (relList && relList.supports && relList.supports("modulepreload")) {
+    return;
+  }
+  for (const link of document.querySelectorAll('link[rel="modulepreload"]')) {
+    processPreload(link);
+  }
+  new MutationObserver((mutations) => {
+    for (const mutation of mutations) {
+      if (mutation.type !== "childList") {
+        continue;
+      }
+      for (const node of mutation.addedNodes) {
+        if (node.tagName === "LINK" && node.rel === "modulepreload")
+          processPreload(node);
+      }
+    }
+  }).observe(document, { childList: true, subtree: true });
+  function getFetchOpts(link) {
+    const fetchOpts = {};
+    if (link.integrity) fetchOpts.integrity = link.integrity;
+    if (link.referrerPolicy) fetchOpts.referrerPolicy = link.referrerPolicy;
+    if (link.crossOrigin === "use-credentials")
+      fetchOpts.credentials = "include";
+    else if (link.crossOrigin === "anonymous") fetchOpts.credentials = "omit";
+    else fetchOpts.credentials = "same-origin";
+    return fetchOpts;
+  }
+  function processPreload(link) {
+    if (link.ep)
+      return;
+    link.ep = true;
+    const fetchOpts = getFetchOpts(link);
+    fetch(link.href, fetchOpts);
+  }
+})();
+function save(state) {
+  try {
+    localStorage.setItem("claylearn", JSON.stringify(state));
+  } catch (e) {
+  }
+}
+function load(defaultState) {
+  try {
+    const v = localStorage.getItem("claylearn");
+    if (v) {
+      return { ...defaultState, ...JSON.parse(v) };
+    }
+  } catch (e) {
+  }
+  return defaultState;
+}
+const DEFAULT_STATE = {
+  name: "Explorer",
+  theme: "clay",
+  sound: true,
+  vol: 0.7,
+  gb: {},
+  // game best scores
+  done: {},
+  // lessons done
+  stars: 0,
+  // total achievements
+  best: {},
+  // quiz best scores per subject
+  day: { d: "", n: 0 }
+  // today's lesson count
+};
+function initState() {
+  const state = load(DEFAULT_STATE);
+  if (typeof state.vol !== "number" || state.vol < 0 || state.vol > 1) {
+    state.vol = 0.7;
+  }
+  if (typeof state.sound !== "boolean") {
+    state.sound = true;
+  }
+  if (typeof state.stars !== "number") {
+    state.stars = 0;
+  }
+  if (!state.gb) state.gb = {};
+  if (!state.done) state.done = {};
+  if (!state.best) state.best = {};
+  if (!state.day) state.day = { d: "", n: 0 };
+  save(state);
+  return state;
+}
+function navigate(path) {
+  location.hash = path;
+}
+function parseRoute() {
+  const hash = location.hash.slice(1) || "/home";
+  const [route, ...parts] = hash.split("/");
+  return {
+    path: hash,
+    route: `/${route}`,
+    params: parts
+  };
+}
+function initRouter(onRouteChange2) {
+  const handleHashChange = () => {
+    location.hash.slice(1) || "/home";
+    onRouteChange2(parseRoute());
+  };
+  window.addEventListener("hashchange", handleHashChange);
+  handleHashChange();
+}
+function go(path) {
+  navigate(path);
+}
+function $(selector) {
+  return document.querySelector(selector);
+}
+function addClass(el, cls) {
+  const elem = typeof el === "string" ? $(el) : el;
+  if (elem) elem.classList.add(cls);
+}
+function removeClass(el, cls) {
+  const elem = typeof el === "string" ? $(el) : el;
+  if (elem) elem.classList.remove(cls);
+}
+function showToast(message, duration = 1800) {
+  const toast = $("#toast");
+  if (!toast) return;
+  toast.textContent = message;
+  addClass(toast, "show");
+  if (showToast.timeout) clearTimeout(showToast.timeout);
+  showToast.timeout = setTimeout(() => {
+    removeClass(toast, "show");
+  }, duration);
+}
+function esc(input) {
+  return String(input).replace(/[&<>"]/g, (c) => {
+    const map = {
+      "&": "&amp;",
+      "<": "&lt;",
+      ">": "&gt;",
+      '"': "&quot;"
+    };
+    return map[c];
+  });
+}
+let S = initState();
+document.documentElement.dataset.theme = S.theme || "clay";
+console.log("Γ£ô Style Lab Arcade - Modularized Build");
+console.log("Γ£ô Services: Storage, Router, Navigator, Bootstrap");
+console.log("Γ£ô State initialized:", { theme: S.theme, sound: S.sound, volume: S.vol });
+window.S = S;
+window.go = go;
+window.save = () => save(S);
+window.showToast = showToast;
+window.esc = esc;
+function onRouteChange(route) {
+  console.log("Route:", route.path);
+}
+initRouter(onRouteChange);

+    if (link.integrity) fetchOpts.integrity = link.integrity;
+    if (link.referrerPolicy) fetchOpts.referrerPolicy = link.referrerPolicy;
+    if (link.crossOrigin === "use-credentials")
+      fetchOpts.credentials = "include";
+    else if (link.crossOrigin === "anonymous") fetchOpts.credentials = "omit";
+    else fetchOpts.credentials = "same-origin";
+    return fetchOpts;
+  }
+  function processPreload(link) {
+    if (link.ep)
+      return;
+    link.ep = true;
+    const fetchOpts = getFetchOpts(link);
+    fetch(link.href, fetchOpts);
+  }
+})();
+function save(state) {
+  try {
+    localStorage.setItem("claylearn", JSON.stringify(state));
+  } catch (e) {
+  }
+}
+function load(defaultState) {
+  try {
+    const v = localStorage.getItem("claylearn");
+    if (v) {
+      return { ...defaultState, ...JSON.parse(v) };
+    }
+  } catch (e) {
+  }
+  return defaultState;
+}
+const DEFAULT_STATE = {
+  name: "Explorer",
+  theme: "clay",
+  sound: true,
+  vol: 0.7,
+  gb: {},
+  // game best scores
+  done: {},
+  // lessons done
+  stars: 0,
+  // total achievements
+  best: {},
+  // quiz best scores per subject
+  day: { d: "", n: 0 }
+  // today's lesson count
+};
+function initState() {
+  const state = load(DEFAULT_STATE);
+  if (typeof state.vol !== "number" || state.vol < 0 || state.vol > 1) {
+    state.vol = 0.7;
+  }
+  if (typeof state.sound !== "boolean") {
+    state.sound = true;
+  }
+  if (typeof state.stars !== "number") {
+    state.stars = 0;
+  }
+  if (!state.gb) state.gb = {};
+  if (!state.done) state.done = {};
+  if (!state.best) state.best = {};
+  if (!state.day) state.day = { d: "", n: 0 };
+  save(state);
+  return state;

+function navigate(path) {
+  location.hash = path;

+function parseRoute() {
+  const hash = location.hash.slice(1) || "/home";
+  const [route, ...parts] = hash.split("/");
+  return {
+    path: hash,
+    route: `/${route}`,
+    params: parts
+  };
+}
+function initRouter(onRouteChange2) {
+  const handleHashChange = () => {
+    location.hash.slice(1) || "/home";
+    onRouteChange2(parseRoute());
+  };
+  window.addEventListener("hashchange", handleHashChange);
+  handleHashChange();

+function $(selector) {
+  return document.querySelector(selector);

+function addClass(el, cls) {
+  const elem = typeof el === "string" ? $(el) : el;
+  if (elem) elem.classList.add(cls);

+function removeClass(el, cls) {
+  const elem = typeof el === "string" ? $(el) : el;
+  if (elem) elem.classList.remove(cls);

+function showToast(message, duration = 1800) {
+  const toast = $("#toast");
+  if (!toast) return;
+  toast.textContent = message;
+  addClass(toast, "show");
+  if (showToast.timeout) clearTimeout(showToast.timeout);
+  showToast.timeout = setTimeout(() => {
+    removeClass(toast, "show");
+  }, duration);
+}
+function esc(input) {
+  return String(input).replace(/[&<>"]/g, (c) => {
+    const map = {
+      "&": "&amp;",
+      "<": "&lt;",
+      ">": "&gt;",
+      '"': "&quot;"
+    };
+    return map[c];
+  });
+}
+let ctx = null;
+let master = null;
+let fxBus = null;
+function getContext(volume = 0.7) {
+  if (!ctx) {
+    const AudioContextClass = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
+    if (!AudioContextClass) {
+      return null;
+    }
+    try {
+      ctx = new AudioContextClass();
+    } catch (e) {
+      return null;
+    }
+    master = ctx.createGain();
+    master.connect(ctx.destination);
+    const delay = ctx.createDelay(1);
+    delay.delayTime.value = 0.17;
+    const feedback = ctx.createGain();
+    feedback.gain.value = 0.38;
+    delay.connect(feedback);
+    feedback.connect(delay);
+    const wet = ctx.createGain();
+    wet.gain.value = 0.5;
+    delay.connect(wet);
+    wet.connect(master);
+    fxBus = delay;
+  }
+  if (ctx.state === "suspended") {
+    ctx.resume();
+  }
+  if (master) {
+    master.gain.value = Math.pow(volume, 1.5) * 0.9;
+  }
+  return ctx;
+}
+function getMaster() {
+  return master;
+}
+function getEffectBus() {
+  return fxBus;
+}
+function isAudioSupported() {
+  return !!(window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext);
+}
+function playTone(ctx2, preset, frequency, time, duration, volume, targetFreq) {
+  if (!ctx2 || !getMaster()) return;
+  const osc = ctx2.createOscillator();
+  const gain = ctx2.createGain();
+  const nowTime = ctx2.currentTime + time;
+  osc.type = preset.w || "sine";
+  osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(frequency, nowTime);
+  if (targetFreq) {
+    osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(30, targetFreq), nowTime + duration);
+  }
+  const volume_scaled = Math.max(2e-4, volume * (preset.v || 1));
+  gain.gain.setValueAtTime(1e-4, nowTime);
+  gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(volume_scaled, nowTime + 8e-3);
+  gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(1e-4, nowTime + duration);
+  osc.connect(gain);
+  gain.connect(getMaster());
+  if (preset.fx) {
+    gain.connect(getEffectBus());
+  }
+  osc.start(nowTime);
+  osc.stop(nowTime + duration + 0.05);
+}
+function playNoise(ctx2, time, duration, volume, highpass) {
+  if (!ctx2 || !getMaster()) return;
+  const bufferLen = Math.max(1, Math.floor(ctx2.sampleRate * duration));
+  const buffer = ctx2.createBuffer(1, bufferLen, ctx2.sampleRate);
+  const data = buffer.getChannelData(0);
+  for (let i = 0; i < bufferLen; i++) {
+    data[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * (1 - i / bufferLen);
+  }
+  const source = ctx2.createBufferSource();
+  source.buffer = buffer;
+  const filter = ctx2.createBiquadFilter();
+  filter.type = "highpass";
+  filter.frequency.value = highpass || 1e3;
+  const gain = ctx2.createGain();
+  gain.gain.value = volume;
+  source.connect(filter);
+  filter.connect(gain);
+  gain.connect(getMaster());
+  const nowTime = ctx2.currentTime + time;
+  source.start(nowTime);
+}
+function playTick(preset) {
+  const ctx2 = getContext();
+  if (!ctx2) return;
+  playNoise(ctx2, 0, 0.02, 0.45, 5e3);
+  playTone(ctx2, preset, 1800, 0, 0.03, 0.1, null);
+}
+function playBend(preset, startFreq, endFreq, duration) {
+  const ctx2 = getContext();
+  if (!ctx2) return;
+  playTone(ctx2, preset, startFreq, 0, duration, 0.3, endFreq);
+}

+function getPreset(themeId) {
+  return SOUND_THEMES[themeId] || SOUND_THEMES.clay;
+}
+function sfx(eventName, arg, state) {
+  if (!state || !state.sound) {
+    return;
+  }
+  const ctx2 = getContext(state.vol || 0.7);
+  if (!ctx2) {
+    return;
+  }
+  const preset = getPreset(state.theme || "clay");
+  const p = preset.p || 1;
+  const d = preset.d || 1;
+  const playNote = (freq, startTime, duration, volume, targetFreq) => {
+    playTone(ctx2, preset, freq * p, startTime, duration * d, volume, targetFreq && targetFreq * p);
+  };
+  try {
+    switch (eventName) {
+      case "tap":
+        if (preset.k === "click") {
+          playNoise(ctx2, 0, 0.03, 0.5 * preset.v, 2500);
+        } else if (preset.k === "tick") {
+          playTick(preset);
+        } else if (preset.bend) {
+          playBend(preset, 330, 640, 0.12);
+        } else {
+          playNote(520, 0, 0.09, 0.3, 380);
+        }
+        break;
+      case "correct":
+        playNote(523.25, 0, 0.2, 0.3);
+        playNote(783.99, 0.1, 0.28, 0.3);
+        playNote(1975, 0.2, 0.12, 0.12);
+        break;
+      case "wrong":
+        playNote(190, 0, 0.28, 0.28, 120);
+        break;
+      case "lesson":
+        [523.25, 659.25, 783.99].forEach((f, i) => {
+          playNote(f, i * 0.09, 0.22, 0.3);
+        });
+        playNote(1318.5, 0.3, 0.2, 0.2);
+        break;
+      case "end":
+        if (arg === 2) {
+          [523.25, 659.25, 783.99, 1046.5].forEach((f, i) => {
+            playNote(f, i * 0.11, 0.25, 0.3);
+          })[523.25, 659.25, 783.99, 1046.5].forEach((f) => {
+            playNote(f, 0.55, 0.7, 0.16);
+          });
+        } else if (arg === 1) {
+          [523.25, 659.25, 783.99].forEach((f, i) => {
+            playNote(f, i * 0.11, 0.3, 0.3);
+          });
+        } else {
+          [392, 440, 523.25].forEach((f, i) => {
+            playNote(f, i * 0.13, 0.3, 0.25);
+          });
+        }
+        break;
+      case "note":
+        const notes = [261.63, 329.63, 392, 523.25];
+        const freq = notes[arg] || notes[0];
+        playNote(freq, 0, 0.35, 0.35);
+        break;
+      case "pop":
+        playNoise(ctx2, 0, 0.05, 0.4 * preset.v, 800);
+        playNote(900, 0, 0.08, 0.2, 300);
+        break;
+      case "theme":
+        playNote(220, 0, 0.3, 0.22, 1400);
+        playNoise(ctx2, 0, 0.25, 0.1 * preset.v, 1200);
+        break;
+      default:
+        break;
+    }
+  } catch (e) {
+  }
+}
+function canPlayAudio() {
+  return isAudioSupported();
+}
